### [1] [Direct real-space imaging of sign-reversal superconducting state by scanning Josephson tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38421)
*Tianheng Wei,Yanzhao Liu,Wei Ren,Zhen Liang,Ziqiang Wang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用扫描约瑟夫森隧道显微镜在单层Fe(Te,Se)薄膜中观测到半单胞周期的约瑟夫森隧道调制，结果支持符号反转的无节点d波配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于约瑟夫森隧道的相敏实验难以用于多带铁基超导体，因其配对对称性复杂；需要亚单胞尺度的超导相位信息来理解高温超导机制。

Method: 在SrTiO3(001)外延生长的单层高温超导Fe(Te,Se)薄膜上，使用s波超导针尖进行扫描约瑟夫森隧道显微镜（SJTM）测量，并结合单粒子隧道谱。

Result: 观察到半单胞周期的约瑟夫森隧道调制；在s波超导针尖下，沿连接次近邻Fe原子方向的约瑟夫森隧穿几乎消失；单粒子谱显示完整超导能隙。这些结果与符号反转的无节点d波配对对称性一致。

Conclusion: 研究提供了亚单胞尺度的超导相位信息，表明SJTM可用于原子分辨的约瑟夫森干涉实验，并为各类非常规超导体的配对对称性研究提供起点。

Abstract: Phase-sensitive experiments based on Josephson tunneling have been considered as a pivotal method to determine the pairing symmetry in high-temperature (high-Tc) cuprate superconductors. However, in iron-based superconductors, traditional phase-sensitive experiments face setbacks because of the complexity of pairing symmetry in multiband systems. Here, using scanning Josephson tunneling microscopy (SJTM), we report the half-unit-cell periodic Josephson tunneling modulations in monolayer high-Tc Fe(Te,Se) film epitaxially grown on SrTiO3(001). The Josephson tunneling almost vanishes along the lines connecting next-nearest-neighbor Fe atoms under an s-wave superconducting tip. Combined with single-particle tunneling spectra showing the full superconducting gap, our experimental results are consistent with the sign-reversal nodeless d-wave pairing symmetry. Our findings provide sub-unit-cell scale superconducting phase information for understanding the mechanism of high-Tc superconductivity. We anticipate our work as a starting point for atomically resolved Josephson interference experiments using SJTM in various unconventional superconductors.

</details>


### [2] [Layer-Parity Control of the Superconducting Diode Effect in Monolayer NbSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38449)
*Yusuf Wicaksono,Toshikaze Kariyado*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 单层NbSe2的超导二极管效应由磁近邻的层宇称与取向选择：面内交换产生 helical 通道，纯面外交换在 Q_eq=0 时产生 trigonal 通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管效应常与有限动量配对相联系，但模型表明整流更反映自由能对其极小值的不对称性，而非平衡配对动量本身；本文旨在厘清磁近邻层宇称/取向如何分别控制平衡配对动量与二极管效率。

Method: 采用基于Wannier、自洽有限 Q 的 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 方法，并使用费米面自适应求积使临界电流收敛，计算单层 NbSe2 在不同磁近邻层宇称与取向下的超导二极管响应。

Result: 面内交换时，平衡配对动量 Q_eq 与二极管效率均线性依赖层奇交换对比：两 Se 子层平行近邻为互易，反平行近邻使响应加倍。面外分量将两观测量分离：Q_eq 近乎不变，但通过破对将效率压低一个数量级以上，并在低温下反号。纯面外交换受 C3 对称性禁阻 helical 项，保持 Q_eq=0，却由层偶交换产生 trigonal 二极管效应；低温下当交换能高于 0.65 meV 时其效率大于面内情形。

Conclusion: 磁层宇称在 helical 与 trigonal 二极管通道之间做出选择；trigonal 通道是 NbSe2/CrBr3 中无场整流的内禀、对称性允许贡献，并预测双侧面外近邻在反平行时抵消该效应，而在相同每层交换下平行时在计算范围内增强约四倍。

Abstract: The superconducting diode effect is often associated with finite-momentum pairing, although model studies indicate that rectification reflects the asymmetry of the free energy about its minimum rather than the equilibrium pair momentum itself. Using a Wannier-based, self-consistent finite-$\mathbf Q$ Bogoliubov-de Gennes approach with a Fermi-surface-adaptive quadrature that converges the critical currents, we show that the layer parity and orientation of magnetic proximity select the diode response of monolayer NbSe$_2$. For in-plane exchange, the equilibrium pair momentum $\mathbf Q_{\rm eq}$ and the diode efficiency are both linear in the layer-odd exchange contrast: parallel proximity on the two Se sublayers is reciprocal, and antiparallel proximity doubles the response. An out-of-plane component separates the two observables. It leaves $\mathbf Q_{\rm eq}$ nearly unchanged but, through pair breaking, suppresses the efficiency by more than an order of magnitude and, at low temperature, reverses it. Purely out-of-plane exchange, for which $C_3$ symmetry forbids a helical term, keeps $\mathbf Q_{\rm eq}=0$ yet produces a trigonal diode effect, controlled by the layer-even exchange and larger than the in-plane one above $0.65$~meV at low temperature. Magnetic layer parity thus selects between a helical and a trigonal diode channel. The trigonal channel is an intrinsic, symmetry-allowed contribution to the field-free rectification reported in NbSe$_2$/CrBr$_3$ and predicts that two-sided out-of-plane proximity cancels it when antiparallel and, at the same exchange per layer, enhances it about fourfold when parallel, in the computed range.

</details>


### [3] [Chiral $p$-Wave Superconductivity Proximate to a Fractional Chern Insulator and a Topological Charge Density Wave in a Moir\'e Chern Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40469)
*Yunze Ouyang,Fengcheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在短程排斥的自旋极化扭转MoTe₂莫尔Chern带中，发现邻近ν=2/3分数量子Chern绝缘体与ν=3/4拓扑电荷密度波的超导态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究莫尔Chern带中超导与分数量子拓扑、电荷序之间的相互作用，并解释扭转MoTe₂实验中的超导现象。

Method: 对短程排斥下的自旋极化莫尔Chern带进行精确对角化，分析配对束缚、超流刚度、手性p波配对关联及绝热连接；并用弱排斥下的Kohn-Luttinger理论检验费米面不稳定性。

Result: 在0.7≲ν≲0.77发现与实验一致的配对束缚，超导态具有有限超流刚度和手性p波配对关联，并可绝热连接至吸引模型；弱排斥Kohn-Luttinger理论给出相同配对对称性。超导态的Bogoliubov-de Gennes Chern数为C_BdG=-1，与ν=1时正常态Chern数相反。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了超导、分数量子拓扑与电荷序在莫尔Chern带中的交织，并指出一种具有非平凡BdG拓扑的手性p波超导态。

Abstract: We study a spin-polarized moir\'e Chern band of twisted MoTe$_2$ with short-range repulsion, finding superconductivity proximate to a fractional Chern insulator at filling $\nu=2/3$ and a topological charge density wave at $\nu=3/4$. Exact diagonalization reveals pair binding over $0.7\lesssim\nu\lesssim0.77$, consistent with experiment, along with finite superfluid stiffness, chiral $p$-wave pairing correlations, and adiabatic connectivity to an attractive model. At weak repulsion, Kohn-Luttinger theory supports a Fermi-surface instability with the same pairing symmetry. The superconducting state has a Bogoliubov-de Gennes Chern number $C_{\mathrm{BdG}}=-1$, opposite to the normal-state Chern number at $\nu=1$. Our results reveal interplay among superconductivity, fractional topology, and charge order.

</details>


### [4] [A $B_{1g}$ phonon unlocks $d$-wave superconductivity in the repulsive Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37746)
*Wen O. Wang,Tianshu Li,Xiaoyue Ma,Rong Zhang,Lei Zhang,Shruti Agarwal,Benjamin Cohen-Stead,Emily Z. Zhang,Brian Moritz,Steven Johnston,Zhi-Xun Shen,Thomas P. Devereaux*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 有限温度量子蒙特卡洛表明，在掺杂t-t'排斥Hubbard模型中耦合B1g声子可产生真实的d波超导，且转变温度高于单独相互作用机制，并呈现掺杂超导穹顶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究声子与强关联电子排斥如何协同驱动d波超导，并寻找提高超导转变温度的微观机制。

Method: 采用数值精确的有限温度量子蒙特卡洛，在掺杂t-t'排斥Hubbard模型中引入与电子耦合的B1g声子，系统研究超导配对与转变温度随Hubbard排斥、声子频率和掺杂的变化。

Result: B1g声子与Hubbard排斥共同产生显著d波超导，Tc超过任一单独机制；更强Hubbard排斥可能进一步提升配对能标，且在费米子符号问题允许范围内持续增强；不同声子频率Ω的结果按延迟尺度2πT/Ω组织后呈现共同趋势；掺杂依赖上出现明确的超导穹顶。

Conclusion: B1g声子是Hubbard模型中稳健d波超导的合理微观要素，为通过电子-声子与强关联协同提升超导转变温度及设计更高Tc材料提供了物理动机明确的路径。

Abstract: Using numerically exact finite-temperature quantum Monte Carlo, we demonstrate that a $B_{1g}$ phonon coupled to the doped $t$-$t'$ repulsive Hubbard model produces genuine $d$-wave superconductivity at a substantial transition temperature, in excess of either interaction alone. The observed trends suggest that stronger Hubbard repulsion may cooperate with the phonon to further raise the pairing scale. The enhancement persists at stronger repulsion within the accessible window, limited by the fermion sign problem, and follows a common trend across phonon frequencies $\Omega$ when organized by a retardation scale $2\pi T/\Omega$. We also find a well-defined superconducting dome in doping, echoing a characteristic feature of correlated superconductors. Our results identify the $B_{1g}$ phonon as a plausible microscopic ingredient for robust $d$-wave superconductivity in the Hubbard model and suggest a concrete, physically motivated route toward materials with higher superconducting transition temperatures.

</details>


### [5] [Separating the Density Wave Phase from Zero-Resistance Superconductivity in La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38959)
*P. Puphal,A. Suthar,B. Wehinger,V. Sundaramurthy,T. Priessnitz,M. Knauft,P. Sosa-Lizama,Y. -M. Wu,M. Hepting,J. Nuss,M. Isobe,P. A. van Aken,Y. E. Suyolcu,B. Keimer,H. Takagi,P. Reiss*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在清洁的三层Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$单晶中，双轴压缩压力可在比此前报道低60%的压力下诱导零电阻态，并将密度波相与超导相完全分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐中压力诱导超导的关键因素难以识别，原因在于结构变化、氧含量与占位、电子有序相以及达到超导相所需高压之间存在复杂耦合，亟需解耦这些相互依赖关系。

Method: 研究一系列清洁的三层La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$单晶，并施加增强的双轴压缩压力，以考察其对超导态和密度波相的影响。

Result: 增强的双轴压缩压力使零电阻态在比先前报道低60%的压力下出现；同时将低压密度波相与超导相完全分离，从而有效排除密度波序参量的软量子临界涨落作为该体系配对胶的可能性。

Conclusion: 双轴面内压力是促进镍酸盐超导的有力手段，可统一不同Ruddlesden-Popper结构中超导所需的压力尺度，并建立与近期发现的薄膜样品之间的联系。

Abstract: Identifying the key ingredients for superconductivity under pressure in the Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates remains a formidable challenge, due to a complex interplay between structural variations, oxygen content and location, electronically ordered phases, and the need for high pressures to reach the superconducting phase. To disentangle these complex inter-dependencies, here we study a series of clean single crystals of trilayer La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$. We discover that enhanced biaxial, compressive pressures induce a zero-resistance state at a 60\% lower pressure compared to previous reports. At the same time, we completely separate the low-pressure density wave phase from superconductivity, effectively ruling out that soft, quantum critical fluctuations of the density wave order parameter act as the pairing glue in this system. Our results establish biaxial, in-plane pressures as a powerful tool for promoting superconductivity in nickelates, unifying the required pressure scales for superconductivity in different Ruddlesden-Popper structures, and providing a connection to the recently discovered thin film samples.

</details>


### [6] [Coexistence of superconductivity and magnetic order in BaFe$_2$S$_3$: evidence for orbital-selective delocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38966)
*Y. Oubaid,N. S. Dhami,P. Fertey,C. V. Colin,C. Ritter,A. Forget,D. Bounoua,J. -P. Rueff,M. Verseils,P. Foury-Leylekian,V. Bal\'edent*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 高压下准一维自旋梯状铁基化合物中，长程条纹反铁磁序在超导出现时仍保持，而局域Fe磁矩被抑制约80%，表明超导与长程磁序共存并由轨道选择机制驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超导如何从关联电子态中产生是凝聚态物理核心问题；该准一维自旋梯状铁基化合物缺乏多数铁基超导体的二维方格子结构，为研究磁性、电子关联与超导的耦合提供了独特平台。

Method: 结合高压X射线与中子衍射、X射线发射谱和电输运测量，追踪该化合物在进入超导区过程中的结构、磁性和电子演化。

Result: 观察到显著的压力诱导解耦：长程条纹型反铁磁序在压力下直至超导 onset 仍很稳健，而局域铁磁矩被抑制近80%；超导在长程磁序仍存在时出现，二者共存。

Conclusion: 该现象指向轨道选择机制：部分Fe 3d电子保持局域以维持磁性，另一些则离域形成 itinerant 与超导态；超导与长程磁序的共存源于多轨道电子自由度的选择性响应。

Abstract: Understanding how superconductivity emerges from correlated electronic states is a central challenge in condensed matter physics. The quasi-one-dimensional spin-ladder compound \bfs\ serves as a unique platform to study this interplay, as it lacks the two-dimensional square lattice characteristic of most iron-based superconductors. Here, we combine high-pressure x-ray and neutron diffraction, x-ray emission spectroscopy, and electrical transport to map its structural, magnetic, and electronic evolution into the superconducting regime. We \revised{observe} a striking pressure-induced decoupling: while the long-range stripe-type antiferromagnetic order remains remarkably robust under pressure up to the onset of superconductivity, the local iron magnetic moment is suppressed by nearly 80\%. This dichotomy \revised{points to an orbital-selective mechanism}, where specific Fe $3d$ electrons remain localized to sustain magnetism while others delocalize to form the itinerant and superconducting state. \revised{Superconductivity emerges while long-range magnetic order is still present, revealing that the two coexist in \bfs, which we attribute to} the selective response of multiorbital electronic degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [7] [Parity-Resolved Many-Body Kinetics of a Topological Qubit under Nonequilibrium Phonon Pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37793)
*Han Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 结合晶格Lutchyn–Oreg模型、微观电子–声子耦合与宇称分辨多体动力学，研究预设非平衡声子脉冲对双纳米线Majorana量子比特的激发、弛豫和逻辑相干性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电离辐射产生的声子暴是超导量子处理器的相关错误源；需量化Majorana量子比特中声子诱导激发、宇称翻转与退相干，指导器件设计。

Method: 对由两根2 μm近邻化纳米线构成的Majorana量子比特施加预设非平衡声子脉冲；结合晶格Lutchyn–Oreg模型、微观电子–声子耦合和宇称分辨多体动力学，追踪激发、弛豫和逻辑相干性，并与Kitaev链及高密度硬壁边界对照。

Result: 无准粒子逃逸时，复合约130 ns恢复初始Majorana占据；但激发与恢复中交换的声子可保留逻辑态信息，造成永久相干损失。镜像对称线中每根线的分数相干损失为两逻辑态平均激发概率的0.75–1.0倍，即使零Majorana劈裂；破缺镜像对称可在小劈裂下抑制退相位。因子化Boltzmann动力学忽略准粒子关联并高估残余宇称翻转。相同沉积能量密度下，不同声子谱给出不同激发概率与相干损失。与匹配能隙和Majorana衰减长度的Kitaev链比较：低电子密度抑制高能声子激发；高密度硬壁对照在1 meV中心脉冲下激发效率比纳米线高5×10^4倍。磁场与化学势调谐在相同体能隙下通过自旋和电子–空穴结构改变声子敏感性；当隧道耦合等于母超导能隙时，纳入杂化态半导体权重使近阈值激发率降低四倍。

Conclusion: 该框架将声子谱与泄漏、准粒子中毒和相干损失联系起来，为Majorana器件设计提供指导。

Abstract: Phonon bursts from ionizing radiation cause correlated errors in superconducting quantum processors. We study prescribed nonequilibrium phonon pulses in a Majorana qubit formed by two $2\,\mu\mathrm{m}$ proximitized nanowires. Combining the lattice Lutchyn--Oreg model, microscopic electron--phonon coupling, and parity-resolved many-body dynamics, we track excitation, relaxation, and logical coherence. Without quasiparticle escape, recombination restores the initial Majorana occupations in about 130 ns, while phonons exchanged during excitation and recovery can retain logical-state information, leaving permanent coherence loss. For mirror-symmetric wires, the fractional coherence loss per wire is 0.75--1.0 times the excitation probability averaged over the two logical states, even at zero Majorana splitting; breaking mirror symmetry can suppress dephasing at small splitting. Factorized Boltzmann kinetics neglects quasiparticle correlations and overestimates residual parity flips. At equal deposited energy density, phonon spectra yield different excitation probabilities and coherence losses. Comparisons with Kitaev chains matched to the wire's gap and Majorana decay length reveal that low electron density suppresses excitation by energetic phonons, whereas hard-wall boundaries in a high-density control increase excitation efficiency by a factor of $5\times10^4$ relative to the wire for pulses centered at 1 meV. Magnetic-field and chemical-potential tuning yield different phonon sensitivities at the same bulk gap through changes in spin and electron--hole structure. Including hybrid states' semiconductor weights reduces near-threshold excitation rates fourfold when the tunnel coupling equals the parent superconducting gap. Our framework connects phonon spectra to leakage, poisoning, and coherence loss to guide Majorana-device design.

</details>


### [8] [Influences of $N_2$ flow rate on the superconducting properties and dimensionality of epitaxial TiN thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38250)
*Shilong Chen,Yuchuan Liu,Yihui Sun,Xiaoni Wang,Wei Peng,Yu Wu,Gang Mu,Zhirong Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究系统研究了DC反应磁控溅射制备的35 nm外延TiN/MgO薄膜中N2流量和背景真空对表面形貌、超导转变温度、RRR、正常态电阻率、上临界场及超导维度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TiN薄膜因低损耗、稳定化学热性质和良好导电性而在超导量子计算中受关注，需要厘清生长参数与超导性能及超导维度之间的关系。

Method: 采用DC反应磁控溅射在MgO衬底上外延生长35 nm TiN薄膜，表征晶体结构、厚度和表面形貌；系统改变N2流量，测量Tc、RRR、ρn、上临界场及超导维度，并考察背景真空的影响。

Result: 薄膜表面平整，RMS粗糙度仅零点几纳米；N2流量为1.5 sccm时性能最优：Tc最高为4.93 K、RRR最大、ρn最低、Tc附近面外上临界场斜率最低；背景真空变差会降低Tc；上临界场呈显著各向异性，面内上临界场数据表明样品质量优化会使超导行为偏离二维，说明超导维度还受薄膜质量影响而不仅由厚度决定。

Conclusion: 研究为理解TiN薄膜生长参数与超导性能关系提供参考，并给出超越薄膜厚度的超导维度影响因素新视角。

Abstract: TiN thin films have received great attention in the field of superconducting quantum computing due to their low loss characteristics derived from stable chemical and thermal properties, as well as excellent electrical conductivity. In this study, TiN thin films with a thickness of 35 nm were epitaxially grown on MgO substrates using the DC reactive magnetron sputtering technique. The crystal structure, film thickness, and surface morphology of the thin films were characterized. The films exhibit a very flat surface with a root mean square roughness of only a few tenths of a nanometer. The influences of nitrogen ($N_2$) flow rate on the surface morphology, superconducting transition temperature ($T_c$), residual resistivity ratio (RRR), normal-state resistivity ($\rho_n$), upper critical field, and superconducting dimensionality were investigated systematically. The experimental results show that the TiN film deposited with a $N_2$ flow rate of 1.5 sccm exhibits optimal performance, including the highest $T_c$ (4.93 K), the largest RRR, the lowest $\rho_n$, and the lowest slope of the out-of-film upper critical field near Tc. Abstract: We study a spinless $\pi$-flux lattice superconductor whose normal-state momentum-space fundamental domain is a Klein bottle. Within this model, a glide lift and a hidden antiunitary symmetry independently force the Bogoliubov--de Gennes (BdG) Chern number to vanish. A diagonal pairing breaks the antiunitary obstruction exactly when its real part is nonzero; this is necessary, not sufficient, for a chiral phase. We derive explicit Pfaffians determining Chern parity and identify three analytic families of gap closings in a real-pairing section. For a chiral state on a closed real-space Klein bottle, the prescribed pairing and gluing produce a $\Delta C=2$ chirality wall. A translation-invariant unfolding resolves two co-propagating Majorana branches. Their finite-size levels depend on circumference and holonomy; the near-zero pair is lifted at first order by allowed local seam perturbations. A cut-cylinder control has the same low-energy channel count, so gluing reconnects existing gapless content. We also distinguish literal gluing from deck projection: particle--hole symmetry exchanges the deck eigenblocks, precluding their interpretation as independent BdG quotient Hamiltonians. The contribution is a model-specific account of pairing compatibility and closed-surface seam spectra. The energy cost is evaluated for a fixed pairing background; neither a protected zero mode nor a self-consistent wall solution is claimed.

Abstract: Graphene's sp^{2}-bonded carbon lattice makes it exceptionally resistant to covalent functionalization, with existing approaches relying on chemically specific and often harsh methods, fragmenting our understanding of its reactivity. Analysis of the oxidation potentials across substituents reveals trends governed by classical descriptors. Species forming localised {\sigma} bonds display increasing oxidation potentials with the meta-Hammett constant ({\sigma}_m), whereas substituents interacting via delocalised {\pi} orbitals follow an inverse dependence on the modified Swain-Lupton resonance parameter (R). These relationships indicate that graphene's oxidation potential increases with the electron-withdrawing strength of the substituent. Our results provide new insights into the electrochemical activity of inert materials under strong electric fields.

Abstract: Accurately predicting the transport properties of liquid electrolytes is critical for next-generation battery design and requires force fields that are both quantum-mechanically accurate and fast enough for long-timescale simulation. TL;DR: 该文指出逐元素拟合 Kohn-Sham 哈密顿矩阵不能保证对角化后的本征值和本征向量准确，并提出以累积局域态密度（cumulative LDOS）作为完整物理监督信号，在 QH9 上微调哈密顿网络（FermiScope），在不增加逐元素误差的情况下降低 HOMO-LUMO gap 误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kohn-Sham DFT 的 SCF 迭代对大体系和高通量筛选昂贵；机器学习的等变哈密顿模型虽可一次前向预测哈密顿矩阵并对角化得到轨道能、gap、轨道和电子密度，但训练目标多为逐元素匹配参考矩阵，而小的逐元素误差并不保证谱量准确。现有的波函数对齐损失和电子密度匹配损失只覆盖部分误差：前者不显式对角化预测，后者对轨道能整体平移不敏感，因此需要更完整的训练信号。

Method: 作者在参考哈密顿量的本征基中表示预测误差，分析波函数对齐损失与电子密度匹配损失各自只能约束部分误差，并指出轨道能平移不改变电子密度。据此提出以累积局域态密度（cumulative LDOS）为监督：它表示空间中各点能量低于给定阈值的电子数，包含电子密度为特例，能够响应误差的所有分量、唯一确定哈密顿量，并对较大轨道能误差给出不消失的梯度。将该方法称为 FermiScope，用于微调三个哈密顿网络。

Result: 在 QH9 上微调三个哈密顿网络后，FermiScope 降低了 HOMO-LUMO gap 误差且未增加逐元素误差；增益在分布外（OOD）和分子动力学（MD）划分上仍然存在，而在这些划分上波函数对齐损失帮助较小。

Conclusion: 累积 LDOS 是比逐元素匹配、波函数对齐损失和电子密度匹配更完整的训练信号，可同时约束哈密顿矩阵元素与谱性质；FermiScope 表明以该物理可观测量微调哈密顿模型能提升谱量精度并保持矩阵拟合质量，具有更好的泛化性。

Abstract: Kohn-Sham density functional theory (DFT) is the standard method for computing the electronic structure of molecules and materials, but its self-consistent-field (SCF) iterations make it expensive for large systems and high-throughput screening. Machine-learned Hamiltonian models offer a shortcut: an equivariant network predicts the Kohn-Sham Hamiltonian matrix in one forward pass, and diagonalizing the prediction yields orbital energies, the HOMO-LUMO gap, molecular orbitals and the electron density. These models are trained to match the reference matrix entry by entry, yet a small entry-wise error does not guarantee accurate eigenvalues or eigenvectors. Two physics-informed alternatives go partway: a recently proposed wavefunction-alignment loss adds a spectral penalty without diagonalizing the prediction, and matching the electron density of the diagonalized prediction supervises it directly. Neither is complete: by representing the prediction error in the eigenbasis of the reference Hamiltonian, we show that each sees only part of the error, and that shifting the predicted orbital energies leaves the electron density exactly unchanged. The same analysis identifies a complete training signal: the cumulative local density of states (cumulative LDOS), the number of electrons at each point in space with energy below a given threshold. The cumulative LDOS is a single physical observable that contains the electron density as a special case, responds to every component of the error, determines the Hamiltonian uniquely, and yields gradients that do not vanish for large orbitalenergy errors. Fine-tuning three Hamiltonian networks on QH9 in this way, which we call FermiScope, reduces the HOMO-LUMO gap error without increasing the element-wise error. The gains carry over to out-of-distribution and molecular-dynamics splits, where the wavefunction-alignment loss helps less.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The numerical stability of the hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) depends on the representation of their Liouvillian. Here, we introduce a continuous gauge freedom through a generalized Bogoliubov transformation with the parameter $r$. It subsumes earlier phase space stabilization methods as special cases and explains both their stabilizing mechanism and their limitations. We find that gauge optimization balances the suppression of hierarchy growth against errors in reconstructing the reduced density operator, and show that the factor $e^{2r}-1$ controls the feedback strength, with earlier methods located at the boundary for reconstruction-error amplification, $r=\frac{1}{2}\ln 2$. For spin-boson models, this boundary gauge recovers known stabilizations for undamped and weakly damped modes. In the weakly damped case, an interior gauge further reduces the trace error by more than an order of magnitude. For the Holstein model, interior gauges reduce trace drift by about an order of magnitude relative to the previously adopted fixed representation at the same bond dimension. This improvement stabilizes error-sensitive calculations of equilibrium autocorrelation functions. Our formulation thus extends fixed stabilization methods to a continuous family of HEOM representations generated by linear canonical transformations, and establishes a framework for improving numerical stability and accuracy through continuous gauge selection.

Abstract: Multireference calculations often require substantial human expertise and time to choose and refine an active space for the electronic states or chemical process of interest. Here, we ask whether scientific agentic systems (harnesses) can make these decisions reliably. Abstract: In this work, we propose an uncertainty-driven self-refinement learning method using adaptive Chebyshev-polynomial-based Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (ChebyKANs) to build potential energy surface (PES). Then, the multi-configurational time-dependent Hartree (MCTDH) method reproduces the non-adiabatic dynamics with results in close agreement with previously reported findings.

Abstract: Free energies of solvation are crucial to our understanding of chemical reactions, particularly when these take place in water. By means of atomic decompositions of total (bulk) energies from Kohn-Sham density functional theory in a basis of spatially localized molecular orbitals, the present study enables the computation of hydration free energies from local electronic-structure objects derived in the condensed phase. Specifically, our protocol computes an enthalpy of hydration as the sum of the decomposed change in internal energy of a solute upon solvation and a newly developed term that accounts for the response of the environment, which may then be combined with a standard expression for the corresponding change in entropy. In applications to eight neutral solutes in water, internal energies are all found to converge rapidly and in a smooth manner upon an increase in the size of the solvated cluster, and the corresponding enthalpies and free energies of hydration are likewise found to agree with experimental references within statistical uncertainty.

Main category: physics.chem-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的聚合物发现目前分散于数据库、模型、筛选、文献和模拟之间，缺乏可追溯的一体化流程。

Method: 平台整合多源性能查询、图神经网络预测、可信度感知多目标筛选、文献抽取、生成式候选探索及自动交接至分子动力学验证；基于 ADEPT-PolyGraphMT，协调实验、MD、DFT 和基团贡献数据，覆盖约 13,000 个真实和 100 万个虚拟聚合物的 28 种性能；筛选引擎结合可行性过滤、精确 Pareto 排序与综合相似性、物理一致性、预测不确定性和可合成性的 trust score，并进行多样性感知短名单筛选；同时提供约束自然语言接口。

Abstract: Data-driven polymer discovery remains fragmented across databases, models, screening, literature, and simulations. Ablation showed that trust-aware ranking shifted about one fifth of each shortlist toward more synthesizable chemistry closer to known polymers. A constrained natural-language interface translated a free-text request into a validated specification matching expert screening exactly, while staged literature evidence corroborated high glass-transition temperatures in polyimide-dominated shortlists against published experiments. PolyDiscover offers a traceable route from design intent to simulation-validated candidates, easing exploration of polymer chemical space.

</details>


### [24] [The Ensemble Ghost Interaction in Fractional-Occupation Hartree-Fock: Occupation Covariance, Pair Rank, and Prescribed Ensembles of Determinants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38873)

TL;DR: 论文分析了 FON-UHF 与 SA-UHF 的差异，将其归结为“系综鬼相互作用”G，并给出基于自旋轨道得失数目与占据数协方差的预判规则、轨道梯度和化学势误差公式，以及在 eT 程序中的实现和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FON-UHF 在非整数占据密度矩阵上计算 UHF 泛函，SA-UHF 则在共同轨道上对一组可能跨粒子数扇区的行列式能量取平均；二者物理意图相近但能量不同。作者希望明确二者差异 G 的来源、符号和消失条件，并评估其对轨道优化与化学势估计的影响。

Method: 将 G 定义为 FON-UHF 与 SA-UHF 的能量差。对两个行列式，用彼此间获得和失去的自旋轨道数目对 G 分类：pair rank one 时对所有轨道集 G 恒为零；pair rank two 时符号固定；若两个及以上自旋轨道仅被获得或仅被失去，符号也固定；其他情形符号依赖轨道。对更大系综，用占据数的成对协方差刻画 G 的消失：所有成对协方差为零时 G 对所有轨道集为零，完备基极限下也仅此时为零。相同成对项还给轨道梯度增加协方差项，并给出由系综 Fock 矩阵估计化学势的误差。方法在 eT 程序中实现。

Result: 数值计算表明：六氨合钴(III/II) 对在半转移时 G=0.24 Hartree；苯的简并前线轨道间共享分数占据时，G 达 FON-UHF 惩罚的 78–84%；两个堆叠乙烯分子间共享空穴时 G 趋于极限 1/(4R)。

Conclusion: G 可在计算前由自旋轨道得失结构和占据数协方差分类，其非零会显著影响分数占据/系综平均 UHF 的能量、轨道梯度和化学势估计；因此比较或使用 FON-UHF 与 SA-UHF 时必须显式考虑系综鬼相互作用。

Abstract: Fractional Occupation Number unrestricted Hartree-Fock (FON-UHF) evaluates the UHF functional on a density matrix with non-integer occupations. Sector-Averaged UHF (SA-UHF) averages the energies of a prescribed ensemble of determinants, possibly from different particle-number sectors, on common orbitals. The two differ by the ensemble ghost interaction G. We show that, for two determinants, the numbers of spin-orbitals gained and lost between them classify G before any calculation. G vanishes on every orbital set at pair rank one, has a fixed sign at pair rank two and whenever two or more spin-orbitals are only gained or only lost, and otherwise has an orbital-dependent sign. For larger ensembles, G vanishes on every orbital set when all pair covariances of the occupations vanish, and in the complete-basis limit only then. The same pairs add a covariance term to the orbital gradient and give the error of the chemical potential estimated from the ensemble Fock matrix. We implemented both methods in the eT program. Abstract: Quantum hardware noise remains a major obstacle to accurate molecular simulations using the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE). <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续半个世纪的反离子凝聚理论主要基于无盐理想线电荷，给出尖锐的电荷重正化；本文旨在解决真实开放域、含盐体系以及所有电荷区间下的有效电荷与离子气氛问题。

Abstract: For over half a century, the counterion condensation theory has served as the dominant view for highly charged cylinders, prescribing a sharp charge renormalization based on the idealized abstraction of a salt-free line charge. In this work, we resolve this problem for physical open-domain systems across all charge regimes using the method of matched asymptotics applied to the non-linear Poisson--Boltzmann equation with added salt. For subcritical fields, where the dimensionless charge parameter satisfies $\xi < 1$, cylindrical curvature suppresses non-linear coupling, leaving the diffuse atmosphere unmodified. Beyond the critical threshold ($\xi = 1$), where the linear theory breaks down due to a localized Boltzmann explosion, the counterion accumulation smoothly transitions into a local flat-wall near-field geometry. By matching these zones, we construct a unified, parameter-free composite analytical solution capturing the spatial crossover toward the global cylindrical Debye screening tail. In the supercritical regime ($\xi > 1$), this near-field regularization locks the far-field effective charge into distinct, concentration-dependent parallel plateaus. Crucially, within the realistic regime where $\xi \le 5$, these idealized plateaus are not always reached, and the effective charge operates within a smooth, variable transition zone. Abstract: Recent advances in femtosecond extreme ultraviolet photoelectron spectroscopy have enabled insight into photochemistry in gases, condensed phases, and at interfaces. The pump field prepares the excited-state ensemble under experimental conditions, while the multireference treatment describes excited states, conical intersections, and bond-breaking coordinates. Compared with Dyson-norm-based intensities, plane- and Coulomb-wave continuum functions improve the early-time shape of the lowest binding energy signal and show that photoelectron anisotropy is more sensitive to the continuum approximation than the angle-integrated spectrum. More generally, these findings demonstrate how explicit-pulse nonadiabatic dynamics and dynamically correlated multireference electronic structure can resolve competing photochemical reaction pathways through simulated fs-EUV-TRPES.

Abstract: Electronic grand-canonical density functional theory captures how structure and electronic charge respond to electrode potential and, together with statistical mechanics, how these responses give rise to emergent phenomena. Abstract: Polaritonic chemistry holds the promise of controlling chemical reactivity through confined light-matter interactions, yet how reactions respond inside optical cavities remains debated. Here, we take a more zoomed-out view, inspired by electrostatic catalysis. We extend nonempirical linear free-energy relationships to cavity-modified reactions and show that their behavior falls into four distinct reaction types set by simple response-crossing rules. Reactions can follow conventional Bell-Evans-Polanyi (BEP) or counterintuitive Opposite-BEP behavior regardless of cavity tuning, or switch between the two as the cavity frequency is varied. We establish this picture analytically and demonstrate it in the Shin Metiu model and in four molecular reactions treated with ab initio electronic-structure methods. Even at this deliberately coarse-grained level, cavity-modified reactivity already shows qualitatively different and strongly counterintuitive behavior, including reactions that switch character with cavity frequency. Yet this complexity can still be reduced to a simple competition between permanent-dipole and polarizability responses. This reaction-type classification therefore provides a new route to organize and anticipate cavity-modified reactivity.

Abstract: Molecular polaritons have attracted great interest in recent years as a promising avenue for developing optical control of chemical processes. A major open question in this field is the role of quantum optical effects in understanding the molecular dynamics observed in cavities. In this work, we use our recently developed mean-field quantum and full-quantum real-time nuclear--electronic orbital time-dependent density functional theory methods to simulate photon statistics for a cavity mode strongly coupled to a molecular vibrational transition. Our comparative use of these two methods allows us to isolate the contributions of light--matter entanglement to the photon statistics. We consider three initial conditions of the cavity mode: a coherent state, a squeezed coherent state, and a single photon Fock state. These conditions span Poissonian, super-Poissonian, and sub-Poissonian photon statistics. Our full-quantum dynamics initialized with a coherent state show considerable super-Poissonian fluctuations of the photon statistics and light-squeezing of the cavity photons by the vibrational mode. These dynamics are particularly notable considering the quasi-classical properties of the coherent state. The squeezed coherent and Fock state initial conditions also demonstrate noteworthy fluctuations in the photon statistics. All three initial conditions reveal dynamics in the full-quantum photon statistics that differ considerably from the statistics obtained with the mean-field quantum method. Our results demonstrate the importance of considering quantum optical effects for molecules under strong coupling in certain situations.

Abstract: Strong electron correlation remains difficult to describe within conventional density functional approximations, particularly when natural-orbital occupations become strongly fractional. We introduce a systematic particle-hole expansion that generalizes density functional theory (DFT) through an explicit functional of the one-electron reduced density matrix. The previously derived cumulant-based correction emerges as the leading term and the thermal (entropic) correction as an infinite-order member whose coefficients are fixed by a single parameter. The expansion can be formulated as a moment problem solved by semidefinite programming with computational scaling comparable to DFT. We show that both the first- and second-order corrections yield closed-form natural-orbital occupations. Applications to periacenes and Fe-porphyrin demonstrate that the second-order correction substantially reduces excessive orbital fractionalization, yielding natural-orbital occupations and fundamental gaps in improved agreement with correlated calculations.

</details>

Abstract: We construct a time evolution operator for a dynamical quantum models with a multi-component random Gaussian field characterized by a long correlation time. We analyze three paradigmatic cases: non-Abelian $SU(2)$ symmetry class for the three-component Gaussian noise and Abelian symmetry classes $U(1)$ and $Z_2$ for the two- and one-component noises correspondingly. Starting with a limiting case of the infinite correlation time (Keldysh model) we shown that statistics of the two-component random field with a non-zero mean is described by the Rice distribution. We derived and solved the system of coupled linear differential equations for the exact diagonal and off-diagonal single-particle Green's functions. We showed how the finite time correlation in Keldysh models are accounted by a continuous fractions method.

Abstract: Free fermions coupled to particle gain and loss relax to a Gaussian steady state, in which every natural orbital has its own occupation. We find the distribution of these occupations $\varrho_{\rm st}$ for a random Hamiltonian coupled to many random gain and loss channels. Each orbital is filled and emptied at its own rates, and the Hamiltonian, moving particles across the levels during their lifetime, averages the differences between the orbitals. We show that this averaging is incomplete: the spread of the occupations decreases only as the inverse square root of the number of channels per level. At large system size the distribution depends on the grain Hamiltonian only through its density of states $\nu(E)$. The spread is bounded by the baths alone: even Hamiltonians with unbounded, heavy-tailed spectra cannot widen it beyond this bound. The spread suppresses the particle-number fluctuations below their binomial value.

Abstract: Artificial spin ices are geometric arrangement of nanomagnets that exhibit reconfigurable magnetization states and GHz dynamics. Their investigation in the GHz regime is challenging because of the existence of multiple modes and the impact of magnetization deviations at the edges of the nanomagnets. A minimal model to capture such dynamics require three macrospins per nanomagnet, an approach that was implemented for generic artificial spin ices in the tool Gaenice. Here, we use the Gaenice framework to realize a relaxation method to stabilize magnetization states self-consistently, called GaeniceN. We implement the dissipative component of the Landau-Lifshitz equation in an iterative approach to efficiently converge to the relaxed state. As an example, we find our approach to correctly predict the transition between phases in a pyramid ASI subject to a low-magnitude external field by computing the system's average energy. Because they are rooted on the same framework, GaeniceN produces the initial condition needed for Gaenice to compute dynamics such as ferromagnetic resonance or band structures. Combined, these tools will be able to aid in the design of artificial spin ices with applications in magnonics.

</details>

Abstract: In a ballistic system, the anomalous dc Hall response generically depends on microscopic details, including the geometric embedding of the orbitals and the precise implementation of the measurement. Here, we consider the question under what circumstances the anomalous Hall conductivity can be regarded as an intrinsic property of a system. We show that the full dc Hall response $\sigma_{xy}^{\rm H}$ becomes embedding-independent in the presence of relaxation by impurity scattering, although the individual (intrinsic) band and (extrinsic) side-jump and anomalous-distribution contributions to the Hall conductivity separately remain embedding-dependent. We further derive a symmetry criterion for when the band term itself becomes embedding-independent, but argue that this alone neither identifies it with the full Hall conductivity $\sigma_{xy}^{\rm H}$, nor makes it a reliable approximation for $\sigma_{xy}^{\rm H}$. More broadly, our results establish embedding dependence as a diagnostic of whether a response coefficient represents a stationary intrinsic property of a material or retains sensitivity to measurement protocol.

Abstract: Motivated by few-electron partitioning experiments [Shaju et al., Nature 642, 928 (2025)], we study the partition statistics of $N$-electron states at a fractional quantum Hall point contact. Abstract: Semiconductor quantum dots emitting in the telecommunication O- and C-bands are promising candidates as single-photon sources for fiber-based quantum communication. In this work, we show how coherent quasi-resonant and phonon-assisted excitation help reveal the intrinsic optical properties of InAs quantum dots embedded in relaxed InGaAs matrices on top of metamorphic InGaAs layers for achieving emission in the telecom bands. This allows us to observe linewidths as narrow as 21 $\mu$eV, the disappearance of slow decay channels, and single-photon purity reaching second-order autocorrelation values in the order of $10^{-2}$. Abstract: Twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) near the first magic angle ($\theta\approx1.05^{\circ}$) hosts flat bands that provide a fertile ground for exploring strongly correlated and topological phases. The extent to which moir\'e lattice reconstruction at different length scales affects the electronic structure through electro-mechanical coupling remains unresolved. The additional inclusion of atomic lattice dilation enerates a strong flexo-electric response that explicitly breaks particle-hole symmetry, pushing compact, localized $f$-states at the AA sites down in energy. Consequently, these heavy fermion states energetically coexist with itinerant $c$-electrons at the AB/BA sites. Our spatially resolved spectroscopic measurements quantitatively visualize this coexistence, confirming the persistence of the heavy fermion model in the small-angle regime $\theta<1^{\circ}$. Our findings provide microscopic insights into how flexo-electricity affects the low-energy electronic structure of small-angle TBG. More broadly, our study establishes small-angle TBG as a new class of moir\'e materials to explore the interacting Kondo lattice problem with high tunability of the $f-$occupation and $c-$density through twist-angle and electrostatic back gates, respectively.

Instead, the absolute field-induced transmission change peaks at an AlScN thickness of approximately 100nm, with the precise optimum depending on the acoustic wavelength. Abstract: Spatially resolved control of coherent electron transport offers a route to understanding and engineering interference phenomena in hybrid superconducting devices. An analytical model identifies the origin of this local response with coherent electron lensing, in which the tip modifies the phase accumulated along interfering trajectories.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者构建 HERA，利用实验室成员整理的知识库（测量协议、软件工具、文献）自主执行低温输运测量。评估包括：与专家对两个多层石墨烯器件堆叠顺序进行盲法对照；对知识库进行消融；数百次离线重放，让模型仅设计核心测量而不实际执行；并迁移到另一材料体系寻找与表征超导性。

Result: HERA 在两个器件上的结论均与专家密封结论一致；消融与重放研究表明，使用知识库后各模型都能设计更好的测量并进行更可靠的推理；在新材料体系中，专家用数小时制定新实验协议后，HERA 可自主完成超导性的发现与表征。

Conclusion: 结果表明，配备面向任务设计的知识库后，语言模型智能体能够开展可靠、可审查的实验科学，并具备向新问题扩展的能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents have begun to carry out laboratory measurements, but their scientific conclusions are useful only if they hold up under scrutiny. Demonstrating this requires controlled studies because models are stochastic and the context a model is given steers its conclusions. Here we introduce HERA, an autonomous agent designed to carry out cryogenic transport measurements of quantum devices. HERA draws on a knowledge base curated by lab members that holds measurement protocols, software tools, and literature references. We assessed HERA with a blinded trial against an expert on the stacking order of two multilayer graphene devices, an ablation of its knowledge base, and hundreds of offline replays in which models designed the trial's central measurement without running it. HERA's conclusions matched the expert's sealed conclusions on both tested devices. The ablation and the replay studies measured the impact of the knowledge base across different models, showing that with it every model designed better measurements and reasoned more soundly. To test how readily HERA extends to a new problem, we tasked it with finding and characterizing superconductivity in a different material system. This required a few hours of expert time to develop a new experimental protocol, after which HERA did both autonomously. These results show that, with a knowledge base designed for the task, language model agents are capable of sound experimental science.

</details>

*Oles Matsyshyn,Valla Fatemi,Justin C. W. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出基于Berry曲率偶极（BCD）的量子电路元件，在超导微波电路中实现无需破坏时间反演的量子三波混频，并演示多光子真空Rabi分裂与玻色薛定谔猫态的动力学稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子三波混频通常依赖Josephson结阵列并由磁通激活，往往涉及时间反演对称性破缺；需要一种无需磁场、且保持时间反演对称性的相干非线性量子资源。

Method: 利用非中心对称量子材料中的BCD非线性霍尔效应，将其耗散less、量子相干少光子区与超导微波电路集成，构建BCD电路量子电动力学（circuit QED）平台。

Result: 实现不破坏时间反演对称性的量子三波混频；展示多光子真空Rabi分裂；实现玻色薛定谔猫态的动力学稳定。

Conclusion: BCD电路QED将量子材料的非线性响应转化为量子信息处理的物理资源，为超导量子电路提供可集成的相干三波混频方案。

Abstract: Quantum three-wave mixing is an important resource in superconducting quantum circuits. The traditional implementation uses Josephson junction arrays and is activated by magnetic flux. Here we introduce the Berry curvature dipole (BCD) quantum circuit element, which achieves quantum three-wave mixing without breaking time-reversal symmetry. This quantum three-wave mixing results from the BCD nonlinear Hall effect in noncentrosymmetric quantum materials taken to a dissipationless, quantum coherent few-photon regime. We show how nonlinear Hall materials can be integrated to superconducting microwave circuits to accomplish multiphoton vacuum Rabi splittings and dynamical stabilization of bosonic Schr\"odinger cat states. Such BCD circuit quantum electrodynamics are a concrete exemplar of a physical resource for quantum information processing enabled by nonlinear response in quantum materials.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 针对具有子晶格对称界面的FM/AM与AFM/AM结，分析热驱动磁子自旋流中界面角无关分量和d波角度依赖分量，并结合跨界面成对自旋的选择性隧穿机制解释输运。

Result: FM/AM结中角度依赖分量为奇函数，在AM畴平均后抵消；AFM/AM结中角度无关分量因对称性消失，而角度依赖分量在固定AFM奈尔矢量下经畴平均仍保留。选择性隧穿使平行成对自旋透射磁子、反平行则阻挡，从而使AFM/AM结成为磁子阀；反平行键合自旋可将电流阻塞至受磁子子晶格混合限制的程度。该各向异性隧穿表现为[310]与[130]法向界面间电流符号反转，阀效应可通过固定界面下电流大小读取AM相对于AFM的奈尔矢量。

Conclusion: d波交变磁体结中的热磁子自旋流具有可调控的角度依赖和畴平均特性，AFM/AM界面可实现磁子阀并用于探测交变磁体与反铁磁体的奈尔矢量相对取向。

Abstract: We investigate the thermally driven magnon spin current across a junction between a $d$-wave altermagnet (AM) and a ferromagnet (FM) or an antiferromagnet (AFM).
  The current consists of a component independent of the interface angle and an angularly dependent component with the $d$-wave form.
  For a sublattice-symmetric interface, the two junctions behave oppositely under reversal of the altermagnetic domain: in the FM/AM junction the angularly dependent component is odd and cancels on averaging over the domains, whereas in the AFM/AM junction the angularly independent component vanishes by symmetry and the angularly dependent component survives the domain average at a fixed N\'eel vector of the AFM.
  Both follow from a selective tunneling: a pair of spins bonded across the interface transmits magnons when the spins are parallel and not when antiparallel, which makes the AFM/AM junction a magnon valve: the configuration in which the bonded spins are antiparallel blocks the current up to the sublattice admixture of the magnons.
  The anisotropic tunneling is detectable as the sign reversal of the current between the interfaces normal to $[310]$ and $[130]$, and the valve reads the N\'eel vector of the altermagnet relative to that of the antiferromagnet through the magnitude of the current at a fixed interface.

</details>

Abstract: Solid-state color centers embedded into mechanical resonators, where orbital states mediate interactions between spins and long-lived phonons, are a promising platform for hybrid quantum systems. The negatively charged silicon vacancy center in diamond (SiV-) is particularly favorable for strain-coupling to nanomechanical resonators because of its large spin-strain susceptibility and compatibility with device integration. Tensioned nanomechanical resonators offer an attractive route due to their ultra-long mechanical coherence times enabled by diluting defect-induced dissipation, though it is unclear if the same dilution effect undermines strain-mediated coupling to material defect centers. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a tensioned diamond nanobeam platform that enables enhanced strain coupling to SiV- orbital states. Through monitoring the optical response of the SiVs to the static and dynamical strain of the mechanical resonator, we observe that dynamical strain localizes near the clamps of these resonators under static strain. This allows us to enhance SiV-mechanical coupling and the mechanical coherence simultaneously, and provide a SiV-strain sensitivity with greatly improved accuracy over previous literature values. Thus, our work opens a pathway towards seconds-long coherence hybrid quantum systems utilizing point defects in diamond nanomechanics for quantum information processing.

</details>

Abstract: Multilayer graphenes have emerged as a versatile avenue towards exploring novel electronic phases of matter. Recently, nanoscale multilayer graphene islands have been realized experimentally, where the island is less than 50 nm in diameter and distinct in layer configuration from the contact portion of the same graphene sheets, the latter of which act as leads for transport probes. Here, we theoretically investigate how the proximity between these islands and their chemically identical environment modifies the electronic structure. Within the tight-binding approximation, we find that the proximity effect is enhanced by the closely matched momenta between both parts, creating an unusually strong band renormalization which persists as the island size is increased, and only limited by the coherence length. An effective single-unit-cell Hamiltonian to capture these modifications is derived, expressed in terms of interlayer hopping parameters that scale with the relative island area. We predict a substantially altered low-energy density of states for 3- and 4-layer graphene islands, demonstrating the tunability of the electronic structure by spatial size.

</details>


### [55] [Multiple Current Enhancement Regimes and Interaction Induced Re-entrant Transport in a Quasiperiodic Hubbard Ring with Antiferromagnetic Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39529)
*Souvik Roy,Ranjini Bhattacharya*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究一维Aharonov-Bohm量子环中由广义AAH调制、交错格点势和反铁磁序共同导致的持续电荷流与自旋流，并比较非相互作用与Hartree-Fock平均场相互作用情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在阐明准周期调制、反铁磁序、能带填充和电子关联如何共同调控持续电流的幅度、自旋分辨特征及多重增强区间。

Result: 半满时随调制增强出现两个持续电流增强峰，准周期跃迁调制对双峰结构的形成至关重要；自旋分辨键流显示两峰间主导自旋贡献发生切换。四分之一填充时自旋流仅有一个增强峰。Hubbard相互作用对半满双峰影响不同：抑制主峰，而次峰呈非单调、可重入响应，并在有限相互作用下恢复电流。ED和DMRG支持平均场描述的可靠性。

Conclusion: 准周期调制、反铁磁序、能带填充与电子关联共同决定持续电流的幅度和自旋分辨性质，可产生多个增强区间以及相互作用诱导的可重入行为。

Abstract: We investigate persistent charge and spin currents in a one-dimensional quantum ring threaded by an Aharonov-Bohm (AB) flux, considering generalized Aubry-Andr\'e-Harper (AAH) modulation with staggered onsite energies and antiferromagnetic (AFM) ordering in both the noninteracting and interacting regimes, with the latter treated using a self-consistent Hartree-Fock mean field theory (MFT). At half filling, two distinct current-enhancement peaks emerge as the modulation strength increases, with quasiperiodic hopping modulation playing a crucial role in establishing the two-peak structure within a restricted parameter regime. Spin-resolved bond-current profiles reveal a switch in the dominant spin contribution between the two peaks, while the inverse participation ratio (IPR) and flux-dependent spin-resolved ground-state energies provide complementary insights into the spatial characteristics of the eigenstates and their spin-dependent flux sensitivity. At quarter filling, the spin current exhibits a single enhancement peak, in contrast to the two-peak response observed at half filling. The Hubbard interaction affects the two half-filled peaks differently: it suppresses the primary peak, whereas the secondary peak exhibits a nonmonotonic, re-entrant response, with the current recovering at finite interaction strength. The interacting results are further characterized through the Drude weight, and comparisons with exact diagonalization (ED) and density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations for a finite half-filled system support the reliability of the MFT description. Our findings elucidate how quasiperiodic modulation, AFM order, band filling, and electronic correlations collectively govern the magnitude and spin-resolved character of persistent currents, giving rise to multiple enhancement regimes and interaction-induced re-entrant behavior.

</details>

*Prathyush P. Poduval,Rofii,Muhammad S. Ukhtary,Thomas L. Schmidt,Eddwi H. Hasdeo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文用半经典库仑相互作用模型研究陈数 \(\mathcal{C}\) 与 \(-\mathcal{C}\) 畴壁处多条手征边缘态的集体等离激元，并揭示陈数对边缘等离激元诱导轨道磁化（逆法拉第效应）的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶陈绝缘体具有按陈数倍增的无耗散手征边缘态；作者希望理解这些边缘态的集体等离激元行为，特别是陈数如何决定等离激元诱导的静态轨道磁化，并与传统边缘等离激元区分。

Method: 采用包含库仑相互作用的半经典模型，分析陈数为 \(\mathcal{C}\) 与 \(-\mathcal{C}\) 的畴壁处边缘等离激元的色散、电场分布及逆法拉第效应；通过边缘态空间尺度与等离激元波长两个竞争长度尺度描述磁化空间分布。

Result: 长波极限下等离激元呈线性色散，速度随 \(\mathcal{C}\) 线性增加；边缘等离激元产生向体内衰减的椭圆偏振电场，驱动电子做圆周运动并形成静态轨道磁化。拓扑边缘等离激元的磁化具有由边缘态有限宽度和等离激元波长共同决定的空间分布；固定边缘电荷幅度时磁化随 \(\mathcal{C}\) 显著增强；在畴壁处低频趋于有限平台，高频按 \(1/\omega^2\) 衰减；温度依赖非单调，并在中间温度出现最大值。

Conclusion: 陈数可作为调控等离激元诱导轨道磁化的关键参数；高阶陈绝缘体中的拓扑边缘等离激元在磁化空间分布、频率和温度依赖上明显不同于传统边缘等离激元。

Abstract: Higher Chern insulators host multiple dissipationless chiral edge states, one per unit of Chern number. We study the collective plasmon modes of these edge states at a domain wall between Chern numbers $\mathcal{C}$ and $-\mathcal{C}$, using a semiclassical model with Coulomb interactions. The plasmon dispersion is linear at long wavelengths, with a velocity that increases linearly with $\mathcal{C}$. As in conventional two-dimensional metals, the edge plasmon generates an elliptically polarized electric field that decays into the bulk and drives electrons in circular orbits, producing a static orbital magnetization (inverse Faraday effect). For topological edge plasmons, the magnetization has a nontrivial spatial profile set by two competing length scales: the finite extent of the edge states and the plasmon wavelength. At fixed edge-charge amplitude, it grows strongly with $\mathcal{C}$. At the domain wall, it approaches a finite plateau at low frequency and decays as $1/\omega^2$ at high frequency. Its temperature dependence is nonmonotonic, with a maximum at an intermediate temperature. These results show how the Chern number controls plasmon-induced magnetization, in contrast to conventional edge plasmons.

</details>

*Debasis Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态计算受大脑神经元与突触机制启发，有望在硬件中实现复杂神经网络；但高密度、低功耗的神经形态电路需要具备可调突触权重和非易失信息存储能力的器件。

Method: 以综述/章节形式阐述eNVM器件在神经形态计算中的应用，涵盖阻变存储器（ReRAM）、相变存储器（PCM）、自旋电子存储器等，并讨论其挑战、机遇及近期系统集成进展。

Result: 指出eNVM器件因电导调制可模拟突触可塑性，且具备非易失性和可扩展性，适合大规模神经形态架构；但摘要未给出具体实验数据或量化结果。

Conclusion: eNVM是推进神经形态计算硬件实现的重要候选技术，未来需继续应对器件与集成挑战，并推动其在类脑系统中的实际应用。

Abstract: This chapter explores the critical role of emerging non-volatile memory (eNVM) devices in advancing neuromorphic computing. Inspired by the human brain's remarkable ability to perform complex tasks like pattern recognition and adaptation, neuromorphic computing offers immense potential for implementing complex neural networks (NNs) in real hardware. Despite ongoing research into the brain's complexities, its fundamental operation relies on neurons (processing units communicating via electrochemical signals) and synapses (junctions between neurons). The modifiable strength of the synapse is key to learning and memory functionality, allowing the brain to adapt and rewire itself. For dense and low-power circuits in neuromorphic computing, these eNVM devices are ideal due to their synaptic-like behavior and non-volatile information storage. Properties such as conductance modulation of these devices emulate synaptic plasticity, which is crucial for learning, and their scalability makes them promising for large-scale neuromorphic architectures. This chapter will delve into the various types of eNVM devices such as resistive RAM (ReRAM), phase-change memory (PCM), spintronics-based memory devices, and their applications in neuromorphic computing, discussing their challenges and opportunities, and highlighting recent advances in their integration within neuromorphic systems.

</details>


### [58] [Moir\'e-enabled chiral plasmons in twisted bilayer graphene nanoislands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39737)
*Magnus Husted,Viktor Kanstrup,Eduardo J. C. Dias,Joel D. Cox*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层石墨烯扭转形成的moiré超晶格会重塑电子结构，但其如何影响有限纳米尺度双层的集体光学响应仍 largely unexplored。

Abstract: Multiterminal Josephson junctions have recently attracted substantial attention. The ability to study Andreev bound states in a multi-phase parameter space opens up exciting avenues for generating topologically non-trivial phases in superconducting devices. A particularly interesting case is the trijunction based on a topological insulator (TI), which is predicted to host Majorana zero modes for suitable phase configurations (Fu and Kane, PRL 2008). Here we turn to the platform of the side-contacted TI trijunction and present experimental evidence for a multiterminal Josephson effect. We study the transport properties and find typical features of a two-dimensional critical current contour (CCC) as well as bias regimes in which only a single junction remains dissipationless. We pay particular attention to these features and study the effects of magnetic fields on single junctions within the trijunction, where, depending on the field direction, the supercurrents show Fraunhofer patterns, SQUID oscillations, or a monotonic decay. We find good agreement with semiclassical simulations for a single TI junction that take into account the top and bottom surface states. We observe pronounced non-convex shape deformations of the CCC under perpendicular magnetic fields that reflect the flux buildup in the loop formed by our trijunctions and provide a simple model sufficient to explain the observed effect. Our results provide the foundations for future studies of Majorana zero modes on this platform.

Moreover, we observe a strong modulation of the Ginzburg-Landau coherence length from 467 nm to 145 nm as the in-plane magnetic field increases from 0 to 2 T, whereas the critical current Ic barely changes. Unlike previous studies in RMG, we observe a competition between superconductivity and a correlated insulator at v = -2, monitored by displacement fields and twist angles. Note that we also observe integer Chern insulators with C = 1, 3, and -3 at v = 1/2, 1, and 3, respectively, for large D > 0 in the same superconducting device. Abstract: Coupling quantum materials to electromagnetic cavities or resonators without external driving has gained prominence as a new control mechanism. Here we develop an equilibrium theory of a mesoscopic, disordered two-dimensional superconductor coupled to a subwavelength terahertz resonator, focusing on the interplay between superconducting phase fluctuations in the sample and electromagnetic fluctuations of the resonator. Modeling the resonator as a lumped-element circuit, we show that the coupling enhances, rather than suppresses, the superconducting transition temperature $T_c$. When the sample is smaller than the Pearl length, phase fluctuations near the Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) transition strongly amplify this enhancement. Crucially, the resistive quasiparticle response of the sample must be included in the analysis: at low frequencies the superfluid shunts the normal-fluid channel, reducing the total sample impedance. For representative parameters, a $1 \,\mu\mathrm{m} \times 1 \,\mu\mathrm{m}$, $8 $nm-thick disordered film, we find a relative $T_c$ enhancement of about $1\%$, an order of magnitude larger than the $0.1\% $ obtained when phase fluctuations are neglected. These results identify superconductors with soft phase fluctuations as promising platforms for equilibrium control with terahertz resonators.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广义转移矩阵方法主要针对无自旋或标量规范情形，缺乏对自旋自由度及矩阵值阿贝尔/非阿贝尔规范场的系统处理；作者希望建立可同时描述非厄米谱、边界条件和自旋相关输运的统一框架。

Abstract: We extend the generalized transfer-matrix method introduced in [H. Ghaemi-Dizicheh, Phys.\ Rev.\ B \textbf{107}, 125155 (2023)] to one-dimensional spinful non-Hermitian tight-binding lattices with matrix-valued Abelian and non-Abelian gauge couplings. We derive a minimal channel-resolved transfer matrix and identify its eigenvalues with the generalized Bloch factors. The same transfer matrix determines the periodic- and open-boundary spectra, generalized Brillouin zone, skin localization, and finite-sample scattering conditions. In the Abelian limit, the model separates into fixed spin sectors, yielding exact spectral results and closed finite-length transport expressions in terms of Chebyshev polynomials. These results reveal bipolar spin-dependent skin localization and distinguish the reciprocal hopping scale, which governs poles, zeros, and reflectionlessness, from the directed-amplification factor controlling transmission asymmetry, transparency, and invisibility. For non-Abelian gauge fields, noncommuting link matrices coherently mix the spin sectors, such that four transfer eigenmodes jointly determine the non-Bloch spectrum and transport eigenchannels. We show that this mixing produces spin-hybridized lasing and coherent-perfect-absorption channels, Zeeman-tunable lasing thresholds, and direction-dependent reflectionless transport accompanied by pronounced spin conversion. The resulting boundary-condition criteria describe lasing, coherent perfect absorption, reflectionlessness, transparency, and invisibility, while identifying spin polarization and conversion as experimentally accessible signatures that distinguish Abelian from non-Abelian non-Hermitian transport.

</details>


### [67] [Micromagnetic Lagrangian and Hamiltonian Normal-Mode Formulation of Cavity Magnon-Photon Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40416)
*Claudio Serpico,Marco Lembo,Giovanni Miano*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文建立了一个从线性化Maxwell-LL方程出发的多模磁子-光子耦合框架，避免传统磁矢势描述在模态截断下的不准确性，并给出可量子化的极化激元基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于磁矢势的描述会出现大静磁项相消，在模态截断下不准确，因此需要更稳健的磁子-光子相互作用建模与量子化方法。

Method: 引入电矢势和相互作用Lagrangian，将磁化与腔场按未耦合磁子和电磁子系统的本征模展开，耦合系数由本征函数重叠积分给出；针对陀螺项导致的非正则Lagrangian，用Faddeev-Jackiw方法构造Hamiltonian和Poisson括号，并通过广义Bogoliubov变换对角化得到正则极化激元变量。

Result: 应用于矩形微波腔中的垂直磁化YIG圆盘时，发现腔模与多个静磁模发生杂化；当样品靠近导体壁时，出现明显的腔壁诱导频率移动。

Conclusion: 该框架避免了传统磁矢势方法中静磁项抵消和模态截断误差问题，为多模磁子-光子耦合提供了自然的量子化极化激元描述，并揭示腔壁对模式杂化和频率修正的显著影响。

Abstract: We investigate magnon-photon interactions in a resonant cavity starting from the coupled Maxwell and Landau-Lifshitz equations linearized about an arbitrary micromagnetic ground state. By introducing an electric vector potential and a suitable interaction Lagrangian, the formulation avoids the cancellation of large magnetostatic contributions that arises in the conventional magnetic-vector-potential description and becomes inaccurate under modal truncation. The magnetization and cavity fields are expanded in the normal modes of the uncoupled magnetic and electromagnetic subsystems, with coupling coefficients given by overlap integrals of the corresponding eigenfunctions. The resulting multimode Lagrangian has a noncanonical structure due to the gyrotropic terms, and the corresponding Hamiltonian formulation is obtained using the Faddeev-Jackiw approach. The radiative magnon-photon interaction is encoded in the resulting Poisson-bracket structure, while the cavity-induced modification of the magnetostatic field enters explicitly in the Hamiltonian. The coupled system is diagonalized through a generalized Bogoliubov transformation, yielding canonical polariton variables that provide a natural basis for quantization. Application to a perpendicularly magnetized YIG disk in a rectangular microwave cavity shows hybridization between the cavity mode and several magnetostatic modes, together with appreciable cavity-wall-induced frequency shifts when the sample is placed close to the conducting walls.

</details>


### [68] [Electric field control of anisotropic spin traffic between spin-filtering and magnon-scattering in a layered antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40423)
*Mohamed Soliman,Jaime Garrido Aldea,Davy Borowski,Dorye L. Esteras,Neeraj K. Rajak,Niels Chapuis,Abdelkarim Ouerghi,Arnaud Gloppe,J\'er\^ome Robert,Bernard Doudin,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Jos\'e H. Garc\'ia,Stephan Roche,Jean-Fran\c{c}ois Dayen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于层状A型反铁磁半导体CrSBr的双栅场效应晶体管可通过位移场独立调控自旋输运，位移场而非掺杂决定磁阻比的符号与大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状A型反铁磁半导体兼具稳健磁性与可栅控半导体特性，是自旋电子器件的潜力平台；但需要明确载流子密度与位移场对磁输运的独立作用及微观机制。

Method: 制备CrSBr双栅场效应晶体管，独立控制载流子密度与位移场；开展系统磁输运测量，结合第一性原理计算与有效自旋电路模型分析各向异性磁输运。

Result: 位移场主导磁阻比（MRR）的符号和幅度；识别出负与正MRR分量，并将其分别关联到层间与层内自旋相关输运机制；理论计算与自旋电路模型复现了各向异性磁输运。

Conclusion: 位移场是调控CrSBr自旋输运的高效电学参数，多栅架构有望推广至其他层状反铁磁半导体以实现类似自旋输运控制。

Abstract: Layered A-type antiferromagnetic (AFM) semiconductors offer a unique platform for spintronic devices by combining robust magnetism with gate-tunable semiconducting properties. We present a dual-gate field-effect transistor based on CrSBr, a layered A-type AFM with pronounced transport anisotropy and complex spin dynamics. Our device provides independent control over carrier density and displacement field. Systematic transport measurements reveal that the displacement field, rather than doping, predominantly governs the sign and magnitude of magnetoresistance ratio (MRR). We identify the origins of both negative and positive MRR components and connect them to interlayer and intralayer spin-dependent transport mechanisms. We interpret these findings with ab initio calculations and an effective spin-circuit model that reproduces the anisotropic magnetotransport. Our results identify the displacement field as an efficient electrical control parameter of spin transport in CrSBr and suggest that multi-gate architectures could provide similar control in other layered antiferromagnetic semiconductors.

</details>


### [69] [Time Reflection-Refraction Crossover at Topological Kramers Edge States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40442)
*Junpeng Hou,Ya-Jie Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究量子自旋霍尔（QSH）螺旋边缘态在空间均匀时间调制下的动力学，证明可在同一拓扑相内实现时域反射-折射交叉，并通过体态虚跃迁实现固定晶体动量下反向传播边缘通道的相干转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态非磁性背散射受时间反演对称性保护而被禁止，但如何在不破坏拓扑保护的前提下主动调控螺旋边缘态并探测其量子几何仍待解决。

Method: 对QSH相施加空间均匀、保持瞬时哈密顿量时间反演对称性的时间调制；利用有限体隙和Kramers交叉处消失的边缘劈裂，使调制对体态激发有抑制而对边缘通道非绝热；分析经体态的虚跃迁、$qT$标度（$q$为动量偏移、$T$为ramp持续时间）及相干干涉；在绝热极限追踪初始时间反演配对波包。

Result: 发现时域反射-折射交叉；固定ramp形状下近交叉处由$qT$主导交叉动力学，随ramp变慢相干干涉可抑制并恢复反射；绝热极限下两初始时间反演波包沿各自传播分支获得相等几何位移，群速度贡献相消，从而隔离出Berry曲率贡献并可直接观测。

Conclusion: 空间均匀时间调制可在保持时间反演对称性的同时控制拓扑边缘态，并为直接观测Berry曲率及量子几何提供途径。

Abstract: Time-reversal symmetry protects helical edge states against static nonmagnetic backscattering. We show that spatially uniform temporal modulation produces a time reflection-refraction crossover within the same quantum spin Hall phase while preserving time-reversal symmetry of the instantaneous Hamiltonian. A finite gap to the bulk and an edge splitting that vanishes at the Kramers crossing allow the modulation to suppress bulk excitation while remaining nonadiabatic between the edge channels. Virtual transitions through bulk states enable coherent conversion between counter-propagating edge channels at fixed crystal momentum. Near the crossing, $qT$ governs the leading crossover dynamics for a fixed ramp shape, where $q$ is the momentum offset and $T$ the ramp duration. Coherent interference can suppress and restore reflection as the ramp slows. In the adiabatic limit, two initially time-reversed wave packets follow their respective propagation branches and acquire equal geometric displacements. Their group-velocity contributions cancel, isolating the contribution from the Berry curvature to be directly observable. Temporal modulation thus controls protected states and quantum geometry along a topological edge.

</details>


### [70] [Light-Cone Friedel Oscillations: a Window into van Hove Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40648)
*Archisman Panigrahi,Vladislav Poliakov,Leonid Levitov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出利用单杂质周围的Friedel振荡在实空间呈现的“光锥型”van Hove选择性，通过STM判断超导配对是否发生在van Hove点附近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: van Hove奇点可增强超导配对 susceptibilities，但实验上很难确定配对是否真的发生在这些鞍点附近，而非费米面其他区域。

Method: 作者分析van Hove点附近鞍点色散导致的Friedel振荡，指出其不同于常规同心圆，而形成与各van Hove点对应的实空间“光锥”扇区；STM围绕单个杂质测量这些扇区中准粒子是否打开能隙。若超导能隙在van Hove附近打开，则会抑制与能隙函数角向峰值对应的振荡方向。

Result: 该方案给出可STM探测的van Hove超导签名：每个van Hove点对应独立光锥扇区，超导能隙选择性抑制相关方向的Friedel振荡，从而可测量鞍点准粒子扇区是否及多强被能隙化。

Conclusion: 所提出的STM协议能够判定超导配对是起源于van Hove点附近还是费米面其他位置，为实验检验van Hove超导机制提供直接判据。

Abstract: Van Hove (vH) singularities can enhance superconductivity by generating a large pairing susceptibility. Yet, it remains challenging to experimentally establish whether pairing really occurs near these points. We show that the saddle-point dispersion near a vH point gives rise to a `light-cone' vH-selective behavior of Friedel oscillations, which is drastically different from the familiar concentric circles. Each vH point maps onto its own real-space light-cone sector, allowing STM around a single impurity to measure whether---and how strongly---that saddle-point quasiparticle sector is gapped. A superconducting gap near vH points suppresses the oscillations in the directions corresponding to peaks in the gap function angular dependence, a behavior that provides a signature of vH-related pairing at STM-accessible length scales. This STM protocol, targeting vH superconductivity, is capable of determining whether superconducting pairing emerges near the vH points or elsewhere on the Fermi surface.

</details>

### [71] [Large connectivity expansion of Ising Spin Glasses on random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38724)
*Luca Maria Del Bono,Flavio Nicoletti,Federico Ricci-Tersenghi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文在稀疏随机图上研究Ising自旋玻璃，通过在完全连通平均场极限附近展开腔方程，得到高连通性下的解析展开，并计算平均场有限连通系统的de Almeida-Thouless线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确腔解通常需要数值迭代求解，计算成本高；作者希望获得闭式解析近似，以更高效地刻画有限连通平均场系统的临界行为，并理解其相对完全连通平均场的修正来源。

Method: 将腔方程围绕完全连通平均场极限展开，重点处理临界行为；识别三类修正：腔场非高斯性、腔场与其涨落之间的统计相关性、腔滤波函数的修正。采用两种展开：非均匀展开同时展开三类贡献；均匀展开只展开统计量而保留腔滤波函数不变。

Result: 非均匀展开可直接与Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型比较，但因de Almeida-Thouless线在零温极限发散而在低温失效；均匀展开能较好描述系统临界行为。作者还给出T=0极限的扩展结果，并以闭式解析方程计算T-H平面中的de Almeida-Thouless线。

Conclusion: 该解析展开相较精确数值腔方法具有显著计算优势，并揭示有限连通性对平均场自旋玻璃临界行为的主要修正机制；均匀展开在低温临界行为描述上更稳健。

Abstract: We compute a high-connectivity analytical expansion for Ising spin glasses on sparse random graphs. We expand the cavity equations around the fully-connected mean-field limit, focusing on the critical behavior of the system: our approach allows us to compute the de Almeida-Thouless line of mean-field finitely-connected systems in the temperature $T$--magnetic field $H$ plane, by means of closed analytical equations. The exact cavity solution of such systems can only be obtained by solving numerically iterative equations, so our approach brings a notable computational advantage. On the theoretical side, we are able to identify three sources of corrections to the fully-connected mean-field solution: i) the non-Gaussianity of the cavity fields, ii) the statistical correlation between the cavity fields and their fluctuations, and iii) the corrections to the cavity filter functions. These corrections are treated using two different expansions: a non-uniform expansion, where we expand all three contributions around the mean-field solution, and a uniform expansion, where we only expand the statistics but leave the cavity filters untouched. The former allows for a direct comparison with the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model but fails at low temperatures, as a consequence of the de Almeida-Thouless line diverging in the zero-temperature limit. On the other hand, the uniform expansion describes with good accuracy the critical behavior of the system. We also extend these results to the $T = 0$ limit.

</details>


### [72] [Spectral theory of large data with power-law latent features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39013)
*Philipp Fleig*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文针对由数据估计的协方差矩阵呈现幂律特征值谱的现象，提出可含测量噪声的生成式潜在特征模型，并建立其谱理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经种群、蛋白质等多领域数据的协方差矩阵常表现为幂律特征值谱，需要可解释的生成模型及相应的谱理论来刻画其 bulk 谱和离群特征值。

Method: 提出生成式潜在特征模型，允许测量噪声；推导协方差矩阵 Stieltjes 变换的闭式领头阶自洽方程，得到 bulk 特征值密度，并给出自洽方程的变分原理；进一步计算孤立顶部特征值的位置与涨落。

Result: 理论在已知代数极限下得到验证，并与数值模拟结果一致；可刻画 bulk 谱及孤立顶部特征值的位置和波动。

Conclusion: 该工作为幂律协方差谱数据提供了统一的生成建模与谱分析框架，可用于含噪观测下理解 bulk 谱和顶部特征值行为。

Abstract: Power-law eigenvalue spectra of covariance matrices estimated from data appear in diverse domains ranging from neural populations to proteins. We introduce a generative latent feature model for such data, optionally corrupted by measurement noise, and develop its spectral theory. We derive closed leading-order self-consistent equations for the Stieltjes transform of the covariance matrix from which the bulk eigenvalue density is obtained and provide a variational principle for the self-consistent equations. We also compute the positions and fluctuations of isolated top eigenvalues. The theory is validated in known algebraic limits and tested against simulations.

</details>


### [73] [Tunable periodic cascade of delocalization transitions in non-Hermitian IAAF model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39514)
*Yi Xue,Yunhua Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文在非厄米插值 Aubry-André-Fibonacci（IAAF）模型中引入虚相位，实现了可调周期级联的离域转变，并给出级联周期仅由虚相位决定的解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联离域转变可用于多步调控离域，但实现可编程的周期级联仍是挑战。作者旨在构造并调控周期性级联离域转变。

Method: 在准周期势中引入虚相位，构建非厄米 IAAF 模型；通过静态局域化表征、相图中的条纹模式、解析推导级联周期，并结合静态与动力学诊断进行验证。

Result: 发现由动能促进的离域与增益-损耗调控的局域化竞争产生周期级联离域转变；相图中出现局域态与扩展态交替的规则条纹；解析得到级联周期仅依赖虚相位，可通过单一参数连续调节；静态和动力学诊断一致确认该现象。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一类具有可调级联周期的离域转变，为非厄米准周期系统中的可编程离域控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Cascade of delocalization transitions provides a route to multi-step control of delocalization, yet achieving a programmable periodic cascade remains a challenge. Here, we demonstrate a tunable periodic cascade of delocalization transitions in a non-Hermitian interpolating Aubry-Andr\'{e}-Fibonacci (IAAF) model by introducing an imaginary phase shift into the quasiperiodic potential. Such a periodic cascade of delocalization transitions arises from the competition between kinetic-energy-facilitated delocalization and gain-loss-regulated localization. In numerical static localization characterizations, successive regular stripe patterns for localized and extended states emerge in phase diagrams. We also derive an analytical expression for the cascade period and find that it relies solely on the imaginary phase shift. This provides a direct way of continuously tuning the cascade period via a single control parameter. Complementary static and dynamical diagnostics consistently confirm this periodic cascade of delocalization transitions in the non-Hermitian IAAF system. Our findings identify a novel class of delocalization transitions with tunable cascading periods, opening a route toward experimentally programmable control of delocalization.

</details>


### [74] [Microscopic Mechanism and Control of Reentrant Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39961)
*Pei-Jie Chang,Quanfeng Lu,Wentao Yang,Dong Ruan,Gui-Lu Long*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文揭示准周期二聚链中同一调制既能诱导局域化又能恢复扩展态的微观机制：Fourier通道间散射使谱边相对局域化边界移动，连续交叉驱动固定IDS标签谱包局域化后再恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期调制增强通常导致局域化，但实验/理论也发现进一步增强可恢复扩展态；同一调制如何同时驱动局域化与恢复尚缺乏微观解释。

Method: 在准周期二聚链中解析Fourier通道之间的散射过程，引入中性辅助共振确定局域化边界，并追踪具有固定积分态密度（IDS）标签的谱包与边界的连续交叉；进一步构造精确子晶格映射分析恢复窗口与阈值。

Result: 散射进入相邻通道并返回会移动谱边，使其相对局域化边界发生连续交叉，从而让同一固定IDS谱包先进入局域化再回到扩展区；该恢复在两类带隙打开家族及相对相位失谐的有限参数范围内持续存在；子晶格映射可实现恢复窗口的补偿调控，并预测各IDS标签的恢复阈值。

Conclusion: 研究给出了准周期二聚链中局域化—恢复转变的微观散射机制，并提供了恢复窗口可控及按IDS标签预测恢复阈值的理论框架。

Abstract: Increasing quasiperiodic modulation can restore extended states after localization, raising the question of how the same modulation drives both localization and recovery. We identify a microscopic mechanism in quasiperiodic dimer chains by resolving scattering between Fourier channels. Specifically, scattering into neighboring channels and back shifts the spectral edge relative to a localization boundary determined by a neutral auxiliary resonance. Their successive crossings drive the same spectral packet with fixed integrated-density-of-states (IDS) labels into localization and back into an extended regime. This recovery persists across finite parameter ranges in both gap-opening families, including relative-phase detuning. Furthermore, an exact sublattice mapping enables compensated control of the recovery window and predicts recovery thresholds for individual IDS labels.

</details>


### [75] [Dynamical mean-field theory for associative memory with block-correlated patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40060)
*Andrea Callegaris,Yoshiyuki Kabashima*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文将生成泛函动力学平均场理论（DMFT）从独立模式推广到固定有限大小的相关二值模式独立块，用于描述关联记忆中的检索动力学及个体记忆与对称混合态之间的竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存储相关模式的关联记忆中，个体记忆的检索会与对称混合态竞争；仅凭平衡态分析不足，需要动力学描述来确定初始状态如何选择不同结果。

Method: 作者把用于独立模式的生成泛函DMFT扩展到相关二值模式的独立块，得到有效单神经元过程，其自洽的交叉话务噪声协方差和自反馈系数依赖块协方差特征值；并针对具有非单调传递函数的分层相关模式进行分析，与直接模拟比较，且在不动点收敛假设下恢复广义自洽信噪分析（SCSNA）方程。

Result: DMFT复现了直接模拟中的检索轨迹和吸引域几何；增加负载可先允许从不完美初始状态检索，继续增加负载则阻止检索，形成重入区域。在该区域下边界附近，直接网络显示静止鞍点分隔共存的混合态与目标检索态；DMFT轨迹的参数敏感性可预测边界附近移动。在上边界处，检索仍可从具有较大目标重叠的初始状态进入，表明吸引域访问方式发生变化。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了相关块模式关联记忆的动力学平均场理论，揭示了重入检索、吸引域几何及鞍点结构，并在不动点条件下统一/恢复广义SCSNA。 

Abstract: In associative memories storing correlated patterns, retrieval of individual memories can compete with symmetric mixed states. A dynamical description is needed to determine how the initial state selects between these outcomes. We extend the generating-functional dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) for independent patterns to independent blocks of correlated binary patterns of fixed finite size. The resulting effective single-neuron process has a self-consistent cross-talk noise covariance and self-feedback coefficients that depend on the block-covariance eigenvalues. For hierarchically correlated patterns with nonmonotonic transfer, increasing the loading can permit retrieval from an imperfect initial state before further loading prevents it. DMFT reproduces the retrieval trajectories and basin geometry found in direct simulations. Near the lower boundary of this re-entrant region, direct networks reveal stationary saddles separating coexisting mixed and target-retrieval states. Parameter sensitivities of DMFT trajectories predict nearby shifts of this boundary. At the tested upper boundary, retrieval remains accessible from initial states with larger target overlap, indicating a change in basin access. Under fixed-point convergence assumptions, the theory recovers the equations of generalized self-consistent signal-to-noise analysis.

</details>
