*Beatrice Baraldi,Stefano Marchesin,Davide Marchesi,Lara Piemontese,Francesco Dallari,S{\o}ren S. S{\o}rensen,Eloi Pineda,Marco Di Michiel,Giulio Monaco,Peihao Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究利用快速扫描量热与原位X射线全散射联用技术，在Pd42.5Cu30Ni7.5P20金属玻璃中追踪物理老化过程，发现一级与二级老化分别对应中程和短程结构长度尺度的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理老化会驱动玻璃趋于平衡并改变其热力学与力学性质，但其微观结构演化机制仍不清楚，尤其缺乏跨宽时间与温度范围的原位结构证据。

Method: 采用低背景的快速扫描量热与原位X射线全散射联合装置，对Pd42.5Cu30Ni7.5P20金属玻璃进行物理老化测量；时间跨度达六个数量级，温度范围从玻璃转变温度以上延伸至其以下超过150 K，并结合结构因子第一、第二峰及焓弛豫分析。

Result: 结构变化主要出现在结构因子第一峰和第二峰附近。在Tg附近及以上，第一 sharp diffraction peak 的变化与焓弛豫高度一致，主要对应中程有序度增加；在更低温度，仅用该贡献无法描述焓演化，揭示出更快的额外弛豫过程，其与第二衍射峰附近变化相关，涉及包括Pd–Pd关联增加在内的短程结构重排。

Conclusion: 研究建立了热力学弛豫与微观结构演化之间的直接联系，表明一级与二级老化过程分别关联于不同的结构长度尺度。

Abstract: Physical aging drives glasses toward equilibrium and changes their thermodynamic and mechanical properties, but the accompanying microscopic structural evolution remains poorly understood. Here, using our recently developed low-background setup combining fast scanning calorimetry with \emph{in-situ} X-ray total scattering, we investigate microscopic structural changes during physical aging in Pd$_{42.5}$Cu$_{30}$Ni$_{7.5}$P$_{20}$ metallic glass. The measurements span an unprecedented six orders of magnitude in time and a temperature range extending from above the glass transition temperature to more than 150\,K below it. We observe dominant structural changes near the first and second peaks of the structure factor. Near and above the glass transition temperature, changes in the first sharp diffraction peak closely follow enthalpy relaxation and are associated predominantly with increasing degrees of medium-range ordering. At lower temperatures, the enthalpy evolution can no longer be described by this contribution alone, revealing an additional, faster relaxation process that correlates with changes near the second diffraction peak and thus short-range structural rearrangements, including increased Pd--Pd correlations. These results establish a direct connection between thermodynamic relaxation and structural evolution, providing strong evidence that primary and secondary aging processes are associated with distinct structural length scales.

</details>

### [77] [Exploring Fractional Quantum Hall States with Deep Learning: From Abelian to Non-Abelian Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38533)

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种对称适配神经网络波函数，可在包含所有朗道能级的情况下描述阿贝尔与非阿贝尔分数量子霍尔态，并用于识别竞争拓扑序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔系统具有强关联拓扑相和拓扑量子计算潜力，但其微观基态难以确定，因为竞争相常近简并且朗道能级混合难以处理。

Method: 构建对称适配的神经网络波函数，纳入全部朗道能级，并能表示阿贝尔和非阿贝尔态；通过有限尺寸重叠与纠缠谱分析候选基态。

Result: 方法准确刻画复合费米液体及低填充下的维格纳晶化；在部分填充第一朗道能级时，ν=4/3 和 7/5 的结果与 bar-2^k 1^{k+1} 型 parton 相一致，ν=3/2 则呈现类反 Pfaffian 纠缠结构。

Conclusion: 神经网络波函数为研究分数量子霍尔系统中竞争拓扑序提供了灵活且有效的统一框架。

Abstract: Fractional quantum Hall systems host strongly correlated topological phases with potential applications in topological quantum computation, yet identifying their microscopic ground states remains challenging because competing phases are often nearly degenerate and Landau-level mixing is difficult to treat. Here, we introduce a symmetry-adapted neural-network wavefunction that describes Abelian and non-Abelian states while incorporating all Landau levels. The method accurately captures composite-fermion liquids and the emergence of Wigner crystallization at low filling. When the first Landau level is partially filled, finite-size overlaps and entanglement spectra provide evidence consistent with $\bar{2}^{k}1^{k+1}$-type parton phases at $\nu=4/3$ and $7/5$, while the $\nu=3/2$ state exhibits anti-Pfaffian-like entanglement structure. These results demonstrate that neural-network wavefunctions offer a flexible framework for studying competing topological orders in fractional quantum Hall systems.

</details>


### [78] [ML-COHP: predicting bond descriptors using latent space of a universal interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40445)
*Daniil S. Alekseev,Ilya V. Chepkasov,Alexander G. Kvashnin*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该工作用MACE基础模型的冻结描述符快速预测ICOHP键强度，避免每个结构都做DFT单点加LOBSTER投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICOHP是DFT中衡量键强的标准量，但每个结构都需单独DFT单点和LOBSTER投影，百原子级晶界或催化纳米颗粒耗时数天，难以大规模或连续构型扫描。

Method: 从MACE基础模型提取冻结描述符，设计对称pair head读取两原子的标量通道和键投影矢量通道，并用同一模型元素嵌入构建pair基线进行校正；在高熵二硼化物、缺陷石墨烯上Pt团簇及吸附O2的Pt/Pd纳米颗粒上测试。

Result: 对强于0.5 eV的键，误差为1.6–4.1%，约为同数据训练的高斯过程模型的一半；仅用高温HEB2快照训练即可将弛豫晶界胞的LOBSTER ICOHP复现到0.06 eV，耗时约1秒而非5–51小时；矢量通道使误差相对标量通道减半，基线使模型仅需两个训练结构即可使用；结合微调MACE势几何，可连续覆盖O2覆盖度并揭示O2使Pt-rich壳层金属-金属键损失数倍于Pd壳层。

Conclusion: 基于MACE描述符的ICOHP预测器能以近秒级成本获得接近LOBSTER精度的键强信息，适用于复杂缺陷、纳米颗粒和连续吸附构型的高通量键合分析。

Abstract: The integrated crystal orbital Hamilton population (ICOHP) is the standard measure of bond strength in a DFT calculation, but every structure needs its own DFT single point and LOBSTER projection, which takes days for a cell of a hundred atoms, such as a grain boundary or a catalytic nanoparticle. We predict ICOHP from the frozen descriptors of a MACE foundation model. A symmetric pair head reads the scalar and bond-projected vector channels of the two atoms and corrects a pair baseline built from the element embeddings of the same model. We tested the model on the high-entropy diboride (Hf,Zr,Ti,Nb,Ta)B$_2$, Pt clusters on defective graphene and Pt/Pd nanoparticles with adsorbed O$_2$. The error on bonds stronger than 0.5 eV is 1.6-4.1%, about half that of the Gaussian-process model of Ferreira et al. trained on the same data. Trained only on high-temperature HEB$_2$ snapshots, the model reproduces the LOBSTER ICOHP of relaxed grain boundary cells to 0.06 eV in about a second, against 5-51 hours of LOBSTER. The vector channels halve the error compared to the scalar ones, and the baseline keeps the model usable with two training structures. With geometries from a fine-tuned MACE potential, the model gives the bonding of Pt/Pd particles over a continuous O$_2$ coverage, and DFT on these particles shows that O$_2$ costs a Pt-rich shell several times more metal--metal bonding than a Pd shell.

</details>


### [79] [Dual Area-Volume-of-Fluid (DAVOF): co-advecting volume and face-area fractions for a closed-form, cell-local interface normal and position on unstructured meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38863)
*Tomislav Mari\'c*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出在非结构多面体网格上，将与单元体积分数一致的“面面积润湿分数”进行输运，并利用单元自身面的面积/体积分数闭式恢复界面法向与位置，无需邻域模板或迭代；结合平面裁剪与环法向稳定VOF输运，在球/椭球上验证精度、曲率收敛与守恒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统几何VOF依赖邻域模板的体积分数重构界面方向，在非规则单元和存在wisps时退化，且由其构造的曲率会驱动毛细流的寄生电流，因此需要更鲁棒、精确且守恒的几何VOF重构与输运方法。

Method: 一致输运每个单元的体积分数及每个面面积的润湿分数；由单元自身面的面积分数求和并应用液体子体积上的散度定理得到界面法向，再由面积与体积分数闭式确定平面位置；对六面体、扰动及多面体单元上的平面精确。用一环恢复法向拟合二次曲面获得曲率；采用平面裁剪与单元环并集上的同一恒等式构造环法向以稳定输运；初步输运实现为每单元一个抛物面，其坐标系来自法向、曲率张量来自环、位置来自体积分数。

Result: 球与椭球测试中，法向达到面分数的阶，位置对所有状态和网格均二阶；通过一环法向的二次曲面得到收敛曲率，而参考模型无曲率。平面裁剪加环法向使状态稳定，球跨越3.3个直径保持有界守恒，按裁剪律预测为面分数一阶，位置与形状误差达到plicRDF水平。初步抛物面输运在N=32至128的分辨率阶梯上保持球有界守恒，每周期面分数阶数约为2.8（精确法向）和2.1（环二次曲面法向）；闭环阶数取决于输入法向的阶。

Conclusion: 该工作为几何VOF在非结构多面体网格上的进一步发展奠定基础；当前输运闭环受输入法向阶数限制，获得二阶输运法向是研究目标，而闭式面分数重构、环法向稳定与守恒输运展现出良好潜力。

Abstract: Geometric Volume-of-Fluid (VOF) on unstructured polyhedral meshes reconstructs the interface orientation from the volume fractions of a stencil of neighbours; on irregular cells and in the presence of wisps it degrades, and the curvature built on it drives the parasitic currents of capillary flows. We transport, consistently to the volume fraction of every cell, the wetted fraction of every face area. From the area and volume fractions, closed-form equations recover the interface normal and position of a cell, for the first time in VOF: the normal from one sum over the cell's own faces, the divergence theorem on the liquid sub-volume, and the plane's position from a second, without neighbours or iteration. Both identities are exact for a plane on hexahedral, perturbed and polyhedral cells. On a sphere and an ellipsoid the normal has the order of the face fractions, the position is second order for every state and mesh, and a quadric through one ring of recovered normals gives a converging curvature where the reference models show none. Transported with the plane clipping, the ring normal, the same identity on the union of the cell's ring, stabilises the state and carries a sphere over 3.3 diameters bounded and conservative, first order in the face fractions as the clipping law predicts, at plicRDF's position and shape errors. A preliminary transport implementation with one paraboloid per cell, its frame from the normal, its curvature tensor from the ring and its position from the volume fraction, carries the sphere bounded and conservative over the resolution ladder with face fractions of order 2.8 per period with the exact normal and 2.1 with the ring quadric's normal over N = 32 to 128. The loop has the order of the normal it is fed; a transported normal of second order is the research goal. This document is the basis on which the further development of the method is documented.

</details>


### [80] [PhysicsNeMo-Mesh: GPU-Native Meshes and Simulation Cases for Physics AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39072)
*Peter D. Sharpe,Corey Adams,Negin Sobhani,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: PhysicsNeMo-Mesh 是 PhysicsNeMo 的 GPU 批处理网格模块，在 PyTorch 自动微分内提供类 PyVista 的网格处理与类 xarray 的语义标注，实现面向使用点的快速预处理，并保留 PDE/边界条件等语义信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理仿真机器学习面临三个问题：样本大导致 I/O 限制 GPU 利用率；传统预处理昂贵，只能一次性在数据整理阶段完成，难以按模型和工作流在使用点定制；等变与网格原生架构需要通常被预处理丢弃的信息。

Method: 该模块在 GPU 上批量执行网格操作，并置于 PyTorch 自动微分中；为点、单元和整体网格上的字段附加名称与形状，仿真案例保存完整 PDE 问题规格，包括边界条件及其指定值；通过快速 GPU 网格处理和案例加载支持在使用点按需预处理，并保留字段秩、命名边界等语义结构。

Result: 在单张 GB200 GPU 上，网格操作比 72 线程 VTK 快 6.2–1,068 倍；加载完整案例比读取其 VTK 文件快 8.0–88 倍（读取相同字段时为 8.0 倍）；与训练重叠时，对 127 ms 的 Transolver 步骤仅损失 2.1% 吞吐。公开 recipe 中一个 DrivAerML 数据集可同时供给 Transolver、GeoTransolver 和 GLOBE；当形状与壁面条件均变化时，逐边界条件将热传导误差从 87% 降至 6–9%。

Conclusion: PhysicsNeMo-Mesh 兼顾中间数组存储的速度与 VTK 等归档格式的灵活性，使一份数据集服务多种模型和工作流，并保留下一代架构所需的语义结构。

Abstract: PhysicsNeMo-Mesh is the mesh module of the open-source PhysicsNeMo library. It provides mesh processing similar to PyVista, but on the GPU, in batches, and inside PyTorch's automatic differentiation. Like xarray for unstructured meshes, it attaches semantics to data: fields on points, cells, and whole meshes have names and shapes, and a simulation case keeps the complete PDE problem specification, including boundary conditions and their prescribed values. It addresses three problems that plague machine learning for physical simulation. First, GPU utilization is bottlenecked by I/O, because samples are very large relative to the compute per forward pass. Second, because traditional preprocessing is expensive, it is necessarily done once, during curation, rather than differently for each model and workflow at the point of use. Finally, advanced equivariant and mesh-native architectures require information that curation ordinarily discards. Fast GPU mesh processing makes it practical to preprocess at the point of use: on one GB200 GPU, mesh operations run $6.2\text{--}1{,}068\times$ faster than VTK with 72 threads, and loading a whole case is $8.0\text{--}88\times$ faster than reading its VTK files ($8.0\times$ when both read the same fields). Overlapped with training, this preprocessing costs $2.1\%$ of throughput for a $127\,\text{ms}$ Transolver step. So one dataset serves many models and workflows: in PhysicsNeMo's public recipe, one stored DrivAerML dataset feeds Transolver, GeoTransolver, and GLOBE. PhysicsNeMo-Mesh combines the speed of an intermediate array store with the flexibility of an archival format such as VTK. When shapes and wall conditions both vary, per-boundary conditions cut a heat-conduction error from $87\%$ to $6\text{--}9\%$. And by keeping field ranks and named boundaries, it retains the semantic structure that next-generation architectures will need.

</details>


### [81] [A Gaussian-Process Surrogate for Control Rod Worth with Calibrated Uncertainty: Application to VERA Core Physics Benchmark Problem 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40389)
*Sean Gohlke,Jilang Miao*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文用高斯过程代理反应性随控制棒位置的变化，从核函数导数闭式得到带不确定度的微分棒价值，并在VERA基准题4上验证其精度与区间校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微分/积分控制棒价值通过对离散棒位的特征值计算作差获得，对导数的分辨差且不给出不确定度；需要一种能利用蒙特卡罗计算不确定度、可解析求导并给出校准不确定区间的替代方法。

Method: 在OpenMC与ENDF/B-VIII.0下建模VERA Core Physics Benchmark Problem 4（3×3组件簇，AIC/B4C混合棒束控制组件）。以参数化S型均值函数描述反应性动态范围，以Matérn-5/2高斯过程拟合残差，并将每次计算的蒙特卡罗不确定度作为已知观测噪声。因微分是线性算子，微分棒价值及其方差可由核函数导数闭式给出。用43次训练运行（三种粒子数）和11次独立测试运行评估，并对129次独立运行的标准化留一残差进行校准检验。

Result: 代理模型在高于测试运行噪声的水平上满足固定10 pcm精度目标；129次独立运行的标准化留一残差方差为1.07，95%区间为[0.85,1.39]，表明区间校准良好。总棒价值与OpenMC直接端点差分在合成不确定度内一致，微分棒价值峰值定位误差不超过一个步长。三种拟合质量差异很大的均值函数给出的微分棒价值在0.1 pcm/step内一致，但其不确定带宽度随核平滑度变化可达4倍。

Conclusion: 高斯过程代理可给出精度受控、不确定度校准的积分与微分控制棒价值，避免离散差分对导数分辨不足的问题；微分价值点估计对均值函数选择不敏感，而不确定带宽度主要由核平滑度决定。文中报告了所需运行次数与剩余近似，并概述了向棒位与可溶硼二维扩展的方向。

Abstract: Differential and integral control rod worth are usually obtained by differencing eigenvalue calculations at discrete rod positions, which resolves the derivative poorly and gives it no uncertainty. We build a Gaussian-process (GP) surrogate for reactivity as a function of rod position on VERA Core Physics Benchmark Problem 4, a $3\times3$ assembly cluster with a hybrid AIC/B$_4$C rod cluster control assembly, modelled in OpenMC with ENDF/B-VIII.0. A parametric sigmoidal mean carries the dynamic range, a Matern-5/2 GP carries the residual, and the reported Monte Carlo uncertainty of each run enters as known observation noise. Because differentiation is linear, the differential rod worth and its variance follow in closed form from the kernel derivatives. Trained on 43 runs at three particle counts and tested on 11 independent runs, the surrogate meets a fixed 10 pcm accuracy target above the test-run noise, and its intervals are calibrated: the standardized leave-one-out residuals over 129 independent runs have variance 1.07 with 95% interval [0.85, 1.39]. The total worth agrees with the direct OpenMC end-point difference within combined uncertainty, and the differential worth peak is located to within one step. Three mean functions of very different fit quality give the same differential worth to within 0.1 pcm/step, while the width of its band depends on the kernel smoothness by a factor of four. The number of runs required and the remaining approximations are reported, and the extension to rod position and soluble boron is outlined.

</details>


### [82] [Predicting Weight Windows for Duct-Streaming Shielding Geometries with a Convolutional Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40457)
*Jilang Miao,Ansel Kneier,Jonathan Porrazzo*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 用 1.9M 参数 U-Net 从二维导管几何与源位置毫秒级预测 OpenMC/MAGIC 前向通量权重窗下界，并在未重训练的矩形 Protvino 迷宫中取得与 MAGIC 0.96 的相关性和 54 倍模拟 FOM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于前向通量的权重窗可对导管流屏蔽问题实现全局方差缩减，但每换一个几何都需迭代蒙特卡罗计算来构造权重窗，成本高；作者探索用 CNN 学习该映射以快速生成权重窗。

Method: 在 Protvino 迷宫包围盒上构造 250 个随机混凝土导管，含三类弯曲与矩形族，使用 Cf-252 点源在 OpenMC 中运行 MAGIC 生成下界；训练一个 1.9M 参数 U-Net，输入为二维导管几何与源位置，输出为下界的对数值。

Result: 在留出导管上，携通量单元误差为 0.14–0.51 decades；重复 MAGIC 运行在该区域的可复现性为百分之几 decades，故残差主要为模型误差，而低于 10^-6 的参考值本身为噪声。未经重训练应用于矩形 Protvino 迷宫时，预测与 MAGIC 相关 0.96；将其作为权重窗并在部署时钳制最深单元，出口探测器 FOM 为 analog 的 54 倍、同几何 MAGIC 生成运行的 1.7 倍、复用 MAGIC 窗的 0.80 倍（后者构造成本未计）。任意权重窗集的运行成本由最深单元决定，并可由 population proxy 预测。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明 CNN 可作为快速权重窗下界代理模型，实现跨几何迁移并显著优于 analog，接近复用 MAGIC 窗；部署时需处理最深单元钳制，且运行成本可由最深单元/population proxy 预判。

Abstract: Forward-flux-based weight windows give global variance reduction for duct-streaming shielding problems, but each new geometry needs an iterative Monte Carlo calculation to build them. We report preliminary results on a convolutional neural network (CNN) that learns the map from a two-dimensional duct geometry and source position to the lower window bound of such an iteration, here the MAGIC generator in OpenMC, in milliseconds. A set of 250 random concrete ducts of three curved and rectangular families on the Protvino labyrinth bounding box was run in OpenMC with a Cf-252 point source and MAGIC generation, and a 1.9-million-parameter U-Net was trained on the logarithm of the lower bound. On held-out ducts the error in the cells that carry the flux is 0.14 to 0.51 decades; replicate MAGIC runs show this part of the map to be reproducible to a few hundredths of a decade, so the residual is model error, while below $10^{-6}$ the reference itself is noise. Applied without retraining to the rectangular Protvino labyrinth, the prediction correlates with MAGIC at 0.96. Used as weight windows there, with its deepest cells clamped at deployment, it gives an exit-detector figure of merit 54 times the analog value, 1.7 times that of the MAGIC generation run for the same geometry, and 0.80 of that of reused MAGIC windows whose construction it does not pay for. The run cost of any window set is shown to be set by its deepest cells and predictable from a population proxy.

</details>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [Ordered patches cap the stability of disordered frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38501)
*Joohan Nam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结构无序可提高框架抗压稳定性，但其允许的局部有序会通过可局部化失稳给稳定性增益设定上限；除非屏蔽应力，无序不能防止破缺晶体对称性的失效，对立方晶体至多防止体积坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构无序能稳定框架、抵抗压力诱发的失效，但材料中必然存在的有序区域会限制这一增益；需要明确无序稳定性的上限及其失效机制。

Method: 研究静水压下的刚性节点框架，考虑包含任意大、正密度晶态斑块的系综，并给斑块中心施加缩放的晶态预压力；将声学稳定性判据推广到预压力情形，对所有点群进行分析；并用二维 Truchet 框架和 876 个细杆网络进行验证。

Result: 当晶态斑块所受应力超过晶体的可局部化失稳阈值时，系综不能保持线性稳定；若所有 Bloch 模（含声学极限）稳定，则正定性的均匀丧失不能局部化，从而留下稳定窗口。该窗口要求采用保持晶体对称性的应变；否则无序不能防止对称破缺失效，立方晶体至多只能防止体积坍缩。二维 Truchet 框架中植入有序域的阈值距理论上界 1% 内，短于长程有序的关联会使失效局域在有序团簇上；876 个细杆网络中 798 个首先发生可局部化失稳，其余 78 个的中位稳定窗口为 7.6%。

Conclusion: 通过无序获得的稳定性受其允许的局部有序的可局部化失稳所限制。

Abstract: Structural disorder can stabilize frameworks against pressure-induced failure, yet the ordered regions it contains limit this gain. We show that, in rigid-jointed frames under hydrostatic pressure, an ensemble containing arbitrarily large patches of a crystal at positive density, whose centres carry a scaled crystal prestress, cannot stay linearly stable once those patches are stressed beyond the crystal's threshold for localizable instabilities. A homogeneous loss of positive definiteness while all Bloch modes, including the acoustic limit, remain stable cannot localize, leaving a window for stabilization. Extending an acoustic criterion to prestress, we show for all point groups that such a window requires a strain that keeps the crystal's symmetry. Unless the patches are shielded from stress, disorder cannot protect a crystal against a failure that breaks its symmetry, and protects a cubic crystal at most against volume collapse. In two-dimensional Truchet frames, planted ordered domains bring the threshold within 1% of the bound, and correlations short of long-range order localize failure on ordered clusters. Across 876 slender-strut nets, 798 fail first by a localizable instability; the other 78 have a median window of 7.6%. Stability gained through disorder is capped by the localizable instabilities of the local order it permits.

</details>


### [84] [MoireKP: An Automated Workflow for Symmetry-Constrained Moir\'e Continuum Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37760)
*Yan Zhang,Hanqi Pi,Wangqian Miao,Zhong Fang,Hongming Weng,Xi Dai,B. Andrei Bernevig,Quansheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 MoireKP 自动化工作流，从局域轨道第一性原理哈密顿量构建紧凑连续模型，用于莫尔材料低能电子结构计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理哈密顿量虽包含研究莫尔材料所需的电子信息，但其尺寸过大导致后续计算成本高昂，因此需要构建紧凑且保留关键物理的低能模型。

Method: MoireKP 构建谷分辨的截断原子平面波（TAPW）哈密顿量，选择低能基，并将晶体对称操作投影到该基上；由对称性确定允许的哈密顿量项，其系数通过将低能有效哈密顿量投影到相应算符基获得。模型维度、局域参考态和諧波截断可自动选择或由用户指定，且该方法保留所选低能子空间中的波函数信息。

Result: 将该工作流应用于 AA 堆叠扭转双层 MoTe2、AB 堆叠扭转双层 ZrS2 和 A-AB 扭转三层 MoTe2，覆盖 K、M 和 Γ 谷；所有模型沿高对称 k 路径在目标窗口内均以 3.0 meV 以内的误差重现 TAPW 能带能量。

Conclusion: MoireKP 提供了从第一性原理哈密顿量到紧凑对称连续模型的自动化构建流程，在保持目标低能子空间精度的同时降低计算成本，适用于多谷和多层莫尔体系。

Abstract: First-principles Hamiltonians contain the electronic information needed to study moir\'e materials, but their size makes subsequent calculations costly. We present MoireKP, an automated workflow for constructing compact continuum models from localized-orbital ab initio Hamiltonians. The workflow constructs a valley-resolved truncated atomic-plane-wave (TAPW) Hamiltonian, selects a low-energy basis, and projects the crystal-symmetry operations into this basis. These representations determine the symmetry-allowed Hamiltonian terms, whose coefficients are obtained by projecting the low-energy effective Hamiltonian onto the corresponding operator basis. The model dimension, localized reference states, and harmonic cutoff can be selected automatically or specified by the user. This Hamiltonian-based construction retains wave-function information within the selected low-energy subspace. We apply the workflow to AA-stacked twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$, AB-stacked twisted bilayer ZrS$_2$, and A--AB twisted trilayer MoTe$_2$, covering K, M, and $\Gamma$ valleys. All models reproduce TAPW band energies within 3.0 meV in the target windows along high-symmetry $k$ paths.

</details>


### [85] [Electronic-morphological decoupling in mixed-dimensional halide perovskite heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37964)
*Mahmoud Samadpour,Alireza Fathi Beiraghvandi,Kazem Meraji,Tristan Sachsenweger Ballantyne,Fuxiang Ji,Mahdi Mohammadi,Nasim Kabir,Wolfgang Tress*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究表明，在混合维度钙钛矿异质结构中，表面形貌变粗糙并不必然导致电子学异质性增加；低维Ruddlesden-Popper覆盖层可在增加表面粗糙度的同时保持甚至改善光电均匀性，并抑制老化诱导的 disorder。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为薄膜表面越光滑，光电性质越均匀；作者希望检验表面形貌演化是否必然导致混合维度钙钛矿中电子学不均匀性增加，并探索低维覆盖层对3D钙钛矿微观光电性质空间分布的影响。

Method: 采用空间位阻的5-tert-butyl-phenanthridinium阳离子制备低维Ruddlesden-Popper覆盖层；结合统计AFM分析、共聚焦空间分辨PL光谱和准费米能级分裂（QFLS） mapping，将表面形貌与微区光电性质关联，并进行加速老化对比。

Result: 覆盖层虽增加表面粗糙度和高度不对称性，但未增加电子学不均匀性；相比原始3D薄膜，异质结构表现出增强的PL、降低的非辐射复合以及更均匀的空间辐射QFLS。加速老化下差异更明显：原始薄膜出现显著的发射和QFLS空间波动，而覆盖异质结构基本保持光电均匀性。

Conclusion: 低维覆盖层可抑制老化诱导的光电 disorder，实现形貌不规则与电子学均匀性的解耦；该机制为设计耐久、高性能钙钛矿光伏器件提供了原则。

Abstract: Surface morphology is widely considered an indicator of thin-film quality, with smoother films generally expected to exhibit more uniform optoelectronic properties. Here, we demonstrate that substantial surface evolution does not necessarily lead to increased electronic heterogeneity in mixed-dimensional perovskite heterostructures. We investigate how low-dimensional Ruddlesden-Popper capping layers derived from the sterically hindered 5-tert-butyl-phenanthridinium cation affect the spatial distributions of photoluminescence (PL) characteristics and quasi-Fermi level splitting (QFLS) in 3D perovskites. By combining statistical atomic force microscopy (AFM) analysis with confocal, spatially resolved PL spectroscopy, we correlate surface topography with microscale optoelectronic properties.
  Although capping increases surface roughness and height asymmetry, it does not increase electronic inhomogeneity. Instead, the heterostructures exhibit enhanced PL, reduced non-radiative recombination, and improved spatial uniformity of radiative QFLS compared with pristine 3D films. Under accelerated aging, this contrast becomes more pronounced: pristine films develop substantial spatial fluctuations in emission and QFLS, whereas capped heterostructures largely preserve their optoelectronic homogeneity. These findings indicate that the low-dimensional capping layer suppresses aging-induced optoelectronic disorder, demonstrating that electronic uniformity can be maintained despite morphological irregularity. This decoupling provides a design principle for developing durable, high-performance perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [86] [Two-Dimensional Magnetic Materials from Ferromagnetism to Altermagnetism: Fabrication, Symmetry, Evidence, and Device Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38491)
*Guojian Zhao,Ziang Meng,Li Liu,Zhiyuan Duan,Jingyu Li,Sixu Jiang,Xiaoyang Tan,Shiheng Bu,Chen Liang,Xiaoning Wang,Peixin Qin,Zhiqi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述以净磁化和动量空间自旋劈裂/自旋极化模式为主坐标，辅以子晶格结构与相反自旋子晶格间对称性，系统梳理二维磁性；并提出M1–M4证据分级，独立报告证据类型与有序温度来源，同时定性比较器件成熟度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维磁体受组分、厚度、堆垛、应变、缺陷、衬底、铁性序和界面等多因素调控，亟需统一分类框架与证据标准，以澄清相归属并评估器件化潜力。

Method: 综述按净磁化与动量空间自旋劈裂/极化模式组织二维磁性，并结合子晶格结构及相反自旋子晶格间对称关系；引入M1–M4报告类别区分层分辨原子级薄层、非层分辨薄片/薄膜、块体基准和理论候选/设计路线；证据类别与有序温度来源分别报告；用工作温度、读出、翻转、畴控制、稳定性、可扩展性、界面敏感性和集成难度定性比较器件就绪度。

Result: 铁磁体通常具有净磁矩；亚铁磁体可实现完全补偿；常规反铁磁体可保持自旋简并能带；交错磁体在维度相关对称约束下允许非相对论性交替自旋劈裂。当前交错磁性实验证据主要来自M2薄膜和M3块体基准，完全验证的原子级薄M1交错磁相仍是开放实验目标；多数单层/少层提议属于M4，争议案例明确了相归属所需测量。

Conclusion: 可靠的相归属和器件评估要求对同一材料形态在匹配条件下开展互补测量，最好在同一样品上或原位完成；原子级薄交错磁体仍是关键实验目标。

Abstract: Atomically thin magnets are governed by composition, thickness, stacking, strain, defects, substrates, ferroic order, and interfaces. This Review organizes two-dimensional (2D) magnetism using net magnetization and momentum-space spin splitting or spin-polarization patterns as primary coordinates, supplemented by sublattice structure and the symmetry relations between opposite-spin sublattices. Ferromagnets generally carry a net moment; ferrimagnets can be fully compensated. Conventional antiferromagnets may retain spin-degenerate bands, while altermagnets permit nonrelativistic alternating spin splitting under dimension-specific symmetry constraints. M1-M4 reporting classes introduced here distinguish layer-resolved atomically thin forms, non-layer-resolved thin flakes or films, bulk benchmarks, and theoretical candidates or design routes; the evidence category and ordering-temperature provenance are reported independently. Current experimental support for altermagnetism comes mainly from M2 thin-film and M3 bulk benchmarks, leaving a fully verified atomically thin M1 altermagnetic phase as an open experimental goal. Most proposed monolayer and few-layer systems therefore fall within M4, and contested cases clarify the measurements needed for phase assignment. Device readiness is compared qualitatively using operating temperature, readout, switching, domain control, stability, scalability, interface sensitivity, and integration difficulty. Reliable phase assignment and device assessment require complementary measurements from the same material form under matched conditions, ideally on the same specimen or in situ.

</details>


### [87] [Multiscale evaluation of the elastic response of polycrystalline NiAl using molecular statics, EBSD-based finite element and analytical homogenization methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38783)
*Szymon Nosewicz,Marcin Ma\'zdziarz,Pawe{\l} Ho{\l}obut,Witold Chromi\'nski,Katarzyna Kowalczyk-Gajewska,Grzegorz Jurczak*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作建立了一个从分子动力学到有限元/解析均匀化的多尺度框架，用于预测NiAl微观尺度弹性常数，并与实验和经典解析估计进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在微观尺度准确确定NiAl的弹性常数，并考虑晶粒各向异性和形貌对多晶材料有效弹性行为的影响，以连接原子尺度结果与宏观实验。

Method: 采用分子动力学计算单晶NiAl立方Pm3m结构的刚度张量；利用EBSD表征微观结构并重建保留晶粒取向和尺寸分布的三维多晶几何模型；通过有限元单轴拉伸模拟和解析均匀化方法计算多晶NiAl的有效杨氏模量E和泊松比ν，并与实验值及经典解析估计比较。

Result: 有限元预测的E和ν与实验值高度一致；在经典解析方法中，Hashin-Shtrikman上界和自洽近似对实验杨氏模量的复现偏差较小。

Conclusion: 该多尺度数值-解析框架能可靠预测NiAl多晶弹性行为，真实微观结构建模对结果验证重要，且部分经典解析估计可作为有效弹性常数的合理近似。

Abstract: The presented work proposes a multiscale framework for the numerical and analytical determination of the elastic constants of the intermetallic nickel aluminide (NiAl) at the microscopic scale. Molecular dynamics (MD) calculations have been employed to evaluate the stiffness tensors of a single NiAl monocrystalline body with cubic Pm$\bar{3}$m symmetry. The atomistic results were transferred to the upper scale as an input to micromechanical models. Here, finite element modeling (FEM) and analytical homogenization methods were used to simulate the elastic behavior of polycrystalline NiAl, with special attention to grain anisotropy and its morphology. The microstructural features of the heterogeneous body were evaluated experimentally using electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) and subsequently used to generate a 3D polycrystalline geometric model that preserves realistic distributions of grain orientations and grain sizes. The reconstructed NiAl polycrystal was subjected to uniaxial tensile test simulations using FEM to evaluate the effective Young's modulus ($E$) and Poisson's ratio ($\nu$). The numerical results were confronted with the experimental values of $E$ and~$\nu$. With these model assumptions, a very good agreement between numerical and experimental results was achieved. Finally, the experimental results were compared with classical analytical estimates. Among the analytical approaches, the Hashin-Shtrikman upper bound and self-consistent approximation reproduced the experimental Young's modulus with minor deviations.

</details>


### [88] [Collective sliding relaxor ferroelectricity across thousand-layer van der Waals superstructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38928)
*Nianze Shang,Cuiju Yu,Yucai Lin,Juan C. Arias-Mu\~noz,Jiajie Qi,Quanlin Guo,MD Gius Uddin,Youqiang Huang,Tuomas Pajunp\"a\"a,Christopher P. Edwards,Rodolfo E. Canet-Albiach,Faisal Ahmed,Andreas C. Liapis,Yaoqiang Zhou,Zhibin Zhang,Hao Hong,Qing Dai,Andrea Al\`u,Jos\'e L. Lado,Kaihui Liu,Zhipei Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在菱方氮化硼中通过堆垛相工程实现滑移弛豫铁电性（S-RFE）：由层间滑移驱动的滑移畴作为极性纳米区的结构类比物，在电场下协同取向，产生巨大且低滞后的电致伸缩响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弛豫铁电体通常依赖化学复杂性与结构无序，局部成分涨落形成极性纳米区，使其微观起源难以控制和工程化。作者希望在不依赖化学无序的情况下实现可设计的弛豫行为。

Method: 在菱方氮化硼中进行堆垛相工程，引入受控的堆垛相异质性，形成滑移畴；这些畴在电场下通过协同层间滑移集体取向，从而产生滑移弛豫铁电态，并测量其电致伸缩与温度稳定性。

Result: 约300 nm厚（约1000个原子层）晶体中实现最高约-1.6%的可逆电致伸缩形变，滞后可忽略；电致伸缩系数Q33约1200 m^4 C^-2，比多数晶态电致伸缩材料高数个量级；该响应在低至1.6 K仍保持活跃且具巨大机电功能。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了滑移弛豫铁电性作为实现弛豫行为的新路径，揭示原子尺度层间运动可放大为跨约千层的集体介观功能，并为基于范德华材料的机电、光子和量子技术提供材料按需设计平台。

Abstract: Relaxor ferroelectrics are among the most important electromechanical materials owing to their exceptionally large electrostrictive responses. However, conventional relaxor behavior is generally associated with chemical complexity and structural disorder, where local compositional fluctuations generate polar nanoregions, making the microscopic origin of this behavior difficult to control and engineer. Here we report sliding relaxor ferroelectricity (S-RFE), a previously unknown relaxor state in which engineered stacking-phase heterogeneity enables cooperative interlayer sliding to generate collective relaxor behavior. Through stacking-phase engineering in rhombohedral boron nitride, we create sliding domains that act as structural analogues of polar nanoregions and cooperatively align under an electric field through interlayer sliding. This collective state produces reversible electromechanical deformation of up to approximately $-1.6\%$ with negligible hysteresis in approximately 300-nm-thick crystals, corresponding to approximately 1000 atomic layers. The resulting electrostriction coefficient ($Q_{33}$ of approximately $1200\,\mathrm{m}^{4}\mathrm{C}^{-2}$) exceeds those of most crystalline electrostrictors by orders of magnitude. Unlike conventional relaxors, whose electromechanical response is often suppressed by dipolar freezing at low temperatures, S-RFE remains active down to 1.6 K and maintains giant electromechanical functionality. Our results establish sliding relaxor ferroelectricity as a new route to relaxor behavior and reveal how atomic-scale interlayer motion can be amplified into collective mesoscale functionality across approximately one thousand layers, providing a materials-by-design platform for electromechanical, photonic, and quantum technologies based on van der Waals materials.

</details>


### [89] [Spin-Polarized Weyl Fermions and Unconventional Spin Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39604)
*Chenhao Liang,Haohao Sheng,Sheng Zhang,Zhong Fang,Hongming Weng,Zhijun Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出二维非磁体系中临界自旋极化Weyl费米子的通用理论框架，并预言α-Bi型V族褶皱层二维铁电材料（以BiAs单层为代表）可同时实现巨自旋劈裂、临界自旋Weyl费米子及电可切换的非线性与自旋输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非磁材料中实现大且电可控的自旋劈裂，对强自旋极化准粒子、非常规自旋输运及其电调控至关重要，但缺乏通用理论框架和理想材料平台。

Method: 建立二维非磁系统中临界自旋Weyl费米子的通用框架，并结合计算系统研究α-Bi型V族褶皱层二维铁电材料家族；通过组分、应变和磁场调控系统展示性质可调性。

Result: BiAs单层中自旋轨道耦合诱导交换尺度自旋劈裂并产生p波自旋织构；存在四个临界自旋Weyl点且各自位于单一自旋通道，低能极限下涌现的Sz守恒对称性保护近量子化的⟨Sz⟩；产生纯面外Edelstein响应和显著自旋Nernst效应等非常规自旋输运；铁电反转可电切换p波自旋织构和反演奇响应。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了非磁体系中临界自旋Weyl物理的通用框架，并提供可探索临界自旋Weyl物理、非线性Hall现象和非常规自旋输运的候选材料平台。

Abstract: Large and electrically controllable spin splitting in nonmagnetic materials is highly desirable for condensed-matter physics and spintronics, enabling strongly spin-polarized quasiparticles and unconventional spin transport as well as their electrical control. Here, we establish a general framework for critical spin-polarized Weyl fermions in two-dimensional (2D) nonmagnetic systems. We predict the $\alpha$-Bi-like Group-V puckered-layer family of 2D ferroelectrics as a material platform combining critical spin-Weyl fermions, giant spin splitting, and electrically switchable nonlinear and spin transport. Our calculations reveal exchange-scale spin splitting induced by spin-orbit coupling and a $p$-wave spin texture in the BiAs monolayer. Moreover, BiAs hosts four critical spin-Weyl points, each formed within a single-spin channel. The nearly quantized $\langle S_z\rangle$ is protected by an emergent $S_z$-conservation symmetry in the low-energy limit. The spin-Weyl cones also give rise to unconventional spin-transport phenomena, including a purely out-of-plane Edelstein response and a pronounced spin Nernst effect. Ferroelectric reversal further enables electrical switching of the $p$-wave spin texture and inversion-odd responses, while the tunability of these properties through composition, strain, and magnetic field is also systematically demonstrated across the entire family. These results establish both a general nonmagnetic framework and a promising material platform for exploring critical spin-Weyl physics, nonlinear Hall phenomena, and unconventional spin transport.

</details>


### [90] [Exceptional Superconducting Interfaces between Graphene and Atomic Chains in A15-MoRe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39694)
*Hiroto Ogura,Yuto Tsukidate,Shun Kondo,Zhuoqun Li,Natsuki Sasaki,Tsunenobu Sato,Azusa Utsumi,Tomohiro Otsuka,Mitsuhiro Saito,Kazutoshi Inoue,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Yuichi Ikuhara,Toshiaki Kato*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维量子器件亟需高透明且耐磁场的超导接触。MoRe对石墨烯可形成弹道、高透明且高磁场下仍存活的接触，但其异常透明性的微观机制长期未解。

Method: 结合低温输运测量、X射线衍射和原子分辨率电子显微术，表征MoRe/石墨烯界面结构与超导接触性能；并用第一性原理计算分析A15相特有的一维过渡金属原子链与石墨烯的晶格匹配及电子杂化。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了MoRe/石墨烯最佳超导接触的结构与电子学起源，并将A15相超导体确立为面向二维材料 robust 量子电子器的几何理想、通用平台。

Abstract: Realizing highly transparent and magnetic-field-resilient superconducting contacts is a critical bottleneck for the advancement of two-dimensional (2D) quantum devices. Molybdenum-rhenium (MoRe) alloys have been empirically favored as they uniquely yield ballistic, highly transparent contacts to graphene that survive in high-magnetic-field regimes; however, the microscopic origin of this anomalous transparency has remained a long-standing puzzle. Here, we resolve this paradox by revealing that pristine graphene/MoRe interfaces are governed by the classical A15 superconducting phase. Combining low-temperature transport, X-ray diffraction, and atomic-resolution electron microscopy, we demonstrate that as-deposited MoRe spontaneously forms the A15 phase in direct contact with graphene, whereas thermal annealing triggers a phase transition to a hexagonal close-packed (hcp) structure that severely degrades the Josephson transparency. Crucially, the one-dimensional (1D) transition-metal atomic chains unique to the A15 structure exhibit a near-perfect lattice match (mismatch < 1%) with graphene edges. First-principles calculations further prove that the d-orbitals of these historically renowned 1D chains possibly hybridize with graphene's Dirac cone near the K point. These findings not only uncover the structural and electronic origins of optimal MoRe contacts but also establish A15-phase superconductors as a geometrically ideal, universal platform for realizing robust quantum electronics based on 2D materials.

</details>


### [91] [Pulse Laser Deposited GaFeO3 Thin Film Grown on FTO: Correlated Ferroic Orders and Strong Magnetodielectric Coupling at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39696)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用脉冲激光沉积在FTO玻璃上制备约100.6 nm厚多晶正交GaFeO3薄膜，实现室温单相共存亚铁磁与铁电有序，并观察到低场磁介电耦合，面向低场磁电存储与自旋电子器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温单相多铁材料因铁电与磁有序耦合，是下一代磁电存储和自旋电子技术的候选平台；需解决漏电流、氧空位及室温多铁性共存等问题。

Method: 利用脉冲激光沉积在氟掺杂氧化锡导电玻璃衬底上生长约100.6 nm厚多晶正交GaFeO3薄膜；通过核心能级X射线光电子能谱分析Fe价态与氧空位，结合压电力显微镜、铁电/铁磁/介电及磁介电测量表征多铁性与磁电耦合。

Result: XPS显示Fe2+浓度降低、氧空位形成受抑制，从而降低漏电流并改善铁电性；薄膜在室温同时表现亚铁磁性和铁电性。PFM显示可逆局域极化翻转及不对称蝴蝶回线，矫顽电压为-0.31 V和+1.07 V；1 MHz下相对介电常数约1700、介电损耗角正切约2.83；仅在0.1 kOe小磁场下磁介电系数约2.3%，表明较强磁电耦合。

Conclusion: GaFeO3薄膜是室温多功能器件的有前景平台，尤其适用于低场磁电存储和自旋电子架构。

Abstract: Room-temperature single-phase multiferroics exhibiting coupled ferroelectric and magnetic order are promising candidates for next-generation magnetoelectric memory and spintronic technologies. Here, we report the growth of approximately 100.6 nm thick polycrystalline orthorhombic GaFeO3 thin films on fluorine-doped tin oxide-coated glass (FTO) substrates by pulsed laser deposition. Core-level X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy reveals a reduced Fe2+ concentration and suppressed oxygen-vacancy formation, contributing to reduced leakage current and improved ferroelectric characteristics. The films exhibit robust ferrimagnetism and ferroelectricity at room temperature, confirming the coexistence of ferroic orders within a single phase. Piezoresponse Force Microscopy further demonstrates reversible local polarization switching, accompanied by an asymmetric butterfly loop with coercive voltages of -0.31 V and +1.07 V. The films also exhibit a high relative dielectric constant of approximately 1700 with a relatively low dielectric loss tangent of approximately 2.83 at 1 MHz. Moreover, a significant magnetodielectric coefficient of approximately 2.3% is observed under a relatively small magnetic field of only 0.1 kOe, highlighting strong magnetoelectric coupling. These results establish GaFeO3 thin films as a promising platform for room-temperature multifunctional devices, mainly low-field magnetoelectric memory and spintronic architectures.

</details>


### [92] [Odd Elasticity in Strain-Gradient Continua: Anisotropy Classification and Non-Hermitian Elastodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40581)
*Payam Poorsolhjouy*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文将三维应变梯度奇弹性框架系统化：按手性与非手性点群对称性导出四至六阶奇弹性刚度张量，建立静力/动力控制方程与广义声学张量，并揭示各向异性是实现非Hermitian弹性动力学的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇弹性描述含内部能源、非互易反馈的主动非保守系统（如机器人主动超材料），其刚度张量不满足Maxwell-Betti互易性；现有框架需推广到三维应变梯度情形，以纳入异质系统固有长度尺度并支持多尺度均匀化与设计。

Method: 在三维应变梯度奇弹性中放松主对称性，沿手性与非手性分支应用完整三维点群对称层级，穷举推导四阶、五阶和六阶奇弹性刚度张量；利用动态虚功原理建立平衡方程、高阶边界条件和广义声学张量；并对横向半各向同性奇弹性连续体进行数值色散与能量分析。

Result: 非互易模量可在六阶刚度全各向同性、五阶刚度半各向同性及四阶刚度立方对称下存留；但在各向同性与半各向同性极限下，即便存在奇刚度模量，声学张量仍无条件Hermitian，非Hermitian弹性动力学需要各向异性；横向半各向同性数值案例显示色散谱中的例外点及弹性波分量同时放大/衰减的主动能量交换。

Conclusion: 该工作给出各向异性应变梯度主动微结构多尺度均匀化的理论基础，并为非互易应变梯度超材料设计提供依据；关键结论是奇弹性模量本身不足以保证非Hermitian动力学，对称性降低/各向异性是触发例外点与主动能量交换的必要条件。

Abstract: Odd elasticity extends continuum mechanics to active nonconservative systems containing internal energy sources, such as robotic active metamaterials with nonreciprocal feedback. Modeling these systems as effective continua requires relaxing Maxwell-Betti reciprocity, which results in stiffness tensors lacking major symmetry. In this contribution, we extend this framework to 3D strain-gradient odd elasticity to incorporate the length scales inherent in these heterogeneous systems. By applying the full hierarchy of 3D point-group symmetries along chiral and achiral branches, we derive the exhaustive set of fourth-, fifth-, and sixth-order odd elastic stiffness tensors. Further, utilizing the dynamic principle of virtual work, we formulate the equilibrium equations, higher-order boundary conditions, and the generalized acoustic tensor for elastodynamic analysis. The results demonstrate that nonreciprocal moduli survive even under full isotropy in the sixth-order stiffness, under hemitropy in the fifth-order stiffness, and under cubic symmetry in fourth-order stiffness. In contrast, the elastodynamic analysis reveals that under isotropic and hemitropic limits, despite the underlying odd stiffness moduli, the acoustic tensor remains unconditionally Hermitian. Non-Hermitian elastodynamics is shown to require anisotropy. By numerically analyzing a transversely hemitropic odd elastic continuum, we demonstrate that this symmetry reduction results in non-Hermitian elastodynamic phenomena including exceptional points in the dispersion spectra and active energy exchange through simultaneous amplification and attenuation of the elastic wave components. These results provide the necessary theoretical foundation for future multiscale homogenization frameworks for anisotropic strain-gradient active microstructures, and for the design of nonreciprocal strain-gradient metamaterials.

</details>


### [93] [Tuning La0.7Sr0.3MnO3 magnetic anisotropy via interfacial coupling in homometallic trilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37736)
*Nunzia Coppola,Giovanni Vinai,Alksandr Yu Petrov,Chiara Sacco,Hafiz Sami Ur Rehman,Sofia Scarnicci,Lucia Capogna,Cinzia Di Giorgio,Carmela Aruta,Luigi Maritato,Alice Galdi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过MOKE与磁阻测量研究SrMnO3/La0.7Sr0.3MnO3/SrMnO3三明治异质结，发现界面交换作用可调控LSMO的铁磁行为并在低温诱导自旋重取向、界面自旋玻璃态及交换偏置增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索异质结界面处结构/轨道重构与铁磁/反铁磁竞争如何产生复杂磁性，并验证利用异质结界面调控和定制磁性的策略。

Method: 制备两种不同LSMO厚度的SMO/LSMO/SMO三明治结构，结合磁光克尔效应（MOKE）与磁电阻测量，分析界面作用对磁各向异性、交换耦合和输运行为的影响。

Result: 在125 K以下观察到LSMO易磁化轴偏离室温取向的自旋重取向，归因于界面交换作用超过衬底诱导磁各向异性；界面处超交换与交换作用竞争形成高度 frustrated 的界面自旋玻璃态，使矫顽场和交换偏置增强约20倍；界面诱导的磁晶各向异性改变导致低温下各向异性磁电阻响应占主导。

Conclusion: 界面是调控锰氧化物异质结磁性的有效手段；LSMO厚度可调节界面贡献，从而控制自旋重取向、交换偏置和低温各向异性磁电阻行为。

Abstract: In this work we explore the complex magnetic behaviour of SrMnO3/La0.7Sr0.3MnO3/SrMnO3 trilayers combining magneto-optic Kerr effect (MOKE) and magneto-resistance measurements. The interplay of structural and orbital reconstruction and the competition of ferromagnetism/antiferromagnetism at the interfaces is responsible of a variety of complex magnetic properties, highlighting the use of heterostructures as an effective strategy to tune and control magnetic behaviour. We demonstrate the possibility to tune the magnetic properties of a ferromagnetic La0.7Sr0.3MnO3 (LSMO) layer, embedded in two homometallic antiferromagnetic layers (SrMnO3, SMO), by properly varying the role of interfaces using two different LSMO thickness values. Spin reorientation of the LSMO magnetic easy axis away from its room temperature orientation was observed below 125 K, due to the predominance of exchange interaction at the interface over the substrate-induced magnetic anisotropy, in agreement with established models for homometallic manganite heterostructures. The competition of super-exchange and exchange interactions at the interface results in a highly frustrated interfacial spin glass state, resulting in 20-fold enhancement of the coercive field and exchange bias. The modification of the magnetocrystalline anisotropy driven by the interfacial effects leads to a change in the anisotropic magneto-resistance response of the system, which dominates the low temperature behaviour.

</details>


### [94] [Planar energetics do not determine twin persistence in nanocrystalline aluminium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37925)
*Yue Wu,Yuan Tian,Haoyu Wan,Deng Pan,Mingwei Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 平面能量描述量不能决定纳米晶铝中形变孪晶能否持久保留；两个机器学习势在相同载荷下从相同分离层错前驱分别预测64%与14%的残留孪晶覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面心立方金属的孪生能力通常由平面能量描述量衡量，并作为原子间势的验证目标，但这些描述量能否预测纳米晶中孪晶的持久性尚不清楚。

Method: 比较两个机器学习势，使其复现相同的零载滑移曲线和相近的层状能量面；在相同载荷下从共同的分离层错前驱模拟纳米晶铝的孪生演化，并分析畸变部分位错核心内的力、层错能垒以及晶界网络部分滑移前沿的到达顺序。

Result: 两个势预测的残留孪晶覆盖率为64%和14%。差异源于畸变部分位错核心中沿会移除外侧层错的拖尾平移方向的作用力；外侧层错使中间面剪切的增量势垒降低一半以上，但孪晶完成与层错移除之间仅相差10–21 mJ/m²。孪晶能否持久保留由局部牵引相对于全滑移平分线的取向以及晶界网络部分滑移前沿的到达顺序决定。

Conclusion: 平面能量只设定孪晶形成与层错移除的裕度，不能单独预测孪晶持久性；需纳入部分位错核心力、局部应力取向和滑移前沿时序等动力学因素，仅用平面能量验证势函数不足以判断纳米晶中的孪生行为。

Abstract: Planar energetics are the standard descriptors of twinnability in face-centred cubic metals and the usual validation targets for interatomic potentials. Here we show that they do not determine whether deformation twins persist in nanocrystalline aluminium. Two machine-learned potentials reproducing the same zero-load slip curves and closely spaced layered energy surfaces predict, from a common separated-fault precursor under identical loading, retained twin coverage of 64\% and 14\%. The divergence traces to forces within distorted partial cores acting along the trailing translation that would remove an outer fault. Planar energetics do set the margin: an outer fault more than halves the incremental barrier for middle-plane shear, yet twin completion and fault removal remain separated by only 10--21 mJ m\textsuperscript{-2}. Whether an assembled twin persists is decided instead by the orientation of local traction relative to the full-slip bisector and by the arrival order of partial-slip fronts from the grain-boundary network.

</details>


### [95] [Analytical framework for interpreting thermoreflectance measurements of nanoline gratings under finite spot illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37957)
*Abhishek Pathak (Department of Mechanical Engineering,Carnegie Mellon University),Seungwon Park (Department of Mechanical Engineering,Carnegie Mellon University),Shubham Deshmukh (Department of Mechanical Engineering,Carnegie Mellon University),Alan J. H. McGaughey (Department of Mechanical Engineering,Carnegie Mellon University),Jonathan A. Malen (Department of Mechanical Engineering,Carnegie Mellon University)*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于频域热反射（FDTR）的技术，用于测量表面图案化有金属纳米线光栅的材料堆栈的有效热性质，并建立和验证了考虑有限高斯激光光斑与纳米线尺寸的解析热扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FDTR方法需要扩展到具有微纳图案加热器的样品，以测量随加热器尺寸变化的有效热导率，并借助非扩散热输运探测声子平均自由程（MFP）相关贡献。

Method: 推导FDTR相位响应的解析热扩散模型，纳入泵浦/探测激光的有限高斯光斑半径以及金属纳米线光栅尺寸；制备宽度100–1000 nm的Au纳米线光栅于a-SiO2和c-sapphire衬底上并进行FDTR测量验证。

Conclusion: 验证后的模型可作为稳健基线，将FDTR方法扩展到测量有效热导率随加热器尺寸的变化，特别适用于利用非扩散热输运研究声子MFP依赖热导率贡献的材料。

Abstract: A frequency-domain thermoreflectance (FDTR)-based technique is presented to measure the effective thermal properties of a material stack whose surface is patterned with metallic nanoline gratings that are heated and probed by lasers. An analytical heat diffusion model is derived for predicting the FDTR phase response that incorporates a finite laser spot radius with a Gaussian intensity profile for the pump and probe lasers, as well as the dimensions of the nanoline grating. The model is validated by comparing with measurements of Au nanoline gratings, with widths ranging from 100 to 1000 nm, fabricated on a-SiO$_2$ and $c$-sapphire substrates. Due to the short phonon mean free paths (MFPs) in these substrates, thermal transport remains in the diffusive regime for the whole range of nanoline widths. The model and the measurement show excellent agreement across different laser spot radii and nanoline widths, with a bulk thermal conductivity value of $c$-sapphire and the thermal boundary conductances at the Au-Cr/a-SiO$_2$ and Au-Cr/$c$-sapphire interfaces comparable to our prior measurements and the literature. The validated model will serve as a robust baseline for extending this method to measure effective thermal conductivity as a function of the heater size. This approach is particularly relevant for materials where non-diffusive thermal transport regimes enable probing phonon MFP dependent contributions to the thermal conductivity.

</details>


### [96] [Thermal laser evaporation of superconducting Nb films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38070)
*Yifei Yan,David S. Catherall,Finley B. Donachie,Dorsa Najafipanah,Austin J. Minnich*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了用自建热激光蒸发（TLE）系统在c面蓝宝石上沉积Nb薄膜，并评估其电学和超导性能；薄膜在未加热衬底条件下表现出接近块体的超导转变温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLE是一种新兴PVD技术，可用连续波激光蒸发难熔元素；需要评估其沉积难熔超导元素薄膜的质量，以推动TLE作为难熔元素和化合物通用PVD方法的共识。

Method: 采用1070 nm连续波光纤激光器的自建TLE系统，将533 W激光功率聚焦到约500 μm直径的Nb靶上，在c面蓝宝石衬底上沉积Nb薄膜，并表征厚度、粗糙度、电阻率、RRR和超导转变。

Result: 沉积速率达0.19 Å/s；薄膜厚度22.47±0.05 nm，RMS粗糙度0.23±0.02 nm，室温电阻率21.60±0.05 μΩcm，RRR为5.71；Tc为9.24 K，仅比块体值低10 mK，转变宽度29 mK。

Abstract: Thermal laser evaporation (TLE) is an emerging physical vapor deposition (PVD) technique that uses continuous-wave lasers to evaporate even the most refractory elements. The deposition and characterization of superconducting refractory elements are of interest to assess the quality of films deposited by TLE. Here, we report the deposition of niobium (Nb) on c-plane sapphire using a home-built TLE system equipped with a 1070 nm continuous-wave fiber laser. A deposition rate of 0.19 \r{A}/s was achieved with 533 W of laser power focused to a diameter of approximately 500 $\mu$m on a Nb target. A film with a thickness of $22.47 \pm 0.05$ nm and a root-mean-square (RMS) roughness of $0.23 \pm 0.02$ nm exhibited a room-temperature resistivity of $21.60 \pm 0.05$ $\mu\Omega\mathrm{cm}$ and a residual-resistivity ratio (RRR) of 5.71. The superconducting transition temperature, $T_\mathrm{c}$, of the film was measured as 9.24 K, only 10 mK below the bulk value, with a transition width of 29 mK. Among films deposited without substrate heating, the electrical and superconducting properties of the present films meet or exceed those deposited by other techniques. This work contributes to the emerging consensus regarding TLE as a versatile PVD method for refractory elements and compounds.

</details>


### [97] [Emergent higher-order optical nonlinearity in self-assembled polar supertextures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38138)
*Daniel A. Bustamante Lopez,Deepankar Sri Gyan,Xiaojiang Li,Vladimir A. Stoica,Sujit Das,Peter Meisenheimer,Akash Surampalli,Mohit Tanwani,Xianghan Xu,Aiden Ross,Long-Qing Chen,Sang-Wook Cheong,Lane W. Martin,Ramamoorthy Ramesh,Venkatraman Gopalan,Wanzheng Hu*

TL;DR: 该文报道自组装极性斯格明子在红外激发下产生巨大且超快的二阶谐波，并揭示其源于极性拓扑中局域介电常数调制满足近零介电常数条件所增强的高阶非线性响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越二阶的强高阶光学非线性材料通常稀缺，而巨大光学非线性对先进材料调控及连接光-物质现象与下一代光子学具有重要意义。

Method: 利用瞬态二阶谐波产生测量分析非线性功率依赖，并结合相场模拟研究极性超晶格中多种畴结构的平衡态局域介电常数变化及中红外激发下极性斯格明子的快速极化振荡。

Result: 观察到自组装极性斯格明子在红外激发下的巨大超快二阶谐波，其瞬态强度呈现高阶非线性特征；相场模拟显示每个极性织构内存在强且周期性的局域介电常数变化，并可满足近零介电常数条件，从而无需光子学工程即可显著增强非线性光学响应。

Conclusion: 具有本征强光学非线性的自组装极性拓扑可作为量子信息处理与高速光通信的平台。

Abstract: Giant optical nonlinearities enable advanced materials control and bridge exotic light-matter phenomena to next-generation photonics. However, materials exhibiting strong higher-order optical nonlinearity beyond the second order are typically rare. Here, we report the discovery of a giant and ultrafast second-harmonic generation (SHG) in self-assembled polar skyrmions under mid-infrared excitations. The transient SHG intensity exhibits a distinctive power dependence originating from higher-order nonlinearity. More generally, we show that large higher-order nonlinearity emerges across diverse domain structures in polar superlattices. Phase-field simulations demonstrate a strong and periodic local permittivity variation within each polar texture at equilibrium, and further confirm the fast polarization oscillation in polar skyrmions with mid-infrared excitations. This intense local permittivity modulation can satisfy epsilon-near-zero conditions, unlocking dramatically enhanced nonlinear optical responses without photonic engineering. Consequently, the self-assembled polar topologies with strong inherent optical nonlinearity could establish a platform for quantum information processing and high-speed optical communications.

</details>


### [98] [Crystoria: A Large-Scale Crystal Dataset Linking Thermodynamic and Dynamical Stability for Materials AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38195)
*Ke-Xin Pang,Ze-Feng Gao,Yan Gao,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发布 Crystoria 大规模晶体数据集，将动态稳定性标注扩展到约 265 万个无机晶体结构，为材料 AI 和动力学晶体景观研究提供物理基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模晶体数据库富含结构与热力学性质，但动态稳定性因计算成本高而严重缺乏，导致快速扩张的晶体结构空间与可用于材料研究和机器学习的物理信息之间存在关键缺口。

Method: 系统筛选低能量与亚稳无机晶体，标准化其结构身份，并结合机器学习加速的结构弛豫与基于声子的动态稳定性筛选来构建数据集。

Result: Crystoria 包含 2,649,640 个晶体结构，覆盖 89 种元素、230 个空间群中的 219 个，原胞含 1–48 个原子；其中 1,793,353 个结构（67.68%）被判定为动态稳定，并显示出动态稳定性在化学与晶体学空间中的显著差异。

Conclusion: Crystoria 将动态稳定性信息提升至百万级晶体结构规模，为材料人工智能提供物理可靠的数据基础，并支持对动态晶体景观的系统性探索。

Abstract: Large-scale crystal databases have transformed materials discovery by making millions of structures and thermodynamic properties accessible to materials informatics. Yet dynamical stability remains sparsely characterized at a comparable scale because its evaluation is substantially more computationally demanding than conventional structure and energy calculations. This mismatch creates a critical gap between the rapidly expanding crystal-structure space and the physical information available for materials research and machine learning. Here we introduce Crystoria, a large-scale crystal dataset designed to bring dynamical stability to the scale of modern materials data. We construct Crystoria by systematically curating low-energy and metastable inorganic crystals, standardizing their structural identities, and combining machine-learning-accelerated structural relaxation with phonon-based dynamical screening. The resulting dataset contains 2,649,640 crystal structures, spanning 89 elements, 219 of the 230 space groups, and primitive cells containing 1-48 atoms. Among them, 1,793,353 structures (67.68%) are classified as dynamically stable, revealing substantial variation in dynamical stability across chemical and crystallographic space. By bringing dynamical stability to the scale of millions of crystal structures, Crystoria provides a physically grounded foundation for materials artificial intelligence (AI) and enables systematic exploration of the dynamical crystal landscape.

</details>


### [99] [Finite-Temperature Magnetism and Longitudinal Spin Transport in Compensated Half-Metal Ferrimagnets with Near-Zero Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38302)
*Jiahao Shentu,Jie Li,Jianting Dong,Meng Zhu,Chen Wang,Qing Zhang,Yizhuo Song,Wenqi Sun,Jia Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 系统研究了Heusler型全补偿半金属亚铁磁体Mn3Al的电子结构、有限温度磁性和自旋相关输运性质，并预测Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al磁隧道结在300 K下仍具有232%的巨隧穿磁阻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全补偿亚铁磁体具有近零磁矩、完美自旋极化电子结构和超快自旋动力学，可降低杂散场和开关电流，是高速、低功耗自旋电子器件的 promising 材料平台；因此需要评估Mn3Al在磁隧道结中的应用潜力。

Method: 结合密度泛函理论（DFT）与蒙特卡罗模拟研究Mn3Al的电子结构和有限温度磁性，并计算Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al磁隧道结的自旋相关输运性质。

Result: Mn3Al在0 K下为半金属全补偿亚铁磁体，具有100%自旋极化和完全补偿磁化；300 K下净磁化低于11.6 emu/cc，表明其近补偿亚铁磁序在室温下稳健；Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al隧道结在0 K呈现巨隧穿磁阻，300 K仍高达232%。

Conclusion: Mn3Al及相关半金属全补偿亚铁磁体是磁阻型自旋电子应用的优秀候选材料。

Abstract: Fully compensated ferrimagnets, characterized by nearly vanishing magnetic moments at zero temperature, perfect spin-polarized electronic structures, and ultrafast spin dynamics, represent a highly promising materials platform for next-generation spintronic devices. By minimizing stray magnetic fields and reducing switching currents, FCFs enable a viable path toward high-speed, low-power spintronic devices. In this work, we present a systematic investigation of the electronic structure, finite-temperature magnetism, and spin-dependent transport properties of the Heusler-type fully compensated half-metallic ferrimagnet Mn3Al in magnetic tunnel junctions. We demonstrate that Mn3Al is a half-metallic FCF with 100% spin polarization and fully compensated magnetization at 0 K. Combining density functional theory with Monte Carlo simulations, we characterize its finite-temperature magnetic properties and show that Mn3Al exhibits a net magnetization below 11.6 emu/cc at 300 K, confirming the robustness of its nearly compensated ferrimagnetic order at room temperature. Furthermore, we predict that the Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al MTJ yields a giant tunneling magnetoresistance effect at 0 K, which remains as high as 232% at 300 K. Our results establish Mn3Al and related half-metallic FCFs as excellent candidates for magnetoresistance-based spintronic applications.

</details>


### [100] [First-Principles Identification of Higher-Order Configurational Energy Terms and Their Magnetic-State Dependence in Lithium-Rich Oxyfluorides: From QUBO to HUBO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38321)
*Joonho Bae*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作用自旋极化DFT在富锂氧氟化物中发现QUBO无法表示的三阶非加和构型能，并证明立方HUBO可显著降低拟合误差，同时揭示LMTiOF的高阶项强烈依赖收敛电子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUBO模型可能不足以描述无序岩盐正极材料构型能量中的非加和多体相互作用，因此需要评估从QUBO扩展到HUBO的必要性。

Method: 对Li2MnO2F（LMOF）和Li2Mn0.5Ti0.5O2F（LMTiOF）使用保成分的固定几何DFT能量图，比较QUBO与立方HUBO拟合，并用直接DFT能量差验证三阶非加和性及磁初态对结果的影响。

Result: 立方HUBO能够捕捉QUBO缺失的非加和贡献，并在两种材料中大幅降低拟合误差；直接DFT能量差确认三阶非加和性；LMTiOF的三阶贡献对收敛电子态/磁初态表现出强依赖。

Conclusion: 构型能量建模应纳入高阶项，构建DFT能量图时需考虑磁态依赖；所提取的高阶项应理解为保成分交换变量间的有效耦合，而非唯一确定的微观三原子相互作用。

Abstract: In this work, we use spin-polarized density functional theory (DFT) to identify substantial third-order, nonadditive configurational energy contributions in lithium-rich oxyfluorides that quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) models cannot represent. We test the extension from QUBO to higher-order unconstrained binary optimization (HUBO) in two disordered-rocksalt cathode materials, Li$_2$MnO$_2$F (LMOF) and Li$_2$Mn$_{0.5}$Ti$_{0.5}$O$_2$F (LMTiOF), using composition-preserving DFT energy maps. Using fixed-geometry energy maps, we show that cubic HUBO captures nonadditive contributions absent from QUBO models and substantially reduces fitting errors in both materials. Direct DFT energy differences confirm third-order nonadditivity, while comparisons of magnetic initializations reveal a strong dependence of the LMTiOF contribution on the converged electronic state. These results support the inclusion of higher-order terms when modeling configurational energetics and highlight the need to account for magnetic-state dependence in constructing DFT energy maps. The extracted terms describe effective couplings among composition-preserving exchange variables, rather than uniquely identified microscopic three-atom interactions.

</details>


### [101] [Strain-Induced BCC-to-FCC Transformation in Tungsten Uncovered by Molecular Dynamics Simulations with Machine Learning Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38477)
*Qiu-Hong Hu,YueYue Wang,Xinyi Leng,Hezhu Shao,Luru Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过严格验证的机器学习势分子动力学模拟，发现钨在应变诱导下可发生BCC到FCC的相变，FCC相在低温和位错存在时可超过20 at.%，并遵循Bain与Nishiyama-Wassermann取向关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钨通常被认为不发生多晶型转变，但常规实验难以达到相关热力学与力学条件；同时钨的高温结构应用受脆性限制，需探索应变诱导相变及增韧途径。

Method: 采用经严格验证的机器学习势（MLP）分子动力学（MD）方法研究钨的BCC到FCC转变，并进行结构分析以识别相变机制和取向关系。

Result: 模拟表明应变确实可诱导钨发生BCC到FCC转变；低温及存在位错时FCC相超过20 at.%；完美晶体中适中温度有利于相变动力学；结构分析确认Bain和Nishiyama-Wassermann取向关系。

Conclusion: 研究确立了钨中应变诱导多晶型转变的可行性，为实验制备具有更高延展性的双相钨提供关键指导，有望应对钨高温结构材料工程中的长期挑战。

Abstract: Polymorphic transformations occur in a minority of metals. Tungsten is not one of them. This observation does not rule out its intrinsic phase transition possibility, but may be due to inaccessible thermodynamic and mechanical conditions in conventional experiments. Herein, we investigate the body centered cubic (BCC) to face centered cubic (FCC) transformation in tungsten via a rigorously validated machine learning potential (MLP) molecular dynamics (MD) approach. Our simulations demonstrate that strain induced BCC to FCC transformation indeed occurs in tungsten. The FCC phase exceeds 20 at.% at low temperature and in the presence of dislocations. In the perfect crystal moderate temperatures favor phase transformation kinetics. Structural analysis confirms the transformation following the Bain and Nishiyama Wassermann orientation relationship. These results establish the viability of strain induced polymorphic transformation in tungsten and provide a critical guideline for the experimental production of duplex tungsten with enhanced ductility addressing a longstanding challenge in tungsten material engineering for high-temperature structural applications.

</details>


### [102] [Reciprocal transverse Seebeck and Peltier effects in MoSi2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38506)
*Aina Sakamoto,Shoya Ohsumi,Shogo Yoshida,Ryuji Okazaki,Yoshiki J. Sato*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作在MoSi2单晶中同时实验研究了横向Seebeck效应与横向Peltier效应，并在同一均质非磁性材料中建立了二者的互易关系，无需人工界面工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向热电转换可实现电荷流与热流的正交互转换，是传统纵向热电转换的替代方案；但横向Peltier效应研究较少，且缺乏在同一均质材料中对横向Seebeck与Peltier效应的直接对比。

Method: 选用具有轴依赖导电极性的MoSi2单晶，其Seebeck系数符号随晶轴变化。通过多端电压测量确定感应内电场方向并建立横向Seebeck响应；在施加交流电流下利用锁相热成像直接观测与横向Peltier效应对应的横向温度调制。

Result: 实验确定了MoSi2单晶中的横向Seebeck响应，并直接可视化出与相应横向Peltier效应一致的温度调制，从而在同一材料中验证了两种横向热电效应的互易性。

Conclusion: 研究在无人工界面的均质非磁性MoSi2单晶中实现了互易的横向热电转换，为横向Seebeck与Peltier效应的统一实验研究提供了平台。

Abstract: Transverse thermoelectric conversion, in which charge and heat currents are interconverted in orthogonal directions, has recently attracted increasing attention as an alternative to conventional longitudinal thermoelectric conversion. Although transverse Seebeck effect has been investigated in various materials, its reciprocal process, transverse Peltier effect, remains much less explored, and direct comparison of the two effects in the same homogeneous material is still limited. Here, we experimentally investigate both transverse Seebeck and Peltier effects in a single crystal of MoSi2, an axis-dependent conduction polarity material in which the sign of the Seebeck coefficient varies depending on the crystallographic axis. Multi-terminal voltage measurement determines the direction of the induced internal electric field and establishes the transverse Seebeck response. We also directly visualize the transverse temperature modulation consistent with the corresponding transverse Peltier effect by using lock-in thermography under an applied ac current. These results establish reciprocal transverse thermoelectric conversion in a homogeneous nonmagnetic single crystal without engineered interfaces.

</details>


### [103] [Giant piezomagnetic response driven by spin reorientation in the topological Kagome magnet TbMn6Sn6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38528)
*Hanyang Qian,Wen Sun,Juntao Huo,Guowei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在拓扑Kagome磁体TbMn6Sn6中，作者发现由场诱导自旋重取向引发的异常磁致伸缩响应，在240 K下超过120 ppm，并产生约5000 ppm T-1的巨差分压磁系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小磁场变化下实现大应变响应仍是磁力耦合材料的挑战，尤其在拓扑体系中自旋重构与晶格形变的耦合机制尚不清晰。

Method: 作者测量TbMn6Sn6的磁致伸缩、磁化、磁输运、热膨胀和拉曼光谱，并将磁致伸缩峰值位置与磁化和输运异常及声子能量/线宽变化进行关联分析。

Result: 在自旋重取向温度310 K以下，面内磁场引起磁致伸缩突然增大，240 K时超过120 ppm；差分压磁系数约5000 ppm T-1，远高于其他拓扑磁性材料和商用FeGa合金。该峰值与磁化及磁输运异常一致，热膨胀在自旋重取向边界处改变，拉曼测量显示声子能量和线宽相应异常。

Conclusion: Tb和Mn子晶格通过Tb-Mn交换耦合的竞争磁各向异性形成具有不同平衡应变的磁构型，使自旋重取向成为拓扑磁体中实现巨压磁响应的途径，并为近室温磁力传感与开关提供基础。

Abstract: Achieving a large strain response to a small magnetic-field variation remains a challenge in magnetomechanical materials, particularly in topological systems where the coupling between spin reconstruction and lattice deformation remains unclear. Here, we report an anomalous magnetostrictive response associated with field-induced spin reorientation in the topological Kagome magnet TbMn6Sn6. Below its spin-reorientation temperature of 310 K, an in-plane magnetic field induces an abrupt increase in magnetostriction, which exceeds 120 ppm at 240 K. This transition produces a giant differential piezomagnetic coefficient of approximately 5000 ppm T-1, far exceeding the values reported for other topological magnetic materials and commercial FeGa alloys, and placing TbMn6Sn6 among the most field-sensitive magnetostrictive materials. The field position of this peak agrees with the anomalies in magnetization and magnetotransport, demonstrating that the large differential response is tied to field-induced spin reorientation. Thermal expansion also changes across the spin-reorientation boundary. Raman measurements reveal corresponding anomalies in phonon energy and linewidth, indicating that the reorientation modifies the lattice dynamics. The combined results support a mechanism in which competing magnetic anisotropies of the Tb and Mn sublattices, coupled through Tb-Mn exchange, create magnetic configurations with different equilibrium strains. This finding establishes spin reorientation as a route to giant piezomagnetic responses in topological magnets and provides a basis for magnetomechanical sensing and switching near room temperature.

</details>


### [104] [Uniaxial strain tuning of functional properties in antiferroelectric PbZrO3 membrane capacitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38627)
*Umair Saeed,Huazhang Zhang,David Pesquera,Fernando G\'omez-Ortiz,Jessica Padilla- Pantoja,Jose Santiso,Philippe Ghosez,Gustau Catalan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究将PbZrO3反铁电薄膜电容通过金键合转移到柔性衬底，利用面内单轴动态应变调控反铁电-铁电（AFE-FE）切换，并结合原子模拟揭示畴变体本征应变响应与畴变体相对分布重分布的叠加机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PbZrO3是典型反铁电体系，在储能和神经形态计算中具有潜力，但传统薄膜架构受衬底夹持限制；虽自由-standing膜可释放夹持并实现动态应变工程，但外部应变如何系统调控AFE-FE切换尚缺乏理解。

Method: 采用金键合方法将PZO膜电容转移到柔性衬底，研究面内单轴动态应变对AFE-FE切换及功能特性的影响，并用原子尺度模拟进行佐证。

Result: 面内动态应变可调制AFE-FE切换和功能特性；应变依赖演化源于特定取向畴变体的本征应变依赖性，以及应变诱导的不同畴变体相对 population 重分布的外禀效应叠加。

Conclusion: 研究提供了PZO中单轴应变调控AFE-FE切换的预测性理解，并为利用自由-standing反铁电膜的功能潜力铺平道路。

Abstract: Lead zirconate (PbZrO3, PZO) is a prototypical antiferroelectric (AFE) system promising for applications such as energy storage and neuromorphic computing. However, in conventional thin-film architectures, its functional potential is often limited by substrate clamping. Although emerging freestanding membrane techniques offer an approach to release this clamping and enable dynamic strain engineering, a systematic understanding of how external strain modulates the switching between AFE and ferroelectric (FE) states is missing. In this study, we investigate the uniaxial strain effects on PZO membrane capacitors transferred onto flexible substrates via a gold-bonding method. We demonstrate that in-plane dynamic strain modulates the AFE-FE switching and functional properties. Corroborated by atomistic simulations, we reveal that the strain-dependent evolution arises from the superposition of the intrinsic strain dependence of specific domain orientation variants and extrinsic strain-induced redistribution of the relative population of different domain variants. These findings provide a predictive understanding of uniaxial strain tuning of AFE-FE switching in PZO, and pave the way for exploiting the functional potential of freestanding antiferroelectric membranes.

</details>


### [105] [Amorphous selenium-alloyed TeOx thin-film solar cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38643)
*Zhaoheng Ling,Linqu Luo,Chrysa Aivalioti,Temur Maksudov,Mengxi Zhang,Theodoulos Kalogerakis,Lazaros Panagiotidis,Elefterios Lidorikis,Spyros Doukas,Nikolaos Kalfagiannis,Epameinondas Tziortzios,Leonidas Tsetseris,Huihui Zhu,Ao Liu,Yong-Young Noh,Martin Heeney,Shadi Fatayer,Thomas D. Anthopoulos*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过Se合金化调控真空升华TeO_x薄膜的缺陷化学，抑制残余Te^0并制备出TeO_x:Se薄膜太阳能电池，最高效率约1.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeO_2是一种具有加工通用性和新兴p型半导体行为的宽禁带氧化物，适用于光电与光伏；但可规模化制备的TeO_2衍生薄膜常含残余金属Te^0，引入陷阱态、限制空穴传输并促进载流子复合。

Method: 将TeO_2:Se粉末混合物（90:10 wt%）热蒸发后在空气中225 °C退火至多9 h，制备非晶TeO_x:Se薄膜；结合结构、化学、光学和电学表征分析退火对Se再分布、Te^0抑制、表面粗糙度和价带边的影响，并构建ITO/ZnO/TeO_x:Se/MoO_x/Ag薄膜太阳能电池。

Result: 优化条件下TeO_x:Se薄膜光学带隙为0.98 eV；延长退火促进Se再分布，将残余Te^0抑制至约0.4%，降低表面粗糙度并移动价带边。基于80 nm TeO_x:Se活性层且退火9 h的器件实现300–1000 nm宽带光电流，V_OC=0.50 V，J_SC=8.50 mA cm^-2，FF=0.44，最高PCE约1.87%。

Conclusion: Se合金化TeO_x可作为可行的氧化物衍生薄膜光伏吸收层，且化学态控制是提升光伏性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Tellurium dioxide (TeO$_2$) is a wide-bandgap oxide with attractive processing versatility and emerging p-type semiconducting behaviour, making it a promising material system for optoelectronic and photovoltaic applications. However, TeO$_2$-derived thin films prepared by scalable manufacturing processes, such as vacuum sublimation, can contain residual metallic Te$^0$ species that introduce trap states, limit hole transport, and promote carrier recombination. Here, we demonstrate a selenium alloying strategy to control the defect chemistry of vacuum-sublimed TeO$_x$ films. TeO$_2$:Se powder mixtures (90:10~wt\%) were thermally evaporated and subsequently annealed in air at 225~$^\circ$C for up to 9~h, yielding amorphous TeO$_x$:Se films with an optical bandgap of 0.98~eV under optimised conditions. Structural, chemical, optical, and electrical analyses show that prolonged annealing promotes Se redistribution, suppresses residual Te$^0$ to approximately 0.4\%, reduces the film's surface roughness, and shifts the valence-band edge. To evaluate the potential of TeO$_x$:Se for photovoltaic applications, we fabricated thin-film solar cells with an ITO/ZnO/TeO$_x$:Se/MoO$_x$/Ag architecture. Devices based on an 80~nm TeO$_x$:Se active layer annealed for 9~h exhibit optimal performance, with broadband photocurrent generation from 300 to 1000~nm, yielding an open-circuit voltage of 0.50~V, a short-circuit current density of 8.50~mA~cm$^{-2}$, a fill factor of 0.44, and a maximum power conversion efficiency of approximately 1.87\%. These results establish selenium-alloyed TeO$_x$ as a viable oxide-derived absorber for thin-film photovoltaics and highlight chemical-state control as an effective route for improving photovoltaic performance.

</details>


### [106] [A Luminescent Organic Triradical as an Optically Addressable Qudit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38762)
*Kueyoung E. Kim,Avik Bhanja,Alistair Inglis,William K. Myers,Sam L. Bayliss,Richard H. Friend,Petri Murto*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种由三个三苯甲基自由基共价连接至中心苯环构成的发光三自由基，具有双重发光通道、基态四重态相干控制及室温光学磁探测能力，可作为光学可寻址分子qudit。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发光分子三苯甲基自由基可实现光学可寻址电子自旋，在量子传感中具有潜力；作者希望构建多自旋体系以利用比qubit更大的态空间。

Method: 合成三个三苯甲基自由基共价连接中心苯环的发光三自由基，并通过光致发光光谱、时间分辨测量、光检测磁共振（ODMR）及磁光致发光等手段研究其自旋态与光学性质。

Result: 观察到633 nm和690 nm两个不同时间演化的发光带，分别归属为激发四重态和双重态；基态四重态比双重态低1 meV（11.5 K），室温下双重态与四重态布居近等。实现了光学初始化自旋极化、基态四重态相干控制和光学读出；室温ODMR及5 mT下最高约3%的磁光致发光显示光学量子传感潜力。

Conclusion: 该材料是候选的光学可寻址分子qudit，可利用比qubit更大的状态空间，为分子量子传感和量子信息处理提供新平台。

Abstract: Luminescent molecular trityl radicals provide options for optically-addressable electron spins and are promising for quantum sensing. Here, we present a luminescent triradical comprising three trityl groups covalently attached to a central phenyl ring. We find two distinct photoluminescence bands at 633 nm and 690 nm with different temporal evolutions, which we attribute to excited quartet and doublet states respectively. The ground state quartet is 1 meV (11.5K) lower in energy than the doublet, resulting in near-equal population of doublets and quartets at room temperature. We demonstrate optically initialized spin polarization, coherent control of the ground state quartet, and optical readout. Potential for optical quantum sensing is shown via optically detected magnetic resonance at room temperature and magneto-photoluminescence up to 3% at 5 mT. This material is a candidate optically addressable molecular qudit, providing opportunities to leverage larger state spaces than qubit systems.

</details>


### [107] [Autonomous Quantum Measurement with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38850)
*Eunbi Rha,Abhijatmedhi Chotrattanapituk,Mouyang Cheng,Chung-tao Chou,Shenyong Ye,Ashley S. Dale,Long Ju,Luqiao Liu,Mingda Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: QUANTA 是一个面向量子材料表征的自主智能体 AI 框架，在 PPMS 平台上实现闭环测量规划、排障与数据解释，并在 TaP 和 Co2MnGa 上完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料表征依赖大量实验经验、迭代决策和难以记录转移的实验室知识，亟需自主化、可验证且可推广的 AI 实验框架。

Method: 以 PPMS 为实验平台，QUANTA 结合科学文献、实验协议和历史测量的检索增强，集成实验规划、故障排查和数据解释等专用智能体；另设确定性控制层验证仪器操作，并根据 incoming data 评估测量质量、修正实验计划、选择后续测量。

Result: 在拓扑 Weyl 半金属 TaP 的输运测量以及磁性材料 Co2MnGa 的非线性响应研究中展示了自主表征能力。

Conclusion: QUANTA 可作为证据驱动、安全受控、闭环的通用量子材料表征框架。

Abstract: Quantum materials exhibit diverse electronic and functional phenomena, but their characterization requires extensive experimental expertise, iterative decision-making and accumulated laboratory knowledge that is difficult to document and transfer. Here we introduce Quantum Agent (QUANTA), an agentic artificial intelligence (AI) framework for autonomous quantum-materials characterization. Using a widely adopted Physical Property Measurement System (PPMS) as an experimental platform, QUANTA combines retrieval augmentation using scientific literature, experimental protocols and prior measurements with specialized agents for experimental planning, troubleshooting, and data interpretation. A separate deterministic control layer validates instrument actions. Guided by incoming data, QUANTA evaluates measurement quality, revises experimental plans and selects subsequent measurements. We demonstrate these capabilities through transport measurements of the topological Weyl semimetal TaP, as well as investigations of nonlinear responses in magnetic material Co2MnGa. These results establish QUANTA as a generalizable framework for evidence-driven, safeguarded and closed-loop quantum-materials characterization.

</details>


### [108] [Learning Between the Lines: Interaction Interpolation in a Multi Element Machine Learning Interatomic Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38876)
*Ademola Soyemi,Kipton Barros,Timothy C. Germann,Benjamin T. Nebgen,Brenden W. Hamilton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发了一种面向12种金属合金的轻量通用机器学习原子间势，通过逐步主动学习构建紧凑多样数据集，实现向未见过的高阶合金及新元素的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为每个问题定制MLIAP成本高且限制迁移性；基础MLIAP虽可迁移但规模大、计算昂贵，难以常规使用。

Method: 针对Al、Sc、Ti、V、Cu、Zn、Zr、Nb、Mo、Hf、Ta、W十二种元素，采用逐步主动学习筛选紧凑且多样的训练数据，构建轻量通用MLIAP，并利用已见相互作用的插值实现泛化。

Result: 仅用单质和二元训练数据即可迁移到未见的二元以上高阶合金体系（如三元和四元）；通过网络内的炼金术外推可迁移到新元素，新元素及其合金仅需单质数据。

Conclusion: 基于目标明确、多样性驱动数据集训练的MLIAP能以较低计算成本获得迁移性，为通用且资源高效的材料的MLIAP建模提供了实用路径。

Abstract: The continuous development of machine learning inter-atomic potentials (MLIAPs)has greatly accelerated materials research in recent years. However, the need to con- struct custom MLIAPs for each unique problem remains a bottleneck which raises the question of the transferability of MLIAPs. While foundation MLIAPs can achieve transferability due to the broad coverage of chemical space represented in their training sets, their size and computational cost limits their routine use. We address this gap by developing a lightweight general-purpose MLIAP for metal alloys spanning twelve elements (Al, Sc, Ti, V, Cu, Zn, Zr, Nb, Mo, Hf, Ta, W). Here, we utilize a step-wise active learning (AL) approach to develop a compact yet diverse dataset and model that generalizes well by interpolating from previously seen interactions. The resulting potentials transfer effectively to unseen, higher body-order alloys systems, requiring only unary and binary training data to capture ternary and quaternary systems. The model also transfers well to new elements via an alchemical extrapolation within the network, requiring only unary data for the new element and its alloys. These findings suggest that MLIAPs trained on targeted, diversity-driven datasets provide transferability at a relatively low computational cost, offering a practical path toward general-purpose, resource-efficient MLIAPs for materials modeling.

</details>


### [109] [Development and application of a machine learning potential for diamond surfaces and their sliding interfaces with silicon nitride and silica: effect of passivation, orientation and load](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38937)
*Enrico Pedretti,Maria Clelia Righi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作开发了覆盖C/H/N/O/Si的活性机器学习原子间势，并用其模拟金刚石–Si3N4界面滑动，揭示表面钝化、取向和载荷如何通过控制界面成键决定摩擦与磨损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石摩擦磨损受表面化学及滑动界面能否形成共价键控制；第一性原理模拟虽能研究表面取向和钝化，但受限于数百原子和数十皮秒，难以覆盖真实界面滑动尺度。

Method: 构建C/H/N/O/Si化学空间的反应性机器学习势，训练数据覆盖金刚石、氮化硅、氧化硅及其表面、界面和环境分子；在金刚石表面反应势垒及非晶Si3N4/SiO2结构与力学性质上验证后，模拟四种表面取向、清洁及H/OH钝化终止、0.1–23 GPa法向载荷下的金刚石–Si3N4界面滑动。

Result: 识别出两种滑动机制：一是界面局域剪切，剪切应力与界面键密度成正比；二是剪切分布于较软材料内部，完全界面成键导致粘附主导。钝化、表面取向和载荷共同决定界面成键与摩擦。

Conclusion: 该势以近第一性原理精度将反应性模拟扩展到更大长度和时间尺度，可用于研究金刚石磨损的环境敏感性、犁沟微凸体磨损、化学机械抛光及非晶陶瓷膜性质。

Abstract: Friction and wear of diamond are controlled by its surface chemistry, which determines whether covalent bonds form across the sliding interface. First-principles simulations have explored the role of surface orientation and passivation, but are limited to a few hundred atoms and tens of picoseconds. To bridge this gap, we develop a reactive machine learning interatomic potential for the C/H/N/O/Si chemical space, covering diamond, silicon nitride, silicon oxide, their surfaces, interfaces, and environmental molecules. The potential is trained on a large dataset of diverse structures and is tested on multiple physical properties, including ab initio reaction barriers on diamond surfaces, and structural/mechanical properties of amorphous Si$_3$N$_4$ and SiO$_2$. We then simulate sliding of diamond-Si$_3$N$_4$ interfaces with four surface orientations, clean and H/OH-passivated terminations, and normal loads from 0.1 to 23 GPa. We identify two different regimes: one where sliding is localized at the interface, and shear stress is proportional to the interfacial bond density, and another where shear is distributed inside the softer material, and adhesion due to full interfacial bonding dominates. These results show how passivation, surface orientation and load together determine interfacial bonding and friction, with implications for the environmental sensitivity of diamond wear. More broadly, the potential enables reactive simulations of diamond, silicon nitride and silica with near-ab initio accuracy at length and time scales beyond first-principles methods, providing a useful tool for future studies of wear at plowing asperities, chemical-mechanical polishing, and properties of amorphous ceramic films.

</details>


### [110] [Comment on "Is there a density-gradient expansion for the electronic exchange energy?"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38943)
*Mario Benites,Angel Rosado,Efstratios Manousakis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文回应 Perdew 等对作者结果的质疑，主张长波极化函数 $q^2$ 系数中交换分量 $b_x$ 与相关分量 $b_c$ 依赖库仑相互作用的红外正则化，而二者之和 $b_{xc}=b_x+b_c$ 唯一且与正则化无关；因此所谓“唯一”的交换梯度系数并非唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Perdew 等援引 Qian 的推导，声称传统交换梯度系数是唯一确定的，并据此否定作者关于 $b_x$、$b_c$ 分别依赖红外正则化的发现。本文旨在质疑这一唯一性主张，并说明偏好严格无屏蔽库仑结果缺乏充分依据。

Method: 指出 Qian 推导中的积分 $\int_{-a}^{b}dx/x$ 跨越奇点，不是通常意义下的反常积分；其有限 Cauchy 主值仅通过奇点两侧等间隔剔除得到，属于一种特定正则化，非等间隔剔除会给出不同结果。又将 Sham 使用屏蔽相互作用 $e^{-\lambda_0 r}/r$ 得到的交换梯度系数与 Svendsen--von Barth、Engel--Vosko 的裸库仑计算相比较，并考察 $\lambda_0\to0$ 极限；同时证明分离分量中的正则化歧义在 $b_{xc}$ 中抵消。

Result: 单独交换和相关 $q^2$ 系数依赖红外正则化，但其和 $b_{xc}$ 与正则化无关。Qian 的唯一性来自特殊的主值正则化。Sham 的屏蔽库仑计算与裸库仑计算给出的交换梯度系数不同；即使 $\lambda_0>0$ 极小、$1/\lambda_0$ 达到可观测宇宙尺度，使两种相互作用在实验室尺度上不可区分，差异仍存在。

Conclusion: Perdew 等关于传统交换梯度系数唯一确定的论证不成立；严格无屏蔽库仑结果不应仅因其计算被视为有效就被优先采用。作者的计算表明，分离贡献的歧义在组合系数 $b_{xc}$ 中相消，支持 $b_{xc}$ 唯一且正则化无关、而 $b_x$ 与 $b_c$ 分别依赖红外正则化的结论。

Abstract: Perdew et al. [arXiv:2610.07201 (2026)] dispute our finding [Phys. Rev.
