B 113, 115136 (2026)] that the separate exchange ($b_x$) and correlation ($b_c$) contributions to the $q^2$ coefficient of the long-wavelength proper polarization function depend on the infrared regularization of the Coulomb interaction, although their sum $b_{xc}=b_x+b_c$ does not. We question their claim that the conventional exchange coefficient is uniquely determined. In Qian's [J. Math. Phys. 56, 111901 (2015)] derivation, which Perdew et al. cite in support of their claim of a unique exchange-gradient coefficient, an integral of the form $\int_{-a}^{b}dx/x$ crosses a singularity and does not exist as an ordinary improper integral. Its finite Cauchy principal value (which is a particular way of regularizing the integral) is obtained only by excluding equal intervals on the two sides of the singularity; unequal exclusions give different answers. Independently, Sham's calculation with the screened interaction $e^{-\lambda_0 r}/r$ gives an exchange-gradient coefficient different from the bare-Coulomb calculations of Svendsen--von Barth [Int. J. Quantum Chem. 56, 351 (1995)] and Engel--Vosko [Phys. Rev. B 42, 4940 (1990)]. The discrepancy remains when $\lambda_0>0$ is so small that $1/\lambda_0$ is comparable to the size of the observable universe, making the two interactions experimentally indistinguishable on laboratory scales. Why should the exactly unscreened result be preferred, even if its calculation is accepted as valid? Our calculation shows that the ambiguity of the separate contributions cancels in the combined, regulator-independent coefficient $b_{xc}$.

</details>


### [111] [A rhombic spin-1 interpretation of a room-temperature GaN emitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39081)
*Daniel Hashemi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 室温GaN单缺陷ODMR中，g≈4谱线无法由最小S=3/2模型解释；菱形S=1模型可复现三条谱线位置，但现有数据受未解析频窗漂移限制，尚不能唯一确定自旋指派。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释GaN单缺陷室温ODMR谱，特别是更丰富缺陷类别中最小S=3/2模型遗留的g≈4线，并判定其自旋能级结构。

Method: 比较最小S=3/2模型、含全部三条跃迁的二阶S=3/2拟合与菱形S=1模型对谱线位置的描述；检验g≈4线是否接近两条外线之和，并分析频率窗口偏移的影响；提出零场扫频实验。

Result: 菱形S=1模型以8.1–9.8 MHz的均方根误差复现三条线位置；已测试的二阶S=3/2拟合和已发表最小模型不能复现这些位置；若把g≈4线视为两外线之和附近的独立跃迁，拟合强线的ladder模型仍可行；未解析的频窗移动使现有数据无法唯一确立S=1指派。

Conclusion: 现有数据不足以唯一确定孤立S=1模型，需通过零场扫频直接检验该S=1指派。

Abstract: Single defects in GaN show optically detected magnetic resonance at room temperature. In the more abundant class, a minimal $S=3/2$ model leaves a $g\approx 4$ line unexplained. For one defect, this line lies near the sum of the two outer lines, consistent with transitions among three shared levels. A rhombic $S=1$ model reproduces the three line positions with rms errors of 8.1-9.8 MHz. The tested second-order $S=3/2$ fits containing all three transitions and the published minimal model do not reproduce these positions. A ladder fitted to the strong lines remains viable if the $g\approx 4$ line is a separate transition near the sum of the outer two. An unresolved frequency-window shift prevents the existing data from establishing the $S=1$ assignment uniquely. We propose a zero-field sweep that directly tests the isolated $S=1$ assignment.

</details>


### [112] [Lone-Pair Stereoactivity Overrides Mass Scaling of Thermal Expansion and Heat Conduction in Non-vander Waals Layered Bismuth Oxychalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39213)
*Linzhen Li,Guangwu Zhang,Ziyan Qian,Wei Jing,Yang Lu,Jiaqi Gu,Kaifeng Huang,Yanguang Zhou,Qiye Zheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Bi2O2X（X=S、Se、Te）的热膨胀与热输运由Bi3+ 6s^2孤对电子的立体化学活性主导，而非硫族元素质量；Bi2O2Se在热管理上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为更重的硫族元素会软化同构半导体晶格、提高热膨胀并降低热导率；但Bi2O2X是静电结合而非范德华结合的混合阴离子层状晶体，且Bi3+孤对电子可能具立体化学活性。尽管Bi2O2Se已用于fin和gate-all-around晶体管，其热膨胀和热输运机制仍不清楚。

Method: 采用路径积分分子动力学计算热膨胀；结合四声子散射和Wigner相干性的声子输运计算预测热导率；用300–500 K热反射测量和原位X射线衍射验证Bi2O2Se单晶；并对12 nm宽fin进行声子Boltzmann输运模拟。

Result: 300 K体积热膨胀系数从Bi2O2S的8.14×10^-5 K^-1降至Bi2O2Se的6.72×10^-5 K^-1和Bi2O2Te的6.61×10^-5 K^-1，尽管碲化物晶胞大13%；将S质量代入Bi2O2Se仅改变0.6%。正交Bi2O2S中孤对驱动的Bi偏心使b轴膨胀为a轴的1.63倍，但面内热传导各向异性在3%以内。Bi2O2Se热导率最高：300 K面内1.83 W m^-1 K^-1、跨面0.95 W m^-1 K^-1；Bi2O2S受非谐散射限制，Bi2O2Te受慢跨面声子限制。测量与预测吻合在6.4%以内。12 nm fin比Fourier扩散预测更热，Bi2O2Se可最小化热点温度和栅介质处热失配应变。

Conclusion: 孤对电子立体化学活性而非硫族元素质量决定Bi2O2X的热膨胀和热输运；Bi2O2Se因热导率最优和热失配应变较低，是纳米器件热管理的优选材料。

Abstract: Heavier chalcogens are generally expected to soften isostructural semiconductor lattices, raising thermal expansion and lowering thermal conductivity. Layered bismuth oxychalcogenides, Bi2O2X (X = S, Se, Te), are mixed-anion crystals whose covalent slabs and ionic sheets are bound electrostatically rather than by van der Waals forces and whose Bi3+ 6s^2 lone pairs can become stereochemically active. Despite the rapid development of Bi2O2Se for fin and gate-all-around transistors, its fundamental thermal expansion and heat transport mechanisms remain poorly understood. Here, we show that lone-pair stereoactivity, not chalcogen mass, governs both properties. Path-integral molecular dynamics shows that the volumetric thermal expansion coefficient at 300 K decreases from 8.14 x 10^-5 K^-1 in Bi2O2S to 6.72 x 10^-5 and 6.61 x 10^-5 K^-1 in Bi2O2Se and Bi2O2Te, respectively, despite a 13% larger unit cell in the telluride, whereas substituting the S mass into Bi2O2Se changes its value by only 0.6%. Lone-pair-driven Bi off-centering in orthorhombic Bi2O2S makes b-axis expansion 1.63 times a-axis expansion yet leaves in-plane thermal conduction isotropic within 3%. Phonon transport calculations including four-phonon scattering and Wigner coherence predict a thermal conductivity maximum in Bi2O2Se (1.83 and 0.95 W m^-1 K^-1 in the in-plane and cross-plane directions, respectively, at 300 K) because Bi2O2S is limited by anharmonic scattering and Bi2O2Te by slow cross-plane phonons. Thermoreflectance measurements from 300 to 500 K and in situ X-ray diffraction on Bi2O2Se single crystals agree with these predictions within 6.4%. Phonon Boltzmann transport simulations of 12-nm-wide fins, which run hotter than Fourier diffusion predicts, show that Bi2O2Se minimizes the hotspot temperature and the thermal-mismatch strain at the gate dielectric.

</details>


### [113] [Colossal magnetoresistance in narrow-gap magnetic semiconductor Eu$_2$CdAs$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39218)
*Zhiyu Zhou,Zhi-Cheng Wang,Xiyu Chen,Xin-Yu Zhao,Yaran Duan,Shiran Tao,Guang-Han Cao,Shuai Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本工作报道窄带隙Zintl砷化物Eu2CdAs2的合成、相变与磁输运性质：其在TN=9 K发生反铁磁有序，呈低激活能3.2 meV的半导体行为，并在9 T下实现约-10^4%的巨大CMR效应；TN以下出现无宏观铁磁序的蝴蝶型磁阻迟滞，且Eu基Zintl相中最大CMR与低温激活能呈近指数标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现同时具有大CMR效应且响应可精确调控的反铁磁材料仍是材料科学中的重要挑战。

Method: 合成Eu2CdAs2并测量其相变与磁输运性质；结合对已报道Eu基Zintl相的系统调研，分析最大CMR幅度与低温激活能之间的关系。

Result: Eu2CdAs2在TN=9 K发生反铁磁有序，低温激活能为3.2 meV，9 T下CMR达-10^4%；TN以下观察到蝴蝶型磁阻迟滞但无宏观铁磁序，暗示其输运异常由反铁磁畴动力学和/或局域磁极化子的形成与渗流主导；Eu基Zintl相中最大CMR与激活能呈近指数标度趋势。

Conclusion: 带隙工程是调控磁输运的有效策略，Eu基Zintl相可作为探索和操纵CMR效应的通用平台。

Abstract: Discovering antiferromagnetic materials with a sizable colossal magnetoresistance (CMR) effect and precisely tuning their responses remain significant challenges in materials science. This work reports the synthesis, phase transitions, and magnetotransport properties of the narrow-gap Zintl arsenide Eu$_2$CdAs$_2$. The compound undergoes antiferromagnetic ordering at $T_\mathrm{N} = 9$ K and exhibits semiconducting behavior with a low-temperature activation energy of 3.2 meV, achieving a large CMR effect of $-10^4\%$ at 9 T. Below $T_\mathrm{N}$, a distinct butterfly-shaped magnetoresistance hysteresis emerges without any macroscopic ferromagnetic order, revealing a transport anomaly likely dictated by AFM domain dynamics and/or the formation and percolation of localized magnetic polarons. Furthermore, a systematic survey of reported Eu-based Zintl phases uncovers a nearly exponential scaling trend between the maximum CMR magnitude and the low-temperature activation energy. This correlation demonstrates that bandgap engineering serves as an effective strategy for tailoring magnetotransport, establishing Eu-containing Zintl phases as a versatile platform for exploring and manipulating CMR effects.

</details>


### [114] [Polarization-locked chiral persistent spin textures in two-dimensional lead-halide perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39250)
*Palanichamy Gayathri,Biplab Sanyal,Jonas Fransson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文研究手性间隔阳离子形成的二维杂化钙钛矿（极性Sohncke群P2₁）中的自旋轨道场，并用第一性原理对10种极性手性铅卤钙钛矿、其对映体及4种卤素取代类似物进行自旋织构分析，发现强极性化合物导带由持续自旋织构项主导，对映体可切换自旋方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性二维钙钛矿可将极性轴置于无机层内，可能产生不同于常规Rashba/Dresselhaus的自旋轨道场；作者希望厘清该几何构型允许的自旋织构，并检验其对映体与卤素取代对自旋极化及持续自旋螺旋的影响。

Method: 基于P2₁/C₂对称性分析自旋轨道场；对每个能带对拟合34个两带k·p模型，并以自旋方向与第一性原理计算结果的一致性而非能带能量进行排序；比较10种极性手性铅卤钙钛矿、其镜面对映体及4种卤素取代类似物的自旋织构。

Result: C₂点群（二重轴位于层内）禁止螺旋Rashba场，允许线性项γ₁k_xσ_z（面内极性轴的Rashba相互作用）以及手性相关的Dresselhaus项和径向项。所有强极性化合物的导带均可仅用持续项描述，γ₁=1.09–1.98 eV Å，自旋一致度至少0.98，而最佳Rashba或Dresselhaus模型仅达0.20。每种化合物的R和S对映体具有相反极化、相反γ₁及等幅相反持续自旋织构，10种化合物均如此；γ₁大小与面内Pb–X–Pb角差异相关（r=0.95）；沿自旋劈裂方向电子质量为0.26–0.43 m_e，对应持续自旋螺旋波长3.4–8.2 nm。

Conclusion: 该极性手性二维钙钛矿中的导带自旋织构由持续自旋项主导且非常稳健，对映体选择可决定自旋方向；γ₁与结构畸变相关并给出纳米尺度持续自旋螺旋，为手性钙钛矿自旋电子学与对映体自旋调控提供依据。

Abstract: Chiral spacer cations crystallize two-dimensional hybrid perovskites in the polar Sohncke group $P2_1$, which places the polar axis in the inorganic layer. We analyze the spin-orbit field that this geometry allows and test it against first-principles spin textures of ten polar chiral lead-halide perovskites, their mirror enantiomers and four halide-substituted analogues. The point group $C_2$ with the twofold axis in the layer forbids the helical Rashba field and allows a linear term $\gamma_1 k_x \sigma_z$, the Rashba interaction of the in-plane polar axis, together with chirality-related Dresselhaus and radial terms. Thirty-four two-band $k \cdot p$ models were fitted to each band pair and ranked by the agreement of their spin directions with the calculation rather than by the band energies, which cannot distinguish them. In all strongly polar compounds, the conduction band is described by the persistent term alone, with $\gamma_1 = 1.09$-$1.98$ eV {\AA} and a spin agreement of at least 0.98, whereas the best Rashba or Dresselhaus model reaches 0.20. The $R$ and $S$ enantiomers of each compound have opposite polarizations, and therefore opposite $\gamma_1$ and opposite persistent spin textures of equal magnitude, as required by the mirror that relates them; this holds in all ten compounds, so the choice of the enantiomer selects the spin direction. The magnitude of $\gamma_1$ correlates with the disparity of the in-plane Pb-X-Pb angles ($r = 0.95$). With electron masses of $0.26$-$0.43\,m_e$ along the spin-splitting directions, the associated persistent spin helices have wavelengths of 3.4-8.2 nm.

</details>


### [115] [Th$^{4+}$ ion cluster structures and electric field gradients in $^{229}$Th:CaF$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39288)
*Yuxiang Li,Lin Li,Qiaorui Gong,Shanming Li,Chengchun Zhao,Lu Deng,Peixiong Zhang,Yin Hang,Longsheng Ma,Shining Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 第一性原理计算表明，由四个间隙氟离子补偿的中性Th二聚体是Th:CaF2中实验高电场梯度位点3的候选微观结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 229Th低能核同质异能跃迁可用于固态核光钟频率参考；CaF2等宽带隙氟化物可高密度嵌入Th核，但Th4+取代Ca2+需要电荷补偿，缺陷结构会影响核四极劈裂。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算研究由四个间隙F-离子补偿的中性Th二聚体，通过相对形成能筛选不同补偿几何，计算电场梯度、不对称参数和四极劈裂，并与C13测量谱比较。

Result: 筛选出的低能构型具有相似的局域结构、较大的电场梯度和较小的不对称参数，与实验高EFG位点3一致；计算四极劈裂进一步与C13谱进行对照。

Conclusion: 能量学和谱学结果共同支持F-离子补偿的Th二聚体是Th:CaF2中位点3的候选微观结构。

Abstract: The low-energy nuclear isomeric transition of $^{229}$Th is a promising frequency reference for a solid-state nuclear optical clock. Wide-band-gap fluoride crystals such as CaF$_2$ offer transparent hosts for embedding Th nuclei at high density. Substitution of Th$^{4+}$ for Ca$^{2+}$ requires charge compensation, linking the local defect structure to nuclear quadrupole splitting. We use first-principles calculations to examine neutral Th dimers compensated by four interstitial F ions ($\mathrm{F}_{i}^{-}$). Screening different compensation geometries by their relative formation energies identifies a family of low-energy configurations with closely similar local structures. These configurations share large electric field gradients and small asymmetry parameters, consistent with the experimental high-EFG site 3. Their calculated quadrupole splittings are further compared with the measured C13 spectra. Together, the energetic and spectroscopic results support $\mathrm{F}_{i}^{-}$-compensated Th dimers as candidate microscopic structures for site 3 in Th:CaF$_2$.

</details>


### [116] [Predicting unstable planar fault energies in multicomponent alloys from pure elemental components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39495)
*C Tang,J Liu,J Yi,M R Barnett*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用EAM势结合DFT研究不稳定平面层错能（UPFE）的温度与成分依赖性及其与剪切模量的关系，并提出由纯元素估算多组分合金μb_p和筛选UPFE的简便途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UPFE控制滑移与孪生，但无法实验测量；同时其与剪切模量、Burgers矢量乘积μb_p的经验关系尚需系统检验。

Method: 采用EAM势并以DFT补充，计算纯fcc元素、固溶体、金属间化合物及Co-Cr-Fe-Mn-Ni体系中的UPFE，分析温度、成分和化学复杂度对UPFE及μb_p关系的影响。

Result: 低SFE纯fcc元素的UPFE温度敏感性显著低于SFE；UPFE与μb_p的线性关系适用于密排元素和固溶体，但对金属间化合物和非密排元素偏离。Co-Cr-Fe-Mn-Ni二元合金存在成分相关偏差，化学复杂度增加时相关性增强；多组分合金μb_p与成分加权元素μb_p线性相关（R²=0.95），但存在系统负偏差。

Conclusion: UPFE与μb_p的关系受结构与化学复杂度影响；多组分合金的μb_p可由纯元素成分加权近似估算，为低成本筛选UPFE提供依据。

Abstract: The unstable planar fault energies (UPFEs) govern slip and twinning but are not experimentally measurable. Using EAM potentials complemented by DFT, we investigate their temperature and compositional dependence and their relationship with the shear modulus. For pure \textit{fcc} elements with low stacking fault energies (SFEs), UPFEs are substantially less temperature-sensitive than SFEs. The reported linear relationship between UPFEs and the product of shear modulus and Burgers vector ($\mu b_{\mathrm{p}}$) holds for close-packed elements and solid solutions, but deviates for intermetallics and non-close-packed elements. In the Co-Cr-Fe-Mn-Ni system, binary alloys show composition-dependent deviations, while the correlation strengthens with increasing chemical complexity. Furthermore, $\mu b_{\mathrm{p}}$ of multicomponent alloys linearly correlates with the composition-weighted elemental $\mu b_{\mathrm{p}}$ ($R^2=0.95$), despite a systematic negative deviation. This provides a simple, computationally inexpensive route for estimating $\mu b_{\mathrm{p}}$ and screening UPFEs directly from pure elemental constituents.

</details>


### [117] [Ultrasound-Assisted Facile Nanoconfinement of Luminescent Guests in Zirconium Metal-Organic Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39622)
*Yiyi Wang,James D. Taylor,Jin-Chong Tan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文报道了一种加速的声化学方法，可在10分钟内合成UiO-66/UiO-67纳米晶体，并在1小时内原位包封发光客体分子，构建可调谐的guest-MOF发光复合材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统溶剂热法合成MOF及客体封装通常耗时约24小时，亟需快速、高效且可调控的发光MOF复合材料制备策略。

Method: 采用探针超声替代常规浴超声，快速合成多孔锆基UiO-66和UiO-67纳米晶体，并原位封装罗丹明B和荧光素，构建单客体及双客体荧光主客体体系。

Result: 超声10分钟即可获得结晶UiO-66/UiO-67，产率最高达92%，1小时内完成客体封装；所得材料具有宽带发射、可调颜色、高量子产率和改善的寿命，并表现出明确晶面及作为固态照明光活性薄膜的潜力。

Conclusion: 声化学条件、结构缺陷与光物理行为之间存在协同作用，所制备LG@UiO材料可作为可调谐光源，在光学传感和纳米光子学中具有应用前景。

Abstract: An accelerated sonochemical route for fabricating luminescent guest-MOF (LG@MOF) composites is demonstrated, for which light-emitting molecules are nanoconfined within porous zirconium-based hosts using probe ultrasonication rather than conventional bath sonication. Ultrasonication enables the rapid synthesis of crystalline UiO-66 and UiO-67 nanocrystals within 10 minutes, with material yields of up to 92%, and facile in situ guest encapsulation within 1 hour, hence notably faster than the traditional 24-hour solvothermal route. Using UiO-66 and the larger-pore UiO-67 as porous hosts, tuneable single- and dual-guest fluorescent host-guest systems incorporating Rhodamine B (RB) and Fluorescein (F) were achieved. Photophysical characterisation showed broadband emission, tuneable colours, high quantum yield and improved lifetime, highlighting the synergy between sonochemical conditions, structural defects, and photophysical behaviour. Additionally, the sonochemically prepared LG@UiO particles show well-defined crystal facets and potential as photoactive thin films for solid-state lighting, demonstrating their promise as a tuneable light source for optical sensing and nanophotonics.

</details>


### [118] [Origin and photoluminescence of a shallow hydrogen-related donor in silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39623)
*Miaomiao Han,Peter Deak,Bing Huang,Adam Gali*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用高精度密度泛函理论计算，研究硅中氢与自间隙复合体 I_n 的相互作用，提出 I4H_BC 是氢相关浅施主 MT2 的候选结构，并可解释 X_i 光致发光线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅中与氢相关的浅施主（SHDs）及 X、X_i 等 PL 线的原子结构尚不明确，MT2 是否对应双稳态负-U 缺陷也缺乏确定解释。

Method: 采用高精度密度泛函理论计算 H 与自间隙复合体 I_n 的相互作用，分析 I_nH_BC 的形成、电子结构、自旋性质及 PL 特征，并与 MT2、X 线和 X_i 线比较。

Result: H 可插入 I_n 中的 Si-Si 键形成键心间隙氢 I_nH_BC，该反应放热并产生浅有效质量施主；I4H_BC 的电子、自旋和 PL 性质与 MT2 高度接近。该缺陷表现为负-U 双稳态，仅在光照下可检测；H 可通过光助机制在等价 BC 位间跃迁，导致实验观测到的运动平均四方对称。其零声子线不同于裸 I4 的 X 线，而落在 X_i 线范围内。

Conclusion: I4H 可作为一类氢相关浅施主的原型，表明 H 攻击 I_n 团簇时可形成类似 SHDs，并以 X_i 线作为其 PL 指纹。

Abstract: Paramagnetic color centers in silicon are being investigated recently for use in quantum technologies. While the origin of most of the prominent photoluminescence (PL) lines are well understood, that is not the case for those connected to hydrogen, even though it is almost always present in silicon, e.g., shallow hydrogen-related donors (SHDs), but their atomic structure remains elusive. Recent experiments suggested connection between the SHDs and the so-called X and the B$_i$ (or X$_i$) PL-lines. An SHD, termed the MT2 center, was tentatively assigned to a bistable, negative-U defect, arising due to the interaction of hydrogen with the self-interstitial complex I$_4$. Using high-level density functional theory calculations, we investigate the interaction of hydrogen with self-interstitial complexes In. A shallow effective-mass-theory donor arises when hydrogen attacks a Si-Si bond in I$_n$, inserting itself as a bond-center interstitial (I$_n$H$_{BC}$) in an exothermic reaction. We demonstrate this in the case of a single self-interstitial and of a complex of four. The latter I$_\mathrm{4}$H$_\mathrm{BC}$ complex shows electronic, spin and PL properties remarkably close to MT2. We find that hydrogen in the presence of I$_4$ follows the same behavior as in pristine silicon, i.e., it is a negative-U bistable defect, only detectable under illumination. In the I$_4$H$_{BC}$ complex, H can hop via photo-assisted mechanism between equivalent BC sites of the I$_4$ complex, leading to a motional averaged tetragonal symmetry as experimentally observed for MT2. The zero-phonon line is distinct from the X-line (attributed to the bare I$_4$ complex), and falls well into the range of the X$_i$ lines. Our results identify the I$_4$H complex as a prototype for a class of SHDs, inferring that similar SHDs may arise, with the X$_i$ lines as their PL fingerprints, when H attacks I$_n$ clusters.

</details>


### [119] [Observation of light-induced topological state in graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39695)
*Sotirios Fragkos,Ann Chantal Goutier,Umberto Dellasette,Dominique Descamps,St\'ephane Petit,Wiebke Bennecke,Philip Hofmann,Marcel Reutzel,Stefan Mathias,Yann Mairesse,Michael Sch\"uler,Samuel Beaulieu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用圆偏振光驱动石墨烯，并通过圆二色时间分辨极紫外动量显微结合第一性原理计算，观测到狄拉克点附近由泵浦诱导的光电子圆二色性，其随泵浦螺旋性和跨狄拉克能量反转，为光诱导赝自旋织构、贝里曲率及Floquet拓扑绝缘态提供了光谱学证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有输运和全光测量虽提示光诱导反常霍尔响应，但无法在能量和动量空间分辨电子态；此前时间分辨角分辨光电子能谱多使用线偏振驱动，保持时间反演对称性，不能诱导拓扑相。因此需要在圆偏振光驱动下直接探测石墨烯电子态及其拓扑特征。

Method: 结合圆二色时间分辨极紫外动量显微与从头算理论，研究圆偏振光驱动石墨烯的动量分辨光电子谱和圆二色响应，并与计算结果比较。

Result: 观测到狄拉克点附近泵浦诱导的光电子圆二色性；该信号随泵浦光螺旋性反转，并在跨越狄拉克能量时反号，与理论计算一致。

Conclusion: 测得的圆二色响应可关联到光诱导赝自旋织构及其贝里曲率，从而为石墨烯中Floquet工程拓扑绝缘态提供光谱学证据。

Abstract: Driving quantum materials with light can reshape not only their electronic band structures but also the geometry of their electronic wavefunctions. In graphene, circularly polarized light is predicted to open gaps at the Dirac points and induce a pseudospin texture and Berry curvature characteristic of the Haldane model, realizing a Floquet Chern insulator. Transport and all-optical measurements have provided evidence for a light-induced anomalous Hall response, but cannot resolve the underlying electronic states in both energy and momentum space. Time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy has revealed Floquet-Bloch sidebands and hybridization gaps at crossings between Floquet bands. However, these experiments used linearly polarized driving, which preserves time-reversal symmetry and thus does not induce a topological phase. Here, we combine circular-dichroic time-resolved extreme-ultraviolet momentum microscopy with ab initio theory to investigate graphene driven by circularly polarized light. We observe a pump-induced photoemission circular dichroism near the Dirac point that reverses sign with pump helicity and across the Dirac energy, in agreement with our calculations. These results link the measured dichroic response to the light-induced pseudospin texture and its associated Berry curvature, providing spectroscopic evidence for the Floquet-engineered topological insulating state in graphene.

</details>


### [120] [I$_4$ complex acting as near-infrared emitter with a quantum memory in silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39713)
*Miaomiao Han,Song Li,Gerg\H{o} Thiering,Thomas Frauenheim,Peter De\'ak,Bing Huang,Adam Gali*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用 HSE06 杂化泛函 DFT 证明硅中 1039.7 meV 的 X 线源自裸 I4 自间隙复合体，并揭示其具有可用于硅基自旋量子存储的长寿命三重态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外硅色心可作为与 CMOS 工艺兼容的自旋-光子接口，但 X 线长期归属的 I4 复合体之微观结构和自旋性质存在争议且缺乏预测性表征。

Method: 采用 HSE06 杂化泛函密度泛函理论计算 I4 复合体的零声子线、声子边带、EPR 超精细常数、电荷跃迁能级及激发态自旋性质。

Result: 计算定量复现 X 线零声子线、完整声子边带结构（次级峰为强耦合准局域模的一/二声子复制）和 EPR 超精细常数；电荷跃迁能级为 VBM+0.08 eV，排除其与 B3 EPR 中心及深施主能级的关联；发现与单重态相隔约 1.6 meV 的亚稳自旋-1 三重态，与 NL51 EPR 信号一致。

Conclusion: I4 复合体的 D2d 对称性通过一阶自旋-轨道耦合抑制系间窜越和磷光，预示极长自旋-晶格弛豫时间；结合可分辨零场分裂与近红外发射，其三重态构成硅中本征可用的自旋量子存储器。

Abstract: Silicon color centers emitting in the near infrared offer a route to spin--photon interfaces compatible with mature complementary metal--oxide--semiconductor fabrication. A key unresolved candidate is the X-line at 1039.7 meV in the photoluminescence (PL) spectrum, long attributed to a complex of four self-interstitials (I$_4$), yet whose microscopic structure and spin properties have remained controversial and uncharacterized at a predictive level. Using hybrid-functional (HSE06) density functional theory, we unambiguously establish the bare I$_4$ complex as the origin of the X-line: our calculations quantitatively reproduce the zero-phonon line, the complete phonon sideband structure---identifying the prominent secondary peaks as one- and two-phonon replicas of strongly coupled quasi-local modes---and the EPR hyperfine constants. The charge transition level is found at VBM$+$0.08~eV, conclusively ruling out the previously proposed association with the B3 EPR center and its correlated deep donor level. Most significantly, we identify a metastable spin-1 triplet excited state separated from the singlet by $\sim$1.6~meV, consistent with the NL51 EPR signal. The $D_{2d}$ symmetry of the defect suppresses intersystem crossing and phosphorescence by first-order spin--orbit coupling, implying an exceptionally long spin--lattice relaxation time. Combined with a spectrally resolvable zero-field splitting and near-infrared emission, the triplet state of the I$_4$ complex constitutes a viable spin quantum memory intrinsic to silicon.

</details>


### [121] [Direct Observation of Electron Trapping States in SiO$_2$ by Constant-Final-State Photoelectron Yield Spectroscopy: Insights into the Electron-Transfer Model of Contact Electrification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39748)
*Renjiro Shibuya,Manato Tateno,Runa Hoshikawa,Tomohiro Ishibashi,Hirohiko Fukagawa,Hisao Ishii*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用恒末态光电子产额谱（CFS-YS）直接定量测定非晶SiO2的带隙内态密度（in-gap DOS），揭示宽能量分布的电子俘获态介导接触起电中的电子转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SiO2的静电充电对诸多应用至关重要，接触起电（CE）是主要充电来源，电子转移是其主导机制之一；但现有电子转移模型所涉及的DOS尚未被实验表征，且传统光电子能谱因绝缘样品充电效应难以测量SiO2的DOS。

Method: 采用恒末态光电子产额谱（CFS-YS）定量测定非晶SiO2的带隙内DOS，并结合电子注入、真空退火处理以及基于实验DOS的充电模拟，分析电子俘获行为与充电特性。

Result: 观察到带隙内弱占据态；电子注入后其显著增加，表明电子俘获态分布于较宽能量范围；真空退火明显改变带隙内DOS和电子俘获行为，提示包括水在内的表面吸附物起作用；基于实验DOS的模拟近似重现已有充电行为，并显示积累电荷集中在表面附近。

Conclusion: 分布于宽能量范围的带隙内态介导接触起电过程中的电子转移；评估电子转移模型时应考虑实验测定的DOS。

Abstract: Understanding electrostatic charging of silicon dioxide (SiO$_2$) is important for electrostatic control in a wide range of applications. Contact electrification (CE) is a major source of such charging, and electron transfer is considered one of its leading mechanisms. Several electron-transfer models have been proposed to explain CE; however, the density of states (DOS) involved in electron transfer has not been experimentally characterized. Determining this DOS is therefore essential for understanding the charging mechanism. However, measuring the DOS of insulating materials such as SiO$_2$ remains challenging: photoemission spectroscopy, the standard technique for probing the DOS of materials, is unsuitable for highly insulating materials because sample charging during measurement can cause spectral distortion or even complete signal loss. Here, we directly and quantitatively determined the in-gap DOS of amorphous SiO$_2$ using constant-final-state photoelectron yield spectroscopy (CFS-YS). Weak occupied states were observed within the band gap, and their marked increase after electron injection revealed that electron-trapping states are distributed over a broad energy range. Vacuum annealing markedly altered the in-gap DOS and electron-trapping behavior. The observed changes suggest a role for surface adsorbates, including water, in the charging behavior of SiO$_2$. Charging simulations based on the experimentally measured in-gap DOS approximately reproduced the previously reported charging behavior and showed that the accumulated charge was concentrated near the surface. These findings indicate that in-gap states distributed over a broad energy range mediate electron transfer during CE and highlight the importance of considering experimentally determined DOS when evaluating proposed electron-transfer models.

</details>


### [122] [Sign reversal of ST-FMR signals in CoFeB/Pt explained by contributions beyond spin-orbit torque](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39749)
*Christian Berg,Ao Du,Durgesh Kumar,Sinan Shu,Sourabh Manna,Sachin Krishnia,Mathias Kl\"aui*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究指出传统ST-FMR分析中的非力矩电压贡献会在CoFeB/Pt体系中造成阻尼类SOT效率的虚假符号反转，并提出可靠分离SOT信号的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOT对低功耗磁存储很重要，ST-FMR常用于量化SOT效率，但整流电压中可能包含与SOT无直接关系的贡献，导致提取效率不准确并解释文献中SOT效率差异较大的问题。

Method: 在CoFeB/Pt异质结中测量阻尼类力矩效率随Pt厚度的变化，并结合互补的测量几何与分析方式，将力矩诱导信号与具有相同角度依赖的额外电压贡献分离。

Result: 传统ST-FMR分析在Pt厚度约10 nm处给出提取效率的表观符号反转；分离后表明该反转可由非力矩电压贡献产生。

Conclusion: 需要区分SOT诱导信号与寄生电压贡献，采用互补几何和分析方法才能可靠确定SOT效率。

Abstract: Spin-orbit torques (SOT) provide an efficient route for electrical control of magnetization and is of central interest for low-power magnetic memory applications. Spin-torque ferromagnetic resonance (ST-FMR) is a widely used technique to quantify SOT efficiencies. However, the rectified voltage measured in ST-FMR can contain contributions that are not directly related to SOTs, which affects the accuracy of the extracted torque efficiencies. This may contribute to the large variation of reported SOT efficiencies in literature. In this study, we investigate the damping-like torque efficiencies in CoFeB/Pt heterostructures as a function of Pt thickness. Conventional ST-FMR analysis yields an apparent sign reversal of the extracted efficiency at a Pt thickness of approximately 10 nm. By combining complementary measurement geometries and analysis approaches, we separate the torque-induced signal from additional voltage contributions that share the same angular dependence. Our results show that non-torque voltage contributions can produce an apparent sign reversal in conventional ST-FMR analysis, highlighting the need to distinguish SOT-induced signals from parasitic contributions and we show how to reliably determine the SOT efficiencies.

</details>


### [123] [Growth and morphology of core-shell nanowires incorporating dissimilar hexagonal semiconductors: the case of InAs-ZnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39770)
*Danylo Mosiiets (NEEL - NPSC),Susheng Tan (PITT),Sergey Frolov (PITT),Mo\"ira Hocevar (NEEL - NPSC),Joel Cibert (NEEL - NPSC),Edith Bellet-Amalric (PHELIQS)*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过超高真空互联双腔分子束外延生长并用电镜研究了III-V/II-VI半导体核壳纳米线；InAs核与ZnTe壳晶格匹配良好，可实现无缺陷纤锌矿结构复制，并揭示了壳层侧壁刻面演化和生长动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将III-V族InAs核与II-VI族ZnTe壳集成为核壳纳米线的可行性，并获得无缺陷异质外延及壳层形貌/生长机制。

Method: 在超高真空下连接的两个专用分子束外延腔中依次生长InAs核和ZnTe壳，并用电子显微镜表征；分析不同壳层厚度下的晶体结构、侧壁刻面及生长速率随纳米线半径的变化。

Result: ZnTe壳复制InAs核的纤锌矿结构且不产生缺陷，壳厚从约1个单层到25 nm均成立，归因于InAs/ZnTe良好晶格匹配。核侧壁共存(1 100) m刻面和(11 20) a刻面；壳生长时m刻面逐渐增大并最终使a刻面消失。最初几个单层生长快、粘附系数高，随后生长减慢并接近ZnTe核上的速率；速率对纳米线半径的依赖表明发生单核成核。

Conclusion: InAs/ZnTe核壳纳米线可实现宽厚度范围内的无缺陷纤锌矿壳层外延；ZnTe壳生长伴随m刻面扩张和a刻面消失，其动力学由初始高粘附快速生长转向受单核成核控制的较慢生长。

Abstract: Core-shell nanowires incorporating semiconductors from two groups, III-V for the core and II-VI for the shell, have been grown by molecular beam epitaxy in two dedicated chambers connected under ultra-high vacuum, and studied by electron microscopy. During the growth of the shell, the wurtzite structure of the core is replicated in the shell, without any creation of defects. This is true for a wide range of shell thickness, from about one monolayer, up to 25 nm thanks to the good lattice match between the InAs material of the core and the ZnTe material of the shell. While (1 100) m-facets and (11 20) a-facets coexist on the sidewalls of the core, during the growth of the ZnTe shell the size of the m-facets progressively increases at the expense of the a-facets which eventually disappear. A fast growth of the ZnTe shell is observed for the first few monolayers, with a high sticking; this is followed by a slower growth, of the same order of magnitude as observed on a ZnTe core, with a dependence on the nanowire radius which points to the occurrence of mononuclear nucleation

</details>


### [124] [Epitaxial Magnon Junctions with Y3Fe5O12/Gd3Ga5O12/Pt-nanoparticles/Y3Fe5O12](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39841)
*Jianing Lin,Haoyu Zhuang,Tianyi Zhang,Jiyang Ou,Jihao Xia,Hongyu Wang,Jiang Niu,Qinglong Yu,Caihua Wan,Guoqiang Yu,Richeng Yu,Xiufeng Han*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文报道了一种外延磁子结：以GGG/Pt纳米颗粒混合间隔层替代晶格失配的AFI，在保持YIG相干外延的同时实现反平行磁构型，并获得43%的磁子结比（MJR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FI/AFI/FI型高质量磁子结有望实现接近100%的MJR，是高性能非易失存储器的候选；但此前YIG基磁子结中AFI间隔层与YIG晶格失配会破坏外延关系并降低顶层YIG晶体质量，限制磁子传输效率。

Method: 构建含Gd3Ga5O12（GGG）/Pt纳米颗粒混合间隔层的外延磁子结，使两层YIG保持相干外延并形成反平行磁化配置；通过矫顽力调控顶层YIG，并与NiO基磁子结进行对比。

Result: 该结构中顶层YIG晶体质量更优、磁阻尼更低；观察到清晰的磁子结效应，MJR达到43%；两层亚铁磁绝缘体间的层间耦合可增强MJR。

Conclusion: 结果表明，保持相干外延并调控层间耦合可提升磁子结性能，为先进磁子自旋电子器件发展提供了重要思路。

Abstract: The excitation, propagation, manipulation, and detection of magnons lie at the core of magnonics. Among various device architectures, high-quality magnon junctions with a ferromagnetic insulator (FI)/antiferromagnetic insulator (AFI)/ferromagnetic insulator (FI) configuration are predicted to exhibit a near-100% magnon junction ratio (MJR), making them promising candidates for high-performance non-volatile memory applications. Yttrium iron garnet (Y3Fe5O12, YIG) is widely employed as the FI material in such structures for its exceptionally low magnetic damping. However, in previously reported magnon junctions, the epitaxial relationship between the two YIG layers is often disrupted by lattice-mismatched AFI spacers. Although this leads to distinct coercivities, it simultaneously degrades the crystalline quality of the top YIG layer, thereby limiting the magnontransmission efficiency. Here, an epitaxial magnon junction is demonstrated incorporating a hybrid Gd3Ga5O12 (GGG)/Pt-nanoparticles spacer that maintains coherent epitaxy while enabling an antiparallel magnetic configuration. Compared with nickel oxide-based magnon junctions, the coercivity-tuned top YIG layer in our structure exhibits superior crystalline quality and much lower magnetic damping. A clear magnon junction effect is observed with a 43% MJR achieved. The interlayer coupling between the two ferrimagnetic insulators is found to enhance the MJR. These results offer valuable insights for the development of advanced magnon-based spintronic devices.

</details>


### [125] [Ammonium thiocyanate enables in-situ structural templating of MAPbI3 via quasi-2D perovskite scaffold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39887)
*Sophie Tucker,Isaac Metcalf,Rachel C. Kilbride,Elena J. Cassella,Jessica H. Persaud,Faiz Mandani,Niranjana Raj,Max Allen,Charlie H. G. Nicholls,David G. Lidzey,Alice L. B. Pyne,Robert D. J. Oliver,Silvia Motti,Jacky Even,Aditya D. Mohite,Alexandra J. Ramadan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: NH4SCN并非通过垂直取向2D钙钛矿层进行电荷传输来提升准2D钙钛矿太阳能电池性能，而是通过动力学调控准2D相形成并利用晶格匹配诱导高质量(110)取向3D MAPbI3的原位模板生长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NH4SCN已被广泛用于改善准2D钙钛矿太阳能电池的提取与器件性能，但其对钙钛矿结晶的具体作用机制尚不清楚。

Method: 结合光谱与结构研究，分析NH4SCN对淮2D钙钛矿相形成、3D相生长及晶格匹配关系的影响。

Result: NH4SCN使淮2D钙钛矿相能够对主导的3D相进行结构模板化；其作用来自改善3D钙钛矿结构，而非通过垂直取向2D层进行电荷传输。该添加剂提供对准2D相形成的动力学控制，抑制不利的n=1相，并通过延长成核窗口促进高n 2D支架相生长，从而促进MAPbI3的原位模板生长。结构分析显示形成高度(110)有序的3D钙钛矿，且该机制由该相与淮2D相之间的紧密晶格匹配所证实。此外，该结构调控具有阳离子依赖性，需要淮2D与3D钙钛矿相之间紧密晶格匹配并成功实现相关淮2D相的动力学控制。

Conclusion: NH4SCN的益处源于其对淮2D相形成的动力学调控及晶格匹配驱动的3D钙钛矿结构模板生长，而非垂直2D层电荷传输；该机制依赖阳离子并强调淮2D/3D晶格匹配与动力学控制的协同作用。

Abstract: Ammonium thiocyanate (NH4SCN) has been used extensively to improve the current extraction and device performance of quasi-two-dimensional perovskite solar cells. Whilst this additive has been shown to influence quasi-2D perovskite crystallisation, a detailed understanding of its mechanism has not yet been established. Through spectroscopic and structural studies, we demonstrate that NH4SCN enables quasi-2D perovskite phases to structurally template the dominant 3D phase. Contrary to previous reports, we suggest that the benefit of this additive comes from an improvement to the 3D perovskite structure, not from charges transferring through vertically oriented 2D perovskite layers. We show that this additive provides kinetic control over the quasi-2D phase formation, suppressing the unfavourable n = 1 perovskite phase and promoting the growth of high-n 2D scaffold phases by extending the nucleation window. This promotes the in-situ templated growth of MAPbI3. We provide robust structural analysis demonstrating highly (110)-ordered 3D perovskite, with the mechanism confirmed by close lattice matching between this phase and quasi-2D phases. Furthermore, we show that this structural control is cation dependent, requiring both close lattice matching between quasi-2D and 3D perovskite phases, and successful kinetic control of the relevant quasi-2D phases.

</details>


### [126] [Excitons in solid molecular hydrogen from quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39894)
*Edgar Josu\'e Landinez Borda,Ravindra Shinde,Claudia Filippi,Markus Holzmann,Saverio Moroni*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用量子蒙特卡洛研究压缩下固体内分子氢相-I的中性激发，并与BSE和TDDFT比较，重点分析多行列式展开对激子局域化和响应性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不断增加的压力下理解固体内分子氢相-I的最低中性激发及其结构/响应性质，并评估不同激发态方法在激子局域化、跃迁偶极矩和激发诱导力等方面的可靠性。

Method: 采用QMC激发态波函数，包括仅单轨道替换和包含所有单粒子-空穴跃迁的多行列式展开；考虑代表热涨落与量子涨落的多种核构型；将QMC结果与BSE和TDDFT进行比较。

Result: 多行列式展开对QMC激发能影响很小，但对激子局域化及相关量至关重要，且不受完全优化和虚时间投影影响。低密度强绝缘区，多行列式QMC的局域化程度与BSE相当甚至更强；跃迁偶极矩和激发诱导力总体与BSE一致，但在局域化显著不同的核构型上出现明显差异。TDDFT通常给出更扩展的激子和更弱的跃迁偶极矩。

Conclusion: 在低密度强绝缘极限，多行列式QMC能提供更强且与BSE可比/更优的激子局域化；随着压缩接近半导体区，多粒子-空穴跃迁重要性下降，QMC、BSE与TDDFT之间的差异减小。

Abstract: We use quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) methods to investigate the lowest neutral excitation of solid molecular hydrogen in phase-I under increasing compression, with emphasis on structural and response properties. We employ QMC wave functions for the excited state that feature only a single orbital substitution, or a multideterminant expansion including every single particle-hole transition, and compare the results with the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) and time-dependent density-functional theory (TDDFT). We examine a number of nuclear configurations representing thermal and quantum fluctuations. The effect of the multideterminant expansion is marginal on the QMC excitation energies but crucial on the exciton localization and related quantities, irrespective of full optimization and imaginary time projection. In the strong insulating regime at low density, multideterminant QMC results match and in some cases surpass the degree of localization afforded by BSE. A similar trend is observed for transition dipole moments and excitation-induced forces, with generally good agreement between QMC and BSE but marked discrepancies for nuclear configurations where localization differs significantly. TDDFT generally gives a more extended exciton with correspondingly weaker transition dipole moments. These differences decrease upon compression, as the system approaches a semiconducting regime where multiple particle-hole transitions are less important.

</details>


### [127] [Confinement Controlled Phase-Pure 2D Layered Perovskites for Band-Edge Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39974)
*Danila A. Tatarinov,Alexander Schleusener,Roman Krahne*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过Cs/丁基铵A位阳离子合成高n相纯度的二维铅溴钙钛矿微晶粉末，并利用不同n相粉末混合实现吸收与光电流响应的带边工程调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维金属卤化物钙钛矿中连接八面体层数n可调控半导体带隙，对光电子和发光应用至关重要；n相纯度决定发射线宽及能带结构对齐和载流子漏斗设计。

Method: 采用简单、可扩展的合成方法，以铯和丁基铵作为A位阳离子制备二维铅溴钙钛矿微晶粉末，获得高n相纯度样品；用X射线衍射和能量色散谱确认结构与组成，并通过混合不同n相纯粉末及压片进行光谱光电流测量。

Result: 微晶表现出陡峭吸收边和UV-blue范围内单一窄发射峰；通过受控混合n相纯粉末可调节样品光谱吸收，压片的光电流光谱响应和动态响应强烈依赖粉末组成。

Conclusion: 高n相纯度二维钙钛矿微晶粉末及其可控混合为带边工程提供了可行途径，展示了在光电器件能带结构设计和载流子调控方面的应用潜力。

Abstract: Controlling the number n of connected octahedra layers in two-dimensional metal halide perovskites enables fine control of the semiconductor band gap that is highly advantageous for optoelectronic and light emission applications. Phase purity in terms of n is crucial towards narrow emission bands and the design of band structure alignment for charge carrier funneling. We present a simple and scalable synthesis method using cesium and butylammonium as A-cations to fabricate microcrystal powders of 2D lead bromide perovskites that results in samples with very high n-phase purity. The obtained microcrystals show a sharp onset in absorbance and single narrow emission peak in UV-blue spectral range, and we confirm their structure and composition with X-ray diffraction and energy dispersive spectroscopy. We show that we can tailor the spectral absorption of microcrystals samples by controlled mixing of the n-phase pure powders, and we demonstrate this capability with spectral photocurrent measurements using pellets pressed from pure and mixed powders. Both the spectral and dynamic response of the photocurrent of the pellets depend strongly on the composition of the powders, highlighting the design potential of our approach towards band-edge engineering.

</details>


### [128] [Spin-Orbit Coupling Response Theory within the Oriented Spin Space Group Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40149)
*Yuntian Liu,Xiaobing Chen,Yutong Yu,Jes\'us Etxebarria,J. Manuel Perez-Mato,Qihang Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出基于取向自旋空间群（OSSG）与SOC张量的统一对称性框架，用于描述自旋轨道耦合（SOC）诱导的对称性破缺如何决定响应张量的允许性、最低SOC阶数及磁各向异性依赖，并应用于多铁磁电耦合与反铁磁体AHE/自旋磁化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC在凝聚态物理中广泛影响物性，而新发展的自旋空间群理论为刻画SOC相关现象的基本对称性破缺提供了新视角，但其完整潜力尚未充分挖掘；作者希望建立SOC诱导对称性破缺与物理响应及新效应之间的通用联系。

Method: 引入可显式描述SOC诱导对称性约化的取向自旋空间群（OSSG）形式；用连接实空间与自旋空间的SOC张量参数化SOC哈密顿量，并对任意物理量的响应张量按SOC分量展开，从而判定SOC诱导响应是否对称允许、出现的最低SOC阶数及其对磁各向异性的依赖。

Result: 在II型多铁中分析磁电耦合，发现不同体系的磁致电极化可源于不同阶SOC效应并呈现不同磁各向异性特征；在反铁磁体中分析反常霍尔效应与自旋磁化，表明二者在OSSG下具有不同变换性质，可使大AHE响应与弱自旋磁化共存；对MAGNDATA数据库的高通量分析识别出若干有利于该共存的对称性材料类。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了SOC诱导现象的统一对称性描述，并给出了系统识别SOC驱动功能材料的途径。

Abstract: Spin-orbit coupling (SOC) plays a pivotal role in a broad range of phenomena in condensed matter physics. The recently developed spin space group theory offers a new perspective for characterizing the fundamental symmetry-breaking features of SOC-related phenomena, yet its full potential remains to be explored. Here, we provide a general framework that relates the SOC-induced symmetry breaking to modifications of physical properties and emergence of new physical effects. This approach incorporates the recently developed oriented spin space group (OSSG) formalism, which can explicitly describe the symmetry reduction induced by SOC. By parameterizing the SOC Hamiltonian using a SOC tensor that connects real and spin spaces, an expansion in terms of the SOC components can be constructed for the response tensor describing any physical quantity. This framework determines not only whether a SOC-induced response is symmetry allowed, but also the lowest order in SOC at which it emerges and its dependence on magnetic anisotropy. As representative applications, we analyze the magnetoelectric coupling in Type-II multiferroics and show that magnetically induced electric polarization in different systems can be the result of SOC effects of different orders, and can exhibit varying characteristics of magnetic anisotropy. We further apply this theory to the anomalous Hall effect and spin magnetization in antiferromagnets, demonstrating that their distinct transformation properties under the OSSG allow a large anomalous Hall response to coexist with weak spin magnetization. A high-throughput analysis of materials in the MAGNDATA database identifies several symmetry-based material classes favorable for this coexistence. Overall, the proposed framework provides a unified symmetry description of SOC-induced phenomena and offers a systematic way for the identification of SOC-driven functional materials.

</details>


### [129] [Cubic-Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te optical modes across the phonon and phonon-polariton regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40157)
*M. Mina,A. Elmahjoubi,T. Ngulezhu,S. Thiam,A. V. Postnikov,T. Tite,P. Bourges,F. Bourdarot,N. Biniskos,A. Polian,Y. Le Godec,N. Guignot,J. -P. Itie,S. Diliberto,S. Michel,A. Maillard,A. En Naciri,L. Broch,P. Franchetti,K. Strzalkowski,O. Pages*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文以近乎晶格匹配且抗晶格/键畸变能力强的立方 Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te 为基准，研究合金化引起的极小局域畸变如何体现在晶格振动中，并检验渗流模型对局域键环境的解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合金化可调控半导体物理性质，但会引入局部晶格和键畸变；Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te 因接近晶格匹配且键合高度共价、对畸变不敏感，适合作为测试晶格振动对最小合金化畸变敏感性的基准体系。

Method: 结合前向拉曼散射探测准无限波长、平均合金无序的声子-极化激元区，以及非弹性中子散射探测接近晶格常数、深入合金无序的纯机械声子区，并辅以从头算计算，研究立方 Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te（x<0.7）的两个横光学声子区。

Result: 发现短 Zn-Te 键表现出紧凑的光学双模，可区分同质与异质局域键环境，即“1 键:2 模”，可由渗流模型解释；这与历史上基于经典拉曼和红外的 MREI 方案中“1 键:1 模”、光学模式对键环境不敏感的情形不同。

Conclusion: 该测试结果为阳性，表明渗流方案能通过晶格动力学稳健解读合金化诱导的甚至很小的晶格畸变，并强化其作为局域键环境解析框架的地位。

Abstract: Alloying offers a convenient route to tune the physical properties of semiconductors, at the price, however, of local lattice/bond distortions needed to accommodate the lattice mismatch. Being nearly lattice-matched and moreover highly resistant to lattice and bond distortions thanks to a largely covalent bonding, cubic Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te sets a benchmark to test the sensitivity of lattice vibrations to minimum lattice and bond distortions induced by alloying. We explore experimentally the two transverse optical regimes of cubic Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te (x<0.7), with ab initio calculations in support: (1) the phonon-polariton regime at quasi-infinite (photon-like) wavelengths averaging over the alloy disorder, probed by forward-Raman scattering, and (2) the purely-mechanical phonon regime down to minimum wavelengths nearly matching the lattice constant zooming into the alloy disorder, achieved by inelastic neutron scattering. The short Zn-Te bond is found to exhibit a compact optical duo distinguishing homo from hetero local bond environments (1 bond: 2 mode), as explained by the percolation model. This deviates from the historical Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te MREI (modified-random-element-isodisplacement) scheme inspired from classical Raman and infrared studies, in which the optical modes are blind to the bond environment (1-bond: 1-mode). So, the test is positive, reinforcing the status of the percolation scheme as a robust decryptor of (even small) lattice distortions induced by alloying through the lattice dynamics.

</details>


### [130] [N\'eel vector switching by magnetization-dependent layer-resolved spin-orbit torque](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40215)
*Sheng Pan,Weiwei Wang,Jie-Xiang Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 第一性原理计算表明，反铁磁拓扑绝缘体 MnBi2Te4 的层分辨自旋霍尔电导由磁化无关的自旋霍尔项与两个自旋反常霍尔项组成，可在有限 septuple-layer 薄膜中实现 Néel 矢量的可逆、无场翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用层分辨自旋轨道矩和局域自旋-电荷转换控制反铁磁序，并厘清局域反演不对称、表面/内层差异及自旋反常霍尔贡献对电流驱动切换的作用。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算层分辨自旋霍尔电导，分解磁化无关自旋霍尔项与两个自旋反常霍尔项，并分析有限 septuple-layer 薄膜中 Néel 矢量的场致/电流驱动切换行为。

Result: 自旋霍尔电导包含磁化无关项和两个对 Néel 矢量偶宇称、但随局域磁序演化的自旋反常霍尔项；局域反演不对称区分表面与内层。磁化无关自旋霍尔项单独即可反转反铁磁序，而自旋反常霍尔项提供磁化依赖反馈，重分布层分辨力矩并重塑切换轨迹。

Conclusion: 层与界面工程可调控自旋反常霍尔反馈及层分辨自旋轨道矩，从而定制电流驱动的反铁磁切换并实现 Néel 矢量的无场可逆操控。

Abstract: Layer-resolved spin-orbit torques offer a route to controlling antiferromagnetic order through local spin-charge conversion. First-principles calculations show that the layer-resolved spin Hall conductivity of the antiferromagnetic topological insulator $\mathrm{Mn{Bi}_{2}{Te}_{4}}$ consists of a magnetization-independent spin Hall term plus two spin anomalous Hall terms that are even in the N\'eel vector yet evolve with the local magnetic order, while local inversion asymmetry differentiates surface and inner layers. This response enables reversible, field-free switching of the N\'eel vector in finite-septuple-layer films. Although the magnetization-independent spin Hall term alone can reverse the order, the spin anomalous Hall terms provide magnetization-dependent feedback that redistributes the layer-resolved torques and reshapes the switching trajectory, offering a route to tailoring current-driven switching through layer and interface engineering.

</details>


### [131] [Large Magnetocaloric Effect and Adiabatic Temperature Change in the Tellurate: Gd2TeO6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40296)
*Abhijit Nayak (Department of Physics,National Institute of Technology,Andhra Pradesh,Tadepalligudem,India),Gurunathan Angusamy (National Institute of Chemical Physics and Biophysics,Tallinn,Estonia),Kusampal Yadav (School of Physical Sciences,Indian Association for the Cultivation of Science,Kolkata,West Bengal,India),S. K. Panda (Department of Physics,Bennet University,Greater Noida,Uttar Pradesh,India),Devajyoti Mukherjee (School of Physical Sciences,Indian Association for the Cultivation of Science,Kolkata,West Bengal,India),Raivo Stern (National Institute of Chemical Physics and Biophysics,Tallinn,Estonia),Tanmoy Chakrabarty (Division of Sciences,Krea University,Sri City,Andhra Pradesh,India),Krishnamurthy Jyothinagaram (Department of Physics,National Institute of Technology,Andhra Pradesh,Tadepalligudem,India)*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究了多晶稀土碲酸盐Gd2TeO6的结构、磁性、热力学与磁热效应，发现其具有弱反铁磁相互作用、无长程磁有序以及很大的低温磁熵变，是有前景的深冷磁制冷候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gd2TeO6作为一种尚未用于低温磁制冷研究的稀土碲酸盐，需要评估其结构稳定性、磁性基态和磁热性能，以拓展深冷磁制冷材料体系。

Method: 采用粉末X射线衍射确定晶体结构，结合磁化测量、热容测量、Debye-Einstein分析分离晶格与磁贡献，并使用GGA+U第一性原理计算分析竞争性反铁磁态。

Result: Gd2TeO6为单相正交结构（P212121），含两个晶体学不同的七配位Gd位点；2 K以上无常规长程磁有序，约4 K以下出现低场磁不可逆性，Weiss温度为-1.25 K，7 T下磁化达理论饱和磁矩约97%且滞后可忽略。2.5 K时，0-2、0-5、0-7 T的-ΔSM分别为21.40、48.51、54.81 J kg-1 K-1，体积熵变达400.50 mJ cm-3 K-1；14 T下-ΔSM为63.51 J kg-1 K-1，接近理论极限64.25 J kg-1 K-1，0-7 T绝热温变约22.7 K。

Conclusion: Gd2TeO6具有弱主导反铁磁相互作用、无长程有序及接近理论极限的低温磁熵变，是深冷磁制冷应用的 promising candidate。

Abstract: We investigate the structural, magnetic, thermodynamic, and magnetocaloric properties of polycrystalline Gd2TeO6, a rare-earth tellurate unexplored for cryogenic magnetic refrigeration. Powder X-ray diffraction confirms single-phase orthorhombic symmetry (P212121), featuring two crystallographically distinct seven-coordinate Gd sites. Magnetization measurements reveal no conventional long-range magnetic ordering down to 2 K, although low-field magnetic irreversibility emerges below approximately 4 K. Curie-Weiss analysis yields a Weiss temperature of -1.25 K, indicating weak predominantly antiferromagnetic interactions. The magnetization reaches approximately 97% of the theoretical saturation moment at 7 T with negligible hysteresis. Heat-capacity measurements reveal no sharp zero-field anomaly above 2 K, while a broad field-induced maximum indicates redistribution of low-energy magnetic entropy. Debye-Einstein analysis separates the lattice and magnetic contributions. At 2.5 K, the maximum magnetic entropy changes (-DeltaSM) reach 21.40, 48.51, and 54.81 J kg-1 K-1 for field changes of 0-2, 0-5, and 0-7 T, respectively. The corresponding volumetric entropy change reaches 400.50 mJ cm-3 K-1. Measurements up to 14 T yield (-DeltaSM) = 63.51 J kg-1 K-1, approaching the theoretical limit of 64.25 J kg-1 K-1. The estimated adiabatic temperature change is approximately 22.7 K for 0-7 T. First-principles calculations with GGA+U approximation reveal competing antiferromagnetic states, highlighting Gd2TeO6 as a promising candidate for cryogenic magnetocaloric applications.

</details>


### [132] [Soliton-Lattice Origin of Incommensurability in High-Pressure Antimony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40399)
*M. Smirman,K. Sun,H. Sheng,I. I. Mazin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出高压主客体相 Sb-II 是一种孤子晶格：局域的主客体失配核心修饰了 4/3 公度参考结构，从而解释其不可公度性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主客体晶体中不可公度性的微观起源尚未解决，作者旨在揭示高压 Sb-II 相中该现象的机制。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算、Frenkel-Kontorova 模型、机器学习势计算和有限温度分析，研究 (4n+1)/(3n+1) 型孤子晶格近似结构，并与实验衍射结果对照。

Result: 计算揭示起伏且具滞回的配准能量景观；孤子晶格近似存在能量优选的孤子间距，其局域失配核心经第一性原理弛豫验证；有限温度下热展宽的孤子晶格沿客体链表现为原子密度波，与实验衍射一致。

Conclusion: Sb-II 的不可公度性可由孤子配准图像从微观上解释，即局域失配孤子修饰公度参考结构形成孤子晶格。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of incommensurability in host-guest crystals remains unresolved. Here we show that the high-pressure antimony host-guest phase Sb-II is a soliton lattice, where localized guest-host misregistries decorate a
  commensurate 4/3 reference structure. First-principles calculations reveal a corrugated and hysteretic registry landscape, motivating a Frenkel-Kontorova description that accounts for soliton-lattice approximants of the form
  $(4n+1)/(3n+1)$. Machine-learning-potential calculations of these approximants show an energetically preferred soliton spacing, and their localized misregistry cores are also verified by first-principles relaxations. At finite
  temperature, the thermally broadened soliton lattice appears as an atomic density wave along the guest chains, consistent with experimental diffraction. This soliton-registry picture provides a microscopic mechanism for host-guest
  incommensurability in Sb-II.

</details>


### [133] [Light-induced chiral altermagnets from conventional antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40410)
*Jorge Cardenas-Gamboa,Devika Jayadeep,Aritz Leonardo,Gregory A. Fiete,Jeroen van den Brink,Maia G. Vergniory*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种通过相干晶格调控（非线性声子学）光学诱导交错磁体（altermagnet）的对称性引导方案，并在Fe$_2$TeO$_6$中实现光致瞬态交错磁态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体兼具补偿共线磁序与动量依赖的非相对论自旋劈裂，在自旋电子学中具有潜力；但如何可逆、超快地光学诱导传统反铁磁体进入交错磁态仍是关键问题。

Method: 通过对称性分析筛选能解除传统反铁磁体自旋简并、同时保留连接相反自旋子晶格旋转对称性的声子畸变；以几何手性声子为途径，并以Fe$_2$TeO$_6$为原型，利用线性偏振中红外脉冲通过红外活性声子与光学沉默对称破缺模式之间的负双二次耦合驱动结构失稳，结合第一性原理晶格动力学与电子结构计算验证。

Result: 晶格动力学揭示光致瞬态手性畸变的产生；电子结构计算表明该光诱导结构具有交错磁体特征的非相对论自旋劈裂。

Conclusion: 结果建立了通过非线性声子学超快光学控制交错磁态的对称性引导路径，并将交错磁性与结构手性联系起来。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine compensated collinear magnetic order with momentum-dependent nonrelativistic spin splitting, offering new opportunities for spintronic applications. Here, we propose a route to optically induce altermagnetism through coherent lattice control. Using symmetry analysis, we identify phonon distortions that lift the spin degeneracy of conventional antiferromagnets while preserving the rotational symmetries connecting opposite-spin sublattices. Among these, geometric chiral phonons provide a particularly appealing pathway, linking the emergence of altermagnetism to structural chirality. Taking Fe$_2$TeO$_6$ as a prototype, we demonstrate that a linearly polarized mid-infrared pulse can drive a structural instability through negative biquadratic coupling between an infrared-active phonon and an optically silent, symmetry-breaking mode. First-principles-based lattice dynamics reveal the emergence of a transient chiral distortion, while electronic-structure calculations establish that the light-induced structure exhibits nonrelativistic spin splitting characteristic of altermagnetism. Our results establish a symmetry-guided pathway toward the ultrafast optical control of altermagnetic states through nonlinear phononics.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Evidence for a charged vortex lattice in an exciton superfluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37781)
*Ryan L. Lee,Jungwoo Lee,Viliam Va\v{n}o,Bo Zou,Cheng-Li Chiu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Allan MacDonald,Ali Yazdani*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文报道在双层激子凝聚体中利用扫描隧道显微镜观测到由带电涡旋组成的涡旋晶格，为磁场下弱带电激子超流体的理论预言提供了实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子由电子-空穴束缚而成且电中性，因此在激子超流体中实现类似旋转超流体或II型超导体的量子化涡旋晶格长期具有挑战性；近期理论预言强磁场下弱带电激子超流体可因分数电荷涡旋核心相互排斥而稳定形成涡旋-反涡旋晶格。

Method: 在垂直磁场下，以薄六方氮化硼隔离的两层石墨烯的朗道能级之间形成双层激子凝聚体，并用扫描隧道显微镜对其带电涡旋进行成像。

Result: 观测到的涡旋晶格周期随总过剩电荷变化，且对电荷在两层间的分配不敏感，与理论预测量化一致。

Conclusion: 该观测支持磁场下激子超流体中预言涡旋晶格的存在，并为双层激子超流体的相位相干性提供了证据。

Abstract: Lattices of quantized vortices are a defining property of rotating superfluids and of type-II superconductors in magnetic fields. Realizing an analogous vortex lattice in an exciton superfluid-formed from bound electron-hole pairs-has remained challenging because excitons are electrically neutral. Building on previous theoretical studies of the low-energy excitations in an exciton superfluid, recent theory has predicted that in a strong magnetic field, a weakly charged exciton superfluid can stabilize a vortex-antivortex lattice arising from the mutual repulsion of fractionally charged vortex cores. Here we report evidence for this predicted vortex lattice in a bilayer exciton condensate by imaging its charged vortices with the scanning tunnelling microscope. The condensate forms between Landau levels of two graphene layers separated by a thin hexagonal boron nitride barrier in a perpendicular magnetic field. We find that the vortex lattice periodicity scales with the total excess charge and is insensitive to its distribution between the two layers, in quantitative agreement with theoretical predictions. Our observations provide evidence for phase coherence in an exciton superfluid system.

</details>


### [135] [Gravitational anomaly spectroscopy of a Kitaev spin liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38704)
*Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出手征中心荷 c 可由边缘光谱学直接提取：沿边缘施加弱周期性调制，其吸收权重按 Virasoro 代数标度；归一化后组合 C=2D_2-4D_1 在理想边缘任意态中等于 c。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测量 c 依赖热输运，但声子污染信号；波函数探针则要求纯基态，实验与数值上都受限。作者希望找到不依赖纯态、可直接用于边缘谱的 c 测量方案。

Method: 对边缘耦合施加沿边缘传播的弱调制，并用以同一耦合静态变化响应归一化吸收权重 D_n；利用 D_n=2n⟨L_0⟩+(c/12)(n^3-n)，构造 C≡2D_2-4D_1=c。进一步用有无体 vison 的线权重比 Λ=4h_σ/c 无标定地提取非阿贝尔 Ising 任意子的共形自旋 h_σ。数值上结合 Kitaev 蜂窝自旋液体、DMRG 处理固定磁通扇区中的相互作用边缘 Majorana，以及含原生 Floquet 电路的量子处理器协议精确模拟。

Result: 在 7168 个自旋的 Kitaev 蜂窝模型中得 C=0.49999、h_σ=0.06250；用测得谱线自身位移标定时，96 个自旋即可将 c 测到约 1% 误差，并在 20% 随机耦合误差下仍保持约 1%。DMRG 中相互作用边缘 Majorana 使 C 保持在 1.5% 内，而边缘速度变化达 ±13%。量子处理器协议模拟在 96 至 600 个自旋上给出 c 的误差在 3.5% 内。

Conclusion: 中心荷 c 可作为一种边缘光谱量，通过跃迁权重而非激发能隙体现；该方案对热态、相互作用和耦合无序具有鲁棒性，并能无标定地给出非阿贝尔任意子的共形自旋，适用于自旋液体与量子处理器实验。

Abstract: The chiral central charge $c$ measures the gravitational anomaly of a two-dimensional topological phase. It fixes the heat current carried along the edge. So far, it has been accessed through thermal transport, where phonons complicate the signal, or through wave-function probes that need a pure ground state. Here we show that $c$ is a spectroscopic quantity of the edge. A weak modulation of the edge couplings that travel along the edge is absorbed according to the Virasoro algebra. Normalized by the response to a static change of the same couplings, the absorbed weights obey $D_n=2n\langle L_0\rangle+\frac{c}{12}(n^3-n)$, so that $\mathcal{C}\equiv2D_2-4D_1=c$ in any state of the ideal edge theory, thermal or not. The central charge thus appears in transition weights, although it cancels from excitation gaps. The ratio of line weights with and without a vison in the bulk, $\Lambda=4h_\sigma/c$, needs no calibration and fixes the conformal spin $h_\sigma$ of the non-Abelian Ising anyon. For the Kitaev honeycomb spin liquid we find $\mathcal{C}=0.49999$ and $h_\sigma=0.06250$ on 7168 spins. Calibrating with the shift of the measured line itself gives $c$ within about $1\%$ on 96 spins and within $1\%$ under $20\%$ random coupling errors. Interacting edge Majoranas in a fixed flux sector, treated with DMRG, keep $\mathcal{C}$ within $1.5\%$ while the edge velocity changes by $\pm13\%$. An exact simulation of a quantum-processor protocol, including the native Floquet circuit, returns $c$ within $3.5\%$ on 96 to 600 spins.

</details>


### [136] [Topological Quantum Spin Liquids in a Ladder with Majorana fermions and p-wave Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40297)
*Magali Korolev,Fan Yang,Erik S. S{\o}rensen,Karyn Le Hur*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过将二维Kitaev蜂窝自旋模型推广到梯子/带状几何，构造精确可解模型，系统分类全相图中的拓扑量子自旋液体、Majorana金属相及其体边对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在整个相图中仅具有短程自旋关联的拓扑量子自旋液体，并寻找在方格子几何上实现含π通量的拓扑量子自旋模型以及对应拓扑超导模型的可能性。

Method: 构造精确可解的Kitaev蜂窝模型带状推广；用Majorana费米子描述静态涌现的Z2规范场及0或π通量；结合量子场论、数学分析和密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG），并通过观测量进行几何、拓扑和物理分类及体边对应分析。

Result: 全相图包含三个有自旋能隙的自旋液体相和一个Majorana费米子意义上的金属相；有能隙相边缘存在受保护的零能Majorana费米子，拓扑液相体内存在巡游Majorana费米子；并区分了brickwall梯子产生的特殊线与square梯子允许的无能隙Majorana液相。

Conclusion: 该模型为在方格子几何上寻找含π通量的拓扑量子自旋模型提供了希望，并可建立与拓扑超导模型之间的对应关系。

Abstract: In this article, we address the possibility of topological quantum spin liquids with only short-range (spin) correlations in the whole phase diagram in a ladder system, through an exactly solvable model which represents a generalization of the two-dimensional Kitaev honeycomb spin model to a ribbon geometry. This system gives rise to $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge fields emerging in a static way in the ground state through Majorana fermions which then produce either $0$ or $\pi$ flux. Through a quantum field theory perspective, mathematical and Density Matrix Renormalization Group results, we present our progress on the geometrical, topological and physical classification of this model through observables navigating on the whole phase diagram presenting three spin-liquid phases with a spin gap and a metallic phase in the sense of Majorana fermions. We propose a bulk-edge correspondence revealing the presence of protected zero-energy Majorana fermions at the edge in the phases with a gap and revealing also the presence of itinerant Majorana fermions in the bulk in the topological liquid phase. The proposed approach allows us to distinguish between properties of the brickwall ladder giving rise to a special line and those of the square ladder allowing for the gapless Majorana liquid phase. This model gives some hope on finding solutions for topological quantum spin models, e.g. with $\pi$ flux, on a square lattice geometry. This approach also allows for a correspondence onto models of topological superconductivity.

</details>


### [137] [Variational wavefunctions for ideal anyon gas: superfluid and skyrmion Hall crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40657)
*Ya-Hui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用严格嵌入任意子统计的变分波函数（复合玻色子/复合费米子/parton 结合球面神经网络关联）研究理想任意子气体基态，并在 SU(3) 味情形提出天纹霍尔晶体与手征色超导体两种候选；后者在扭曲 MoTe2 中可解释重入整数量子反常霍尔效应与超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想任意子气体在统计角 θ∈(0,π) 时的基态长期未解，分数陈绝缘体的新观测重新激发兴趣；需要能精确处理任意子统计并比较液体、晶体及拓扑有序态的变分方法。

Method: 构造精确满足任意子统计的变分波函数，将理论引导的复合玻色子、复合费米子和 parton 波函数与球面上的神经网络关联相结合；对无自旋、SU(2) semion 及 θ=π/3 的无自旋和 SU(3) 味任意子比较液体与晶体能量，并分析候选态的规范结构与配对形式。

Result: 无自旋和 SU(2) semion 中确认配对任意子超流，能量显著低于最佳晶体态；θ=π/3 无自旋情形液体与晶体能量接近。θ=π/3 且 SU(3) 味时得到两种候选：任意子在自旋织构诱导有效磁场中形成整数量子霍尔态的天纹霍尔晶体，以及由六任意子团簇组成的手征色超导体；后者波函数为超行列式而非 parton 行列式乘积，并在所用变分拟设中能量优于晶体及 U(1)^2、Z_3 规范群的色味锁定相。

Conclusion: 在扭曲 MoTe2 中，重入整数量子反常霍尔相可对应掺杂任意子的天纹霍尔晶体，观测到的超导可对应手征色超导体；该色超导体可转变为 f+if BCS 超导体，并使手征中心电荷跃变 3。

Abstract: The ground state of an ideal gas of anyons with statistical angle $\theta\in(0,\pi)$ is a long-standing open problem, with renewed interest from the recent observations of fractional Chern insulators. In this work we construct variational wavefunctions that build in the exact anyon statistics and combine theory-guided composite-boson, composite-fermion and parton wavefunctions with neural-network correlations on the sphere. For spinless and SU(2) semions we confirm the paired anyon superfluid, well below the best crystal states. At $\theta=\pi/3$ liquids and crystals are closer in energy for the spinless case. For $\theta=\pi/3$ anyons with SU(3) flavor, we find two candidates. One is a skyrmion Hall crystal, in which the anyons form an integer quantum Hall state in the effective magnetic field from the spin texture. The other is a chiral color superconductor of six-anyon clusters, the color-flavor-locked pairing descendant of a holon metal with SU(3) color gauge symmetry and SU(3) flavor symmetry; its wavefunction is a hyperdeterminant rather than a product of parton determinants. Within our variational ansatz the color superconductor has better energy than both crystals and the color-flavor locked phases with U(1)$^2$ and Z$_3$ gauge groups. For twisted MoTe$_2$ this suggests that the reentrant integer quantum anomalous Hall phase is the skyrmion Hall crystal of the doped anyons, and that the observed superconductivity may be the color superconductor, which can undergo a transition to an $f+if$ BCS superconductor with a jump of the chiral central charge by 3.

</details>


### [138] [Meta-Altermagnetism and Enhanced Spin-Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37754)
*Bogeng Wen,Hae-Young Kee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出并论证二维交变磁体可在自旋劈裂范霍夫奇点穿越费米面时发生一级“元交变磁”跃迁，形成交错磁化减弱但磁八极矩与自旋劈裂体电导增强的中间交变磁相，并给出含量子临界端点的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索交变磁性是否存在不连续跳变（元交变磁性），并寻找增强自旋劈裂效应以服务自旋电子学的机制。

Method: 在包含各向异性跃迁积分的两子晶格方晶格上，对扩展t-J模型采用平均场理论，调节化学势和各向异性跃迁强度。

Result: 发现磁序诱导的能带重构使自旋劈裂范霍夫奇点穿越费米面，从而驱动一级元交变磁转变；中间交变磁相具有较低交错磁化，但磁八极矩和时间反演奇的自旋劈裂体电导显著增强；一级转变线终止于量子临界端点，形成丰富相图。

Conclusion: 自旋劈裂范霍夫奇点是增强自旋劈裂效应、推动自旋电子学应用的一条途径。

Abstract: Altermagnets are collinear magnets with zero net magnetization and spin-split electronic bands. Here, we explore whether altermagnetism can exhibit a discontinuous jump, a phenomenon we refer to as meta-altermagnetism. We show that meta-altermagnetism can emerge in 2D systems when magnetic-order-induced band reconstruction drives spin-split van Hove singularities (vHSs) across the Fermi level. Using mean-field theory for an extended $t$-$J$ model on a two-sublattice square lattice including anisotropic hopping integral, we find that a first-order meta-altermagnetic transition produces an intermediate altermagnetic regime with reduced staggered magnetization but strongly enhanced magnetic octupole and time-reversal-odd spin-splitter conductivity. We further show that the first-order transition line terminates at a quantum critical endpoint, giving rise to a rich phase diagram as the chemical potential and anisotropic hopping strength are varied. Our results identify the spin split vHSs as a route to enhancing spin-splitter effect for spintronic applications.

</details>


### [139] [From a Lifshitz Transition to Spin-Density-Wave Order: Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ under Uniaxial Strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38025)
*Evgeny A. Stepanov,Sophie Beck,Maria Chatzieleftheriou,Antoine Georges*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用先进多体理论框架研究单轴应变下Sr2RuO4的谱学与磁性，揭示各向异性Lifshitz转变及由此驱动的磁不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现单轴应变显著提高Sr2RuO4的超导临界温度，并伴随涌现的磁有序迹象，亟需理论解释应变如何同时影响其谱学性质和磁性。

Method: 在先进的多体理论框架中引入单轴应变，计算应变对Sr2RuO4费米面、电子散射率和磁响应的影响。

Result: 应变诱导各向异性Lifshitz转变：沿伸长方向电子散射率增强，而垂直方向趋向相干费米液体行为，与实验一致；该各向异性可捕获大应变下有限温度磁有序的不稳定性。磁响应由两个竞争波矢主导，分别对应γ片层内嵌套及α-β片层间嵌套，其竞争敏感依赖于相应费米面片层的相干性。

Conclusion: 单轴应变通过各向异性费米面重构和片层相干性调控Sr2RuO4的磁不稳定性，为实验中应变增强超导与涌现磁有序提供了统一的理论解释。

Abstract: In recent years, the effects of uniaxial strain on Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ have been extensively investigated, revealing a pronounced enhancement of the superconducting critical temperature accompanied by experimental indications of an emergent magnetically ordered state. In this Letter, we study the impact of strain on the spectral and magnetic properties of this material within an advanced many-body theoretical framework. We find that strain induces an anisotropic Lifshitz transition, which enhances the electron scattering rate along the elongation axis while simultaneously promoting coherent Fermi-liquid behavior in the perpendicular direction, in close agreement with experimental observations. Taking into account this anisotropic behavior allows us to capture the magnetic instability leading to a finite-temperature ordered state at large strain values. Our analysis further reveals that the magnetic response is dominated by two competing wave vectors associated with distinct nesting vectors: one within the $\gamma$ sheet and the other between the $\alpha$ and $\beta$ sheets of the Fermi surface. Both wave vectors can stabilize magnetic order, with their competition depending sensitively on the coherence of the respective Fermi surface sheets.

</details>


### [140] [Magnetism in a homometallic square-planar Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$$_4$Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$ metallacrown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38989)
*Luca Bischof,Ariyan Tavakoli,Sascha Pietruska,Lena Spillecke,Ina Heckelmann,Changhyun Koo,Luca M. Carrella,Eva Rentschler,Hans Christian Schneider,R\"udiger Klingeler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过在salicylhydroxamate配体上引入位阻大取代基，合成了抑制分子间磁相互作用的Cu(II)[12-MC_Cu(II)N(L)-4]^{2-}（Cu4Cu）金属冠醚，并系统解析其内禀基态自旋结构与交换相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典同金属Cu(II)[12-MC_Cu(II)N(Shi)-4]^{2-}是研究交换耦合Cu(II)簇磁性的理想平台，但分子间磁相互作用会干扰本征磁性测量，因此需要位阻修饰以隔离分子并深入表征其基态。

Method: 合成位阻修饰的Cu4Cu衍生物；采用静态磁化测量提取交换耦合常数，并结合基于破缺对称性的从头算DFT进行验证；利用X波段及高频/高场EPR确定g因子和激发态信号。

Result: 得到中心-外围交换J1=-8.1(2) THz（-33.5(8) meV）及环上近邻交换J2=-4.40(12) THz（-18.2(5) meV），均为竞争性反铁磁耦合；低温基态为S=1/2，EPR给出gx=2.028、gy=2.056、gz=2.222，接近轴向各向异性并与DFT一致；未占据态具dxy特征；磁化数据还显示双重态到四重态的能隙Δ_{1/2→3/2}=4.8(4) THz，并有激发态EPR信号佐证。

Conclusion: 位阻修饰有效抑制分子间磁性并揭示Cu4Cu的本征交换耦合、S=1/2基态、近轴向g张量及双重态-四重态激发能隙，实验与ab initio计算相互印证。

Abstract: The well-established homometallic metallacrown Cu(II)[12-MC$_\text{Cu(II)N(Shi)}$-4]$^{2-}$, comprising four square-planarly arranged Cu(II) ions surrounding a slightly off-plane central Cu(II) ion, provides an excellent platform for investigating magnetism in exchange-coupled Cu(II) clusters both by experimental and ab initio numerical methods. In the present work, sterically bulky substituents are introduced into the salicylhydroxamate ligand to obtain a modified derivative, Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$[12-MC$_\text{Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$N(L)}$-4]$^{2-}$ (Cu$_4$Cu), thereby effectively suppressing intermolecular magnetic interactions and enabling a detailed investigation of its intrinsic ground-state spin structure and exchange interactions. Static magnetization reveals competing antiferromagnetic exchange interactions of $J_1=-8.1(2)$ THz ($-33.5(8)$ meV) between the central and the peripheral sites and $J_2=-4.40(12)$ THz ($-18.2(5)$ meV) between neighboring sites on the ring of the metallacrown, which are corroborated by ab initio broken symmetry-based density functional theory (DFT). These couplings yield an $S=1/2$ ground state for Cu$_4$Cu at low temperatures, for which X-band and high-frequency/high-field electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) precisely determine the $g$-factors $g_x = 2.028$, $g_y = 2.056$, and $g_z = 2.222$ close to axial anisotropy and in agreement with our DFT results. The ground-state spin-configuration is characterized by $d_{xy}$-character of the unoccupied states. Furthermore, our magnetization data evidence thermal transitions across a doublet-to-quartet gap of $\Delta_{1/2\rightarrow3/2} = 4.8(4)$ THz which is also indicated by the emergence of an excited-state EPR signal.

</details>


### [141] [Investigation of perovskite related oxides: structural and magnetic properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40139)
*Antara Sarkar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文关注不同结构的稀土-过渡金属钙钛矿中结构依赖的磁相互作用与磁转变，指出A/B位取代、轨道相互作用和无序会调控超交换/双交换、磁各向异性及自旋重取向等行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿因结构多样且磁性质随结构变化，在多铁、磁热和自旋电子器件中具有重要价值；需要理解稀土离子与过渡金属离子在不同结构下的相互作用机制。

Method: 摘要表明研究基于不同稀土(R)与过渡金属(TM)钙钛矿体系，关联其结构特征与磁性转变，分析轨道相互作用、无序及A/B位取代对超交换和双交换相互作用的影响；摘要未给出具体样品、实验手段或定量参数。

Result: 观察到依相互作用程度和结构性质而变的居里温度、自旋玻璃转变和自旋重取向转变；A/B位取代会改变磁相互作用并引入结构畸变；还观察到TM与R自旋相互作用、磁各向异性、轨道取向及无序诱导相互作用的影响。

Conclusion: 钙钛矿的磁性转变和交换相互作用可由结构、轨道、无序及A/B位化学取代协同调控，这为多铁、磁热和自旋电子应用中的材料设计提供了结构-磁性关联依据。

Abstract: Perovskites exist in varied structures and show structure dependent magnetic properties which is of great interest in the field of multiferroics, magnetocaloric and spintronic devices. Here we intend to study the various rare-earth(R) and transition metal (TM) ion interactions depending on their structures. Different Curie temperature, spinglass transition and spin reorientation transitions were observed depending on the degree of interactions and structural properties. It is interesting to study the role of orbital interactions, disorder in magnetic transitions leading to change in superexchange and double exchange interaction. On substitution at A-site or B-site results in change in magnetic interactions, as well as creates structural distortions. We also obtained results where the spin interaction of the transition metal and rare-earth interaction, magnetic anisotropy, orientations of orbitals, disorder induced interactions were observed.

</details>


### [142] [Static Response of the Strongly Coupled Electron Gas from Diffusion Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40093)
*Muhammed H. G\"une\c{s},Yubo Yang,Vitaly Gorelov,Miguel A. Morales,Matteo Gatti,Lucia Reining,Shiwei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用扩散蒙特卡罗计算均匀电子气在2≤r_s≤100范围内的静态密度响应函数χ(q)和局域场因子G(q)，并给出方法学要点、低密度非单调结构及其与短程有序的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零温下G(q)的可靠数据此前主要限于金属密度r_s≤10，限制了介电屏蔽、有效电子-电子相互作用及TDDFT交换关联核的精度。

Method: 采用扩散蒙特卡罗计算静态密度响应χ(q)与G(q)；系统讨论试算波函数中扰动势的优化，给出最优值闭式表达和线性响应窗口的密度依赖；检验backflow关联与系统尺寸效应。

Result: 优化扰动势对响应符号至关重要；backflow不改变χ(q)，G(q)对系统尺寸仅弱依赖，Slater-Jastrow波函数和中等系统即可。结果在金属密度q>k_F与先前DMC一致并扩展至q<k_F，在r_s=2与diagrammatic QMC一致；低密度下G(q)在中间q出现非单调结构，响应趋近非相互作用气体，并与有限温度PIMC定量一致，暗示该结构继承自基态。

Conclusion: 研究为未来QMC计算电子气和真实材料的响应性质提供实用指南，并通过能再现液相区数据的G(q)参数化，将该非单调结构与对关联函数和静态结构因子中的短程有序联系起来。

Abstract: The local field factor $G(q)$ of the HEG describes how exchange and correlation modify the response to a static perturbation, and enters dielectric screening, effective electron--electron interactions, and the xc kernel of TDDFT. Accurate zero-temperature data for $G(q)$, however, remain limited to metallic densities, $r_s\leq10$. In this work, we present diffusion Monte Carlo calculations of the static density response function $\chi(q)$ and of $G(q)$ for $2\leq r_s\leq100$, and describe and discuss the methodological steps that enabled these calculations. We show how optimization of the perturbing potential in the trial wave function has an important effect, as a poor choice can reverse the sign of the response. A closed-form expression is provided for its optimal value, together with the density dependence of the linear-response window. We further show that backflow correlations leave $\chi(q)$ unchanged and that $G(q)$ depends only weakly on system size even at very low density, so that Slater--Jastrow wave functions and modest system sizes suffice. Together, these results provide a practical guide for future QMC calculations of response properties of the electron gas and real materials. Our results agree with previous DMC data at metallic densities for $q>k_F$, and extend them below $k_F$. They also agree with diagrammatic QMC at $r_s=2$. At lower densities, our results show that $G(q)$ develops a nonmonotonic structure at intermediate-$q$, where the response approaches that of the noninteracting gas. Quantitative agreement is seen with finite-temperature path integral Monte Carlo, suggesting that the previously reported finite-temperature structure is inherited from the ground state. Using a parametrization of $G(q)$ that reproduces our data throughout the liquid phase, we relate this structure to short-range order in the pair correlation function and the static structure factor.

</details>


### [143] [Field theory of competing phases in the interacting Kitaev chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37761)
*Pradip Kattel,Thierry Giamarchi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文结合玻色化、DMRG与连续场论，给出了有限密度下相互作用Kitaev链的完整相图与量子临界性，并揭示其拓扑超导相的Majorana边缘模式及SPT--SSB对偶描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Kitaev链研究多集中于可精确求解的特殊晶格极限，缺乏对有限化学势、近邻相互作用和非微扰掺杂效应下拓扑超导与电荷有序竞争的一般性理解。

Method: 采用玻色化与密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）相结合，并发展有限密度相互作用区的连续场论描述；同时构造对偶连续费米子表示以刻画拓扑体能区的对称破缺图像。

Result: 相图包含拓扑超导相、公度电荷密度波Mott相、二者之间的非公度Luttinger液体，以及大化学势下的平庸能带绝缘相；公度--非公度边界由Pokrovsky--Talapov临界性控制，非公度--拓扑超导边界由Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless临界性控制。拓扑相在拓扑/平庸界面支持指数局域Majorana边缘模，并可在对偶表示中描述为常规对称破缺。

Conclusion: 结果提供了一维相互作用拓扑超导体的解析框架，统一描述了拓扑与公度性竞争、由此产生的量子临界性，以及拓扑相的SPT--SSB对偶图像。

Abstract: We study the interacting Kitaev chain with nearest-neighbor interactions and finite chemical potential using a combination of bosonization and density-matrix renormalization-group calculations. Focusing away from special exactly solvable lattice limits, we develop a continuum field-theoretic description of the generic finite-density interacting regime, where doping nonperturbatively modifies the competition between superconducting pairing and commensurate charge ordering. We show that the phase diagram consists of an intermediate incommensurate Luttinger liquid between the topological superconducting and commensurate charge-density-wave Mott phases, as well as a trivial band-insulating regime at large chemical potential. The commensurate--incommensurate and incommensurate--topological-superconducting phase boundaries are governed by Pokrovsky--Talapov and Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless criticality, respectively. We analyze the interacting topological superconducting regime and its boundary excitations. At the semiclassical level, and explicitly in the free-fermion boundary theory, the topological phase supports exponentially localized Majorana edge modes at interfaces between topological and trivial regions. We also construct a dual continuum fermionic representation in which this topological bulk sector is described instead by conventional symmetry breaking. This gives a continuum version of the SPT--SSB correspondence between different fermionic representations. Overall, our results provide an analytical description of one-dimensional interacting topological superconductors, from the competition between topology and commensurability and the resulting quantum criticality to the dual symmetry-breaking description of the topological phase.

</details>


### [144] [Long-Range Toric Code: Finite-Temperature Topological Order in Two Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37765)
*Jacopo Gliozzi,Taylor L. Hughes,Kyung-Su Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 作者提出在二维环面码中加入长程环相互作用，使基态仍保持原环面码的拓扑有序，同时在零温下禁闭任意子；足够长程的相互作用可将禁闭延续到有限温度，形成有限温拓扑序、自纠错量子存储与长程纠缠；较短程时仅能在指数大长度尺度上保留拓扑序特征，实现部分自纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通二维环面码的拓扑序在有限温度下易被任意子激发破坏，难以作为自纠错量子存储器，因此需要寻找能抑制任意子移动、维持有限温拓扑序的机制。

Method: 构造带有额外长程环相互作用的二维环面码模型，并分析该相互作用对任意子的禁闭效应及其随相互作用程长和温度的变化。

Result: 模型保持原环面码的拓扑有序基态并在零温禁闭任意子；对足够长程相互作用，禁闭在有限温仍存在，任意子迁移被强烈限制，产生有限温拓扑序、自纠错量子存储和长程纠缠；对较短程相互作用，拓扑序特征仅能维持到指数大的长度尺度，形成部分自纠错量子存储。

Conclusion: 长程环相互作用可为二维环面码提供有限温拓扑序和自纠错能力；相互作用程长决定其是完整还是部分自纠错，从而连接长程相互作用与有限温拓扑量子存储。 

Abstract: We introduce two-dimensional toric code models with additional long-range loop interactions. Our models preserve the topologically ordered ground states of the ordinary toric code, while confining anyons at zero temperature. For sufficiently long-ranged interactions, we argue that this confinement persists at finite temperatures, strongly constraining anyon mobility and giving rise to finite-temperature topological order. This topological phase acts as a self-correcting quantum memory and exhibits long-range entanglement. For shorter-ranged interactions, signatures of topological order survive only up to an exponentially large length scale at finite temperature, yielding a partially self-correcting quantum memory.

</details>


### [145] [Spin Space Symmetry Without Spin Space Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37770)
*Jeffrey G. Rau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出用母空间群在自旋空间中的实表示来直接刻画自旋空间群，从而把磁补偿、压磁、自旋/电荷输运、Edelstein 效应和动量空间自旋纹理等允许响应的判定，化为空间群表示论中的外幂与点群多极表示的重叠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋空间群为非相对论极限下磁结构提供完整对称性分类，并日益用于组织磁相及其物理性质；但显式构造和识别自旋空间群较繁琐，且需进一步简化其对允许响应的对称约束。

Method: 对给定母空间群的自旋空间群，用其在自旋空间中的实表示（模去仅自旋对称性）来描述；直接基于该表示做空间群表示论分析，将响应条件表述为定义表示的三个外幂与点群多极表示之间的重叠，并利用外幂的波矢内容给出选择定则。

Result: 磁补偿、压磁、自旋与电荷输运、Edelstein 效应及动量空间自旋纹理的有无均可由表示论条件判定；不同物理响应可归结为对定义表示的同一条件，例如 p 波自旋纹理与时间偶 Edelstein 效应条件相同，压磁与补偿序中的 d 波自旋纹理条件相同。外幂的波矢内容给出层级选择定则：均匀响应分别要求一、二或三个有序波矢之和为倒格矢，从而在考虑点群对称性前排除许多响应。文中以共线、共面和不可共面磁序材料为例说明。

Conclusion: 直接使用自旋空间中的实表示可避免自旋空间群的构造与识别，并系统简化允许响应的对称性约束；该方法可推广到磁空间群，与共线序的 Landau 理论相联系，并用于奇宇称磁体的刻画。

Abstract: Spin space groups provide a complete symmetry classification of magnetic structures in the non-relativistic limit and have become an increasingly useful language for organizing magnetic phases and their physical properties. Each spin space group with a given parent space group is specified, up to spin-only symmetries, by a real representation of that space group acting in spin space. We argue that working directly with this representation not only avoids the construction and identification of the group, but also organizes and simplifies the resulting symmetry constraints on allowed responses. Determining the presence or absence of magnetic compensation, piezomagnetism, spin and charge transport, the Edelstein effect, and momentum-space spin textures becomes an ordinary problem in the representation theory of space groups. The resulting conditions reduce to overlaps between the three exterior powers of the defining representation and multipole representations of the point group. Several physically distinct responses then reduce to the same condition on the defining representation. For example, a $p$-wave spin texture and the time-even Edelstein effect are allowed under the same condition, as are piezomagnetism and a $d$-wave spin texture in compensated order.
