The wave-vector content of the exterior powers gives a further hierarchy of selection rules: depending on the relevant power, a uniform response requires one, two, or three ordering wave vectors to sum to a reciprocal lattice vector. These rules can exclude many responses before point-group symmetry is considered. We illustrate the approach with examples drawn from a wide variety of materials hosting collinear, coplanar and non-coplanar magnetic order. Finally, we discuss extensions of these results to magnetic space groups, connections to Landau theories of collinear order, and the characterization of odd-parity magnets.

</details>


### [146] [Level ordering in the linear $E \otimes e$ dynamic Jahn-Teller model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38190)
*Naoya Iwahara*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文严格证明了线性 E⊗e 动态 Jahn–Teller 模型中，对任意耦合强度和每个能级序数 n，固定 vibronic 角动量 j 的子空间能级随 |j| 增大而升高，因此基态始终是 j=±1/2 的双重态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前人们普遍认为基态是 j=±1/2 双重态，但缺乏对任意耦合强度下能级随 |j| 单调排序的严格证明。

Method: 构造一个使 j 降低 1 的幺正变换，并结合变分原理证明 E_{n,1/2}<E_{n,3/2}<E_{n,5/2}<⋯；同时给出基态能隙的显式下界。

Result: 证明了对于任意耦合强度及任意 n，E_{nj} 随 |j| 严格增加，特别地基态必为 j=±1/2 双重态；并得到 E_{0,j+1}-E_{0j} 的显式下界。

Conclusion: 线性 E⊗e 动态 Jahn–Teller 模型的基态双重态性质被严格确立，且能级按 vibronic 角动量绝对值的排序具有普遍性。

Abstract: The ground level of the linear $E\otimes e$ dynamic Jahn-Teller model has long been believed to be the doublet with vibronic angular momentum $j = \pm\tfrac12$. We prove that, for arbitrary coupling strength, the $n$th level $E_{nj}$ in the subspace of fixed $j$ strictly increases with $|j|$ for every $n$, $E_{n,1/2} < E_{n,3/2} < E_{n,5/2} < \cdots$; in particular, the ground level is always the $j = \pm\tfrac12$ doublet. The proof uses only a unitary transformation that lowers $j$ by one, combined with the variational principle. We also give an explicit lower bound on the gap $E_{0,j+1} - E_{0j}$.

</details>


### [147] [Mixed-State Topological Transition and Emergent Fractionalized Supersymmetry in the Non-Abelian Kitaev Spin Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38741)
*Zhou-Quan Wan,Hong Yao,Shao-Kai Jian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究非阿贝尔 Kitaev 自旋液体在键退相位通道驱动下的混合态拓扑相变，并揭示退相位诱导的任意子凝聚、混合态拓扑序以及界面处涌现的 N=1 超对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开放量子体系中退相干如何改变非阿贝尔自旋液体的拓扑性质，并寻找可由数值和实验观测的混合态拓扑相变及涌现超对称。

Method: 采用矢量化图像，将键退相位映射为两个时间反演的分数量化 Majorana 系统与有能隙 Z2 规范场耦合，从而构造无符号问题的蒙特卡洛模拟；同时分析拓扑/平庸 Kitaev 态时间界面处的 Chern 数失配。

Result: 退相位增强导致层间费米子对凝聚，使 Choi 态中的 Ising ⊠ 反 Ising 序转变为 toric-code 序，并在展开后得到内禀混合态拓扑序。时间界面处 Chern 数失配强制无能隙 Majorana 模；退相位驱动混合态拓扑相变，使该模涌现 N=1 超对称。蒙特卡洛给出 ν=1.27(3) 以及 η_b≈η_f≈0.40，与精确超对称值 5/4 和 2/5 一致。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了退相位驱动的非阿贝尔自旋液体混合态拓扑相变图像，表明分数化超对称伙伴可作为规范不变的 spin string 观测，为未来实验探测混合态拓扑序和涌现超对称提供具体预言。

Abstract: We study a mixed-state topological transition in the non-Abelian Kitaev spin liquid driven by a bond-dephasing channel. In the vectorized picture, bond dephasing maps onto interactions between two time-reversed fractionalized Majorana systems coupled to gapped $\mathbb Z_2$ gauge fields, enabling sign-problem-free Monte Carlo simulations. We show that increasing dephasing condenses an inter-copy fermion pair, converting ${\rm Ising}\boxtimes\overline{\rm{Ising}}$ to toric-code order in the Choi state and yielding intrinsically mixed-state topological order upon unfolding. More interestingly, at a temporal interface between topological and trivial Kitaev states, a Chern-number mismatch enforces a gapless Majorana mode. Dephasing then drives a mixed-state topological transition at which the gapless mode develops emergent $\mathcal N=1$ supersymmetry. Our Monte Carlo simulations yield $\nu=1.27(3)$ and $\eta_b\simeq\eta_f\simeq0.40$, consistent with the exact supersymmetric values $5/4$ and $2/5$. The supersymmetric partners are fractionalized and appear physically as distinct gauge-invariant spin strings, providing concrete observables for future experiments.

</details>


### [148] [Atomic Charge Chirality from Electric Multipoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39150)
*Satoru Hayami,Rikuto Oiwa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出用电多极矩 $Q_{lm}$ 作为局域原子电荷分布手性的判据，并给出 11 个晶手性点群区分对映体所需的最低多极矩内容及轨道基限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要从局域电荷分布的多极矩角度理解晶体手性的微观编码，并明确不同手性点群区分相反手性所需的最小多极结构。

Method: 对 11 个晶手性点群分析最低阶多极矩；对轴群 $C_n$、$D_n$（$n=2,3,4,6$）考察 $m=n$ 的连续阶 $l=n$ 与 $n+1$ 方位各向异性的相对取向；对立方群 $T$、$O$ 分别分析；并在无自旋 $s$-$p$-$d$-$f$ 原子基下检查标量电荷密度的多极阶上限。

Result: $C_n$、$D_n$ 的手性源于两个 $m=n$ 方位各向异性（阶数 $l=n$ 与 $n+1$）的共存，形成随纬度变化的 $n$ 重电荷分布扭转；极分量虽在 $C_n$ 中允许，但不是手性机制所必需。$T$ 与 $O$ 的实现不同，八面体手性最早出现在九阶。无自旋 $s$-$p$-$d$-$f$ 基的标量电荷密度仅含至六阶多极矩，故不能编码 $C_6$、$D_6$ 或 $O$ 的手性。

Conclusion: 该工作将电多极矩内容与原子轨道自由度同晶体手性的微观编码联系起来，给出了基于 $Q_{lm}$ 的手性判据及轨道基表示能力的限制。

Abstract: We establish an electric-multipole ($Q_{lm}$) criterion for chirality of local atomic charge distributions. For the 11 crystallographic chiral point groups, we identify representative lowest-rank multipole contents required to distinguish the opposite handednesses. For the axial groups $C_n$ and $D_n$ with $n=2,3,4,6$, chirality arises from the relative orientation of two azimuthal anisotropies with $m=n$, belonging to consecutive ranks $l=n$ and $n+1$. Their coexistence produces a latitude-dependent twist of the $n$-fold charge distribution. The polar component, although allowed in $C_n$, is distinct from this chiral mechanism and is not required for handedness. The cubic groups $T$ and $O$ exhibit distinct realizations, with octahedral chirality first appearing at rank nine. We further show that, within a spinless $s$-$p$-$d$-$f$ atomic basis, the scalar charge density contains multipoles only up to rank six and therefore cannot encode the handedness of $C_6$, $D_6$, or $O$. These results connect electric-multipole content and atomic orbital degrees of freedom to the microscopic encoding of crystallographic handedness.

</details>


### [149] [A Zoo of Fermionic Relative R\'enyi Negativities in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39349)
*Linjing Jiang,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一类相对 Rényi 负度作为混合态纠缠见证，并通过费米子部分时间反演与尺寸超算符的联系建立路径积分表示，从而在 SYK 模型及其推广中利用大 N 展开研究强关联费米子系统中的混合态纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联费米子系统的混合态纠缠表征困难：对数负度计算代价高且精确对角化仅限小系统，而传统 Rényi 负度代理（twisted/untwisted）对可分离态也可能为正，因此需要可靠且可计算的纠缠诊断量。

Method: 引入相对 Rényi 负度族作为纠缠见证；将费米子部分时间反演与尺寸超算符关联，得到这些 Rényi 负度的路径积分表示，并在原始 SYK 模型和 Maldacena-Qi 耦合 SYK 模型的热态中通过大 N 展开进行评估。

Result: 发现一类特殊的相对 Rényi 负度可作为对数负度的合理代理，并揭示简单观测量无法探测的一阶纠缠转变；这些负度还可作为自由能，提示存在一类新的转变。

Conclusion: 相对 Rényi 负度是研究强关联费米子系统中混合态纠缠的有效探针，并可能定义由纠缠负度作为自由能刻画的新转变类型。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is a fundamental resource that gives rise to correlations with no classical counterpart in strongly correlated fermionic systems. However, its characterization in mixed quantum states remains challenging. Established entanglement diagnostics, such as logarithmic negativity, are computationally demanding in interacting systems, and their evaluation by exact diagonalization is restricted to small system sizes. Meanwhile, conventional R\'enyi proxies, including twisted and untwisted R\'enyi negativities, can be positive even for separable states. In this work, we introduce a family of \textit{relative R\'enyi negativities} that serve as entanglement witnesses. By relating fermionic partial time reversal to the size superoperator, we establish a path-integral representation for these R\'enyi negativities, enabling their evaluation in thermal states of the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model and its generalizations via a large-$N$ expansion. Applying this method, we investigate entanglement in both the original SYK model and the Maldacena-Qi coupled SYK model. We identify a special class of relative R\'enyi negativities which serve as plausible proxies for logarithmic negativity and uncover first-order entanglement transitions that are invisible to simple observables. These results highlight the utility of relative R\'enyi negativities as probes of mixed-state entanglement in strongly correlated fermionic systems and suggest the existence of a new class of transitions where these negativities serve as free energies.

</details>


### [150] [High-Field Heat Capacity of Atacamite Cu$_2$Cl(OH)$_3$ for $\mathbf{H} \parallel b$ axis: From Antiferromagnetic Order to Correlated Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39669)
*L. St\"odter,A. Demuer,T. Kotte,A. U. B. Wolter,B. B\"uchner,K. C. Rule,S. S\"ullow*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文测量了阻挫锯齿链化合物atacamite在H∥b方向高达35 T的比热，观察到从反铁磁长程有序到场诱导自旋液体样态的转变，并发现>32 T时出现自旋能隙激活特征，支持锯齿链解耦为基底和顶端自旋子系统的部分极化图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究atacamite在强磁场H∥b下的磁相变与高场基态，检验此前基于H∥c提出的高场态机制：锯齿链解耦成两个自旋子系统并形成M~Msat/2的部分极化态。

Method: 对Cu2Cl(OH)3 atacamite在H∥b、最高35 T磁场下进行比热测量，分析转变异常随磁场的变化，并用高场低温比热的指数行为提取自旋能隙。

Result: 随磁场增加，反铁磁长程有序对应的尖锐转变异常向低温移动并最终消失，进入无转变异常的自旋液体样态；在>32 T的最高场区出现宽异常，且随磁场升高向高温移动。高场区低温比热可由指数行为很好描述，表明存在热激活跨越自旋能隙。

Conclusion: H∥b的高场行为与H∥c的结果一致，支持atacamite高场态中锯齿链解耦为基底和顶端自旋子系统、形成M~Msat/2部分极化态的场景。

Abstract: We present the heat capacity of the frustrated sawtooth-chain compound atacamite Cu$_2$Cl(OH)$_3$ in high magnetic fields up to 35 T for $\mathbf{H} \parallel b$ axis. We clearly observe the change from antiferromagnetic long-range order, indicated by a sharp transition anomaly shifted to lower temperatures when increasing field, to a field-induced spin-liquid-like state with the absence of any transition anomaly. Here, in highest magnetic fields above 32 T a broad anomaly is present which is shifted to higher temperatures as the field is increased. The low-temperature heat capacity in the high-field region can be described well by an exponential behavior indicating a thermal activation over a spin gap. The findings are consistent with our scenario for the high-field state in atacamite for $\mathbf{H} \parallel c$ axis, where we proposed the sawtooth chains to decouple into two spin subsystems, basal and apical spin sites, leading to a partially polarized state with $M \sim M_\mathrm{sat}/2$.

</details>


### [151] [Phonon Fluctuations Enhanced Superconductivity in a Three-Band Cuprate Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39710)
*Jiarui Liu,Yao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在铜氧化物三带模型中，B1g氧屈曲声子对d波超导的作用取决于其处于绝热还是反绝热区：绝热声子抑制配对，反绝热声子增强配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物高温超导的微观起源仍受交织序影响而不清楚；实验和理论提示声子尤其是B1g氧屈曲模可能起关键作用，但其微观机制尚待阐明。

Method: 采用三带铜氧化物模型研究B1g声子的微观影响，比较给定温度下绝热与反绝热声子对d波超导配对、准粒子相干性及配对顶点的作用，并分析电子-空穴不对称性。

Result: 热激发的绝热B1g声子通过损失准粒子相干性抑制d波配对；反绝热B1g声子同时增强相干性和吸引性d波配对顶点，在多项诊断中增强超导。该增强还因掺杂载流子轨道特征不同而表现出显著电子-空穴不对称性。

Conclusion: 结果确立了B1g声子可与电子关联协同、显著增强铜氧化物d波超导配对的一种微观机制。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in cuprates remains an outstanding puzzle due to the interplay of intertwined orders. Experimental observations and theoretical studies have suggested crucial roles of phonons, with the $B_{1g}$ oxygen buckling mode emerging as a promising candidate. Here, we investigate the microscopic influence of this phonon in a three-band cuprate model. We find that the $B_{1g}$ phonon plays opposite roles in superconductivity depending on whether it lies in the adiabatic or antiadiabatic regime at a given temperature. Thermally populated adiabatic phonons suppress $d$-wave pairing through the loss of quasiparticle coherence, whereas antiadiabatic phonons simultaneously enhance coherence and the attractive $d$-wave pairing vertex, strengthening superconductivity across multiple diagnostics. This enhancement also exhibits pronounced electron-hole asymmetry arising from the different orbital characters of doped carriers. Our results therefore establish a microscopic mechanism through which $B_{1g}$ phonons can cooperate with electronic correlations to substantially enhance $d$-wave superconducting pairing in cuprates.

</details>


### [152] [Monte Carlo simulation of mixed spin-orbit ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39943)
*Yu. D. Panov,V. A. Ulitko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用准经典Monte Carlo热浴算法研究磁场对具有最大纠缠的Kugel-Khomskii模型热力学性质的影响，发现磁场会诱导非零磁化并伴随轨道序出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解磁场如何影响最大纠缠Kugel-Khomskii模型的热力学行为，特别是自旋与轨道自由度之间的关联及有序化机制。

Method: 采用准经典Monte Carlo方法并结合热浴算法，计算单格点关联函数随温度的变化，分析磁场下磁化、轨道序和自旋-轨道关联的行为。

Result: 单格点关联函数的温度依赖表明，施加磁场导致非零磁化，并在布里渊区中与存在非零自旋-轨道关联函数的同一波矢处同时出现轨道序。

Conclusion: 磁场可驱动最大纠缠Kugel-Khomskii模型中磁化与轨道序的同步出现，且轨道序与自旋-轨道关联在动量空间同一位置相联系，揭示了自旋-轨道耦合在磁场诱导有序中的关键作用。

Abstract: The effect of a magnetic field on the thermodynamic properties of the Kugel-Khomskii model with maximal entanglement using the quasi-classical Monte Carlo method with the heat bath algorithm. The temperature dependencies of single-site correlators show that the application of a magnetic field results in non-zero magnetization and the simultaneous appearance of an orbital ordering at the same point in the Brillouin zone where a non-zero spin-orbit correlator exists.

</details>


### [153] [Electronic liquid crystals and superconductivity from doping a moir\'e Mott insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39978)
*Yichi Zhang,Yiyu Xia,Zhongdong Han,Jekwan Lee,Brandon Bauer,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Wenjin Zhao,Kin Fai Mak,Jie Shan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在掺杂莫尔莫特绝缘体——扭转双层WSe2中，作者发现条纹相并观察到条纹相到超导体的量子相变，揭示条纹序与超导序的紧密交织关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子液晶态（条纹相、向列相）预期出现在掺杂莫特绝缘体中，并可能与高温超导竞争或交织；莫尔半导体提供了可调控平台来研究条纹相与超导的关系。

Method: 对扭转双层WSe2进行电输运测量，探测从高温各向同性相到低温各向异性相的热力学相变及费米面重构，并分析条纹—超导体转变附近的附加相变与标度行为。

Result: 发现掺杂莫尔莫特绝缘体中条纹相的普遍出现；输运显示低温各向异性条纹相伴随费米面重构；在条纹—超导转变附近观察到金属条纹到钉扎绝缘条纹的额外热力学相变；该转变表现自相似性。

Conclusion: 结果表明莫尔材料中条纹序与超导序密切交织，为研究莫尔体系中的交织序和条纹—超导量子相变提供了新路径。

Abstract: Electronic liquid crystals, such as stripe phases and nematics, are self-organized states of matter with spontaneously broken rotational symmetry. They are expected to emerge in a doped Mott insulator as a compromise between short- and long-range Coulomb interactions, and are believed to be intimately connected to the emergence of high temperature superconductivity either as a competing order or an intertwined order. Moir\'e semiconductors provide a controllable platform to explore the relationship between stripe phases and superconductivity near a Mott insulator. Here we report the generic appearance of stripe phases in a doped moir\'e Mott insulator and the observation of a stripe to-superconductor quantum phase transition in twisted bilayer WSe2. The stripe phases were revealed by electrical transport studies as a thermodynamic phase transition from a high temperature isotropic phase to a low-temperature anisotropic phase accompanied by a Fermi surface reconstruction. Near the stripe-to-superconductor transition, we also observed an additional thermodynamic phase transition from metallic stripes to pinned insulating stripes. The stripe-to-superconductor transition further exhibits an intriguing self-similarity that highlights the intimate relationship between the two orders. Our results pave the path for studying intertwined orders in moir\'e materials.

</details>


### [154] [Adiabatic Variational Principle: Born-Oppenheimer Theory for Emergent Electronic-Order Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40117)
*Yang Yang,Ali Rayat,Ho Jang,Yunhao Fan,Sankha Subhra Bakshi,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出绝热变分原理（AVP），从相互作用多体哈密顿量直接导出序参量动力学，为相场式模拟涌现电子序的非平衡动力学提供不依赖求解器的微观基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微观多体方法能较准确描述平衡量子态，但涌现序动力学的大规模模拟通常依赖唯象方程和预设自由能泛函。作者希望显式建立传统相场描述中隐含的绝热闭合，并从微观哈密顿量推导集体序参量演化。

Method: AVP假设微观弛豫快于序参量演化，使多体态随集体构型绝热跟随，类比Born-Oppenheimer分离。每个瞬时序参量构型定义受约束的准稳态多体态，约束的拉格朗日乘子给出驱动集体演化的热力学力；再用非线性Mori-Zwanzig投影理论联系可逆动力学与集体变量微观代数，并将耗散和涨落归因于被消除的自由度。

Result: 应用于电荷密度波和自旋密度波动力学时，可恢复Allen-Cahn畴生长，解析演化中的畴网络及其微观电子结构，并得到巡游磁性的广义Landau-Lifshitz动力学。

Conclusion: AVP为涌现电子序非平衡动力学的相场类模拟提供了独立于求解器的微观基础，可把微观多体计算与介观序参量动力学一致地连接起来。

Abstract: Recent advances in microscopic many-body methods have enabled increasingly accurate descriptions of equilibrium quantum states, yet large-scale simulations of emergent-order dynamics typically rely on phenomenological equations with prescribed free-energy functionals. We introduce the Adiabatic Variational Principle (AVP), a framework for deriving order-parameter dynamics directly from an interacting many-body Hamiltonian. When microscopic relaxation is fast compared with order-parameter evolution, the many-body state follows the collective configuration adiabatically, in analogy with the Born--Oppenheimer separation of electronic and nuclear motion. This assumption makes explicit the adiabatic closure implicit in conventional phase-field descriptions. Each instantaneous order-parameter configuration then defines a constrained quasi-stationary many-body state, and the Lagrange multipliers enforcing these constraints directly provide the thermodynamic forces driving the collective evolution. Nonlinear Mori--Zwanzig projection theory further connects the reversible dynamics to the microscopic algebra of the collective variables and relates dissipation and fluctuations to the eliminated degrees of freedom. Applications to charge- and spin-density-wave dynamics recover Allen--Cahn domain growth, resolve evolving domain networks together with their microscopic electronic structure, and yield generalized Landau--Lifshitz dynamics for itinerant magnetism. The AVP thus provides a solver-independent microscopic foundation for phase-field-like simulations of the nonequilibrium dynamics of emergent electronic order.

</details>


### [155] [Cage-related crystal-field effects in the paramagnetic range of PrB$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40176)
*Mehdi Amara,Christine Opagiste,Natalya Yu. Shitsevalova*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过比热、热膨胀和磁性测量研究PrB6在50 K以下顺磁区，发现其晶体场基态存在动力学劈裂迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清PrB6中由非弹性中子散射识别的三度简并晶体场基态与磁熵、磁化率行为之间的矛盾，并探讨与CeB6类似的硼笼内晶体场动力学劈裂及离心Jahn-Teller效应。

Method: 采用比热、热膨胀和磁性测量；利用两次连续磁转变的潜热及改进的晶格贡献确定磁熵随温度变化，并分析低温顺磁磁化率。

Result: 在首个一级磁有序温度T_N=6.7 K以上，每个Pr3+的磁熵接近双重态而非三度简并晶体场基态，需超过30 K才达到三重态熵；低温顺磁磁化率类似非磁性基态；20 K以下出现大热膨胀异常。

Conclusion: 结果表明PrB6中晶体场基态在硼笼内发生动力学劈裂，行为类似CeB6，并与预期的离心Jahn-Teller效应一致。

Abstract: New insights into the paramagnetic range of PrB$_6$ below 50 K are revealed through specific-heat, thermal-expansion and magnetic measurements. The latent heats of the two successive magnetic transitions, together with an improved description of the lattice contribution, enable a detailed determination of the thermal variation of the magnetic entropy. Immediately above the first-order magnetic ordering temperature ($T_N = 6.7$ K), the magnetic entropy per Pr$^{3+}$ is close to that of a doublet, instead of the triplet crystal-field ground state identified by inelastic neutron scattering. Temperatures in excess of 30 K are required to reach the entropy of the threefold-degenerate ground state. This is reminiscent of the CeB$_6$ case and, similarly, strongly suggests a dynamical splitting of the crystal-field ground state within the boron cage. Further evidence of this splitting is provided by the analysis of the low-temperature paramagnetic susceptibility, which resembles that of a non-magnetic ground state. In addition, a large thermal-expansion anomaly is observed below 20 K, consistent with the expected centrifugal Jahn-Teller effect.

</details>


### [156] [Quasi-Phases of Quantum Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40526)
*Nick G. Jones,Paul Fendley,Nathanan Tantivasadakarn,Ruben Verresen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文提出“准相”（quasi-phase）概念，用以刻画连续相变线终止点附近、在整体相图中相连但无法通过临界理论小扰动可及的能隙路径相互连接的能隙区域，从而赋予终止线两侧区域以可操作的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续相变线可在量子多体相图中出现并终止，但终止点两侧的能隙区域是否可像水与蒸汽那样被有意义地视为不同相仍不清楚；任何尖锐刻画都需超越通常物相定义。

Method: 引入准相：临界点附近的能隙区域在更广相图中相连，但不能由临界理论的小扰动经能隙路径连接。以一维自旋链中中心荷 c=1 的连续终止线为例，构造并由多临界母理论描述，包括 c=3/2 与 c=9/5 的 orbifold 理论，对应“商对称性保护”和“表示对称性保护”拓扑现象；并通过弱化 Lieb-Schultz-Mattis 反常给出构造性原理。

Result: 准相可导致同一物相不同区域间的界面模式；其界面模式可携带更高维线性表示，而不同于一维 SPT 相由投影表示类标记的常规情形。描述完整临界线与连接两区域的能隙路径需要更大的多临界母理论。使不同准相可区分会使一个在紫外尺度非必要的临界点在红外尺度成为必要，该条件比仅存在余维一临界线更严格。

Conclusion: 准相揭示了常规物相分类之外更丰富的结构：终止连续临界线两侧的能隙区域可在同一物相内却具有可区分的物理后果，并对临界点必要性与对称性保护拓扑现象给出新的构造性理解。

Abstract: Within quantum many-body phase diagrams, continuous transition lines can appear and then terminate. However, it has remained unclear whether the neighboring regimes can be meaningfully described as distinct phases, akin to water and vapour, since any sharp characterization would have to go beyond the usual notion of phases of matter. We answer this in the affirmative by introducing quasi-phases: gapped regimes near a critical point that are connected in the broader phase diagram, but not by any gapped path accessible from small perturbations of the critical theory. The physical consequences include interface modes between regimes in the same phase of matter. More striking still, describing both the extended critical line and the gapped path connecting the regimes requires a bigger multicritical parent theory. In spin-chain examples with continuous terminating lines with central charge $c=1$, we find multicritical $c=3/2$ and $c=9/5$ orbifolds describing so-called 'quotient symmetry protected' and 'representation symmetry protected' topological phenomena. While one-dimensional symmetry-protected topological phases are labeled by projective representation classes, these quasi-phases can have interface modes carrying higher-dimensional linear representations. We turn this into a constructive principle through a weakening of the Lieb-Schultz-Mattis anomaly. Supporting distinct quasi-phases makes an unnecessary critical point infrared-necessary, even if it is unnecessary at the ultraviolet scale. This condition is more restrictive than simply having a codimension-one critical line, revealing richer structure within phases of matter than their conventional classification suggests.

</details>


### [157] [Algebraic theory of abelian fractons and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40596)
*Wilbur Shirley,Evan Wickenden,Bowen Yang,Agn\`es Beaudry,Michael Hermele*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出用编织融合复形（BFC）及其等价类编织融合理论（BFT）统一描述阿贝尔能隙分形子序和拓扑相中的融合、迁移性与统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将阿贝尔任意子的代数描述推广到具有受限迁移性和空间扩展激发的系统，以刻画平移不变玻色系统中点状与扩展激发的普适代数结构。

Method: 以编织融合复形为基本对象：链复形编码融合与迁移性，编织链映射编码统计；其等价类定义BFT。框架覆盖任意平移不变Pauli码及二维阿贝尔任意子理论（含手征理论），并引入互统计的融合模与统计对偶映射，将远程探测解释为互统计在分形子序中的推广。

Result: 给出非退化BFT可由能隙相实现且为相不变量的猜想证据；导出点状融合与迁移性约束，例如X-cube和各向异性模型的Pauli X或Z扇区不能单独出现在非退化BFT中；证明在唯一无穷空间基态假设下，激发-探测原理等价于非退化性。

Conclusion: BFC/BFT为分形子序提供统一代数框架，互统计与激发-探测原理给出非退化性的不变量刻画和可检验约束。

Abstract: We develop an algebraic theory of fusion, mobility, and statistics in abelian gapped fracton orders and topological phases. The framework generalizes the algebraic description of abelian anyons to systems with restricted-mobility and spatially extended excitations. We consider translation-invariant bosonic systems whose pointlike and extended excitations have finite fusion order. The basic objects are braided fusion complexes (BFCs): chain complexes encoding fusion and mobility, equipped with a braiding chain map encoding statistics. Their equivalence classes define braided fusion theories (BFTs). Our framework encompasses arbitrary translation-invariant Pauli codes, as well as all abelian anyon theories in two spatial dimensions, including chiral theories. We give evidence for the conjecture that every nondegenerate BFT can be realized as the excitation theory of a gapped phase and that the BFT describing such a phase is a phase invariant. We derive constraints on pointlike fusion and mobility, showing, for example, that neither the Pauli X nor Z sectors of the X-cube and anisotropic models can occur alone in a nondegenerate BFT. For nondegenerate theories with only pointlike excitations, we extract a simpler theory of mutual statistics, consisting of a fusion module and a map to its statistical dual. This theory of mutual statistics is an invariant of the BFT and, at odd fusion order, gives an equivalent description of the full braided fusion theory. We interpret remote detection as the generalization of mutual statistics to fracton orders. In the BFC setting, we give a precise formulation of the excitation-detector principle: every nontrivial excitation can be detected by an operator supported at spatial infinity, and every nontrivial detector detects some excitation. Assuming a unique infinite-space ground state, we prove that this principle is equivalent to nondegeneracy.

</details>

### [158] [High-Performance Nuclear Demagnetization Refrigerator Reaching 50 $\mu$K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38622)
*Zhengchao Liu,Junyang Song,Bingbing Tong,Hiroki Ikegami*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 报道一套基于标准湿式稀释制冷机与中型铜核绝热去磁级的核去磁系统，可高效进入深亚毫开尔文温区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子技术与量子材料进展使约0.1 mK及更低的深亚毫开尔文温区在凝聚态物理中具有新机会，需要实用且高效的制冷平台。

Method: 采用标准湿式稀释制冷机配合中型铜核绝热去磁级设计核去磁系统，并用Pt NMR温度计测温及数值分析评估铜核自旋系统与热漏影响。

Result: Pt NMR测温达到50 μK，制冷机可维持100 μK超过10天；即使仅预冷12小时，也能进入深亚毫开尔文温区并维持100 μK超过3天。数值分析表明铜核自旋系统冷却至约18 μK，而温度计处的局部热漏将其测温限制在约50 μK。

Conclusion: 该系统为深亚毫开尔文温区实验提供了实用、高效且可长时间维持的平台，适合广泛的样品测量。

Abstract: Research in the deep sub-mK regime around 0.1 mK may open new opportunities in condensed matter physics in light of recent rapid progress in quantum technologies and quantum materials. Here, we report the design and performance of a nuclear demagnetization system that provides efficient access to the deep sub-mK regime for a wide range of experiments, using a standard wet dilution refrigerator with a medium-sized copper nuclear stage. We show that the temperature measured by a Pt NMR thermometer reaches 50 $\mu$K and that the refrigerator can maintain 100 $\mu$K for more than ten days. Remarkably, even with only 12 hours of precooling, the refrigerator cools into the deep sub-mK regime and can maintain 100 $\mu$K for more than three days, providing sufficient time for sample measurements. Numerical analyses indicate that the copper nuclear-spin system is cooled to about 18 $\mu$K, whereas a local heat leak to the thermometer limits the thermometer temperature to about 50 $\mu$K. These features establish the system as a practical platform for efficient experiments in the deep sub-mK regime.

</details>


### [159] [Quantum Hamiltonian time crystals from covariant quantum Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39886)
*Antti J. Niemi*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文提出经典哈密顿时间晶体的量子对应物：在固定密度矩阵谱与电荷期望下最小化能量，并用对称协变量子Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert方程将其选为周期吸引子，最后在受Cu3分子磁体启发的 chiral spin trimer 中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典哈密顿时间晶体是受约束的能量极小值，沿对称轨道以由Lagrange乘子设定的频率运动；作者希望构造其量子版本，并给出可由动力学自然选择的实现机制。

Method: 在固定谱和电荷期望的密度矩阵集合上最小化能量；引入对称协变的量子Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert方程，使这些约束能量极小值成为周期吸引子，同时保持谱不变并动态确定Lagrange乘子；用受Cu3分子磁体启发的chiral spin trimer进行模型验证。

Result: 量子Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert动力学可选择周期吸引子并动态固定乘子；在手性自旋三聚体中，于制备的磁化强度下实现了该周期运动，且不需要Zeeman项。

Conclusion: 工作建立了经典哈密顿时间晶体的量子对应框架，并展示了无需外场即可在分子磁体相关自旋模型中实现的时间晶体式周期动力学。

Abstract: Classical Hamiltonian time crystals are constrained energy minima that move along symmetry orbits at frequencies set by Lagrange multipliers. We construct quantum counterparts by minimizing the energy over density matrices with fixed spectrum and charge expectations. A symmetry-covariant quantum Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert equation selects them as periodic attractors, preserving the spectrum and fixing the multipliers dynamically. A chiral spin trimer motivated by Cu$_3$ molecular magnets realizes this motion at a prepared magnetization, without a Zeeman term.

</details>


### [160] [Quantum Metric Scaling and Its Exceptions in Classical Flat Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40066)
*Kin Hung Fung,K. T. Law,C. T. Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文在一维局域共振质量-弹簧经典平带中检验量子度规长度作为局域化长度的标度关系，并揭示其失效情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度规长度被提议为一维平带中的局域化长度尺度，但其适用边界和例外尚需在经典平带体系中检验。

Method: 构建由局域共振质量-弹簧系统形成的经典平带，比较局域化长度与量子度规长度，并引入选择性质量无序。

Result: 局域化长度在平带附近遵循量子度规长度，但在精确平带频率处偏离该比例关系；选择性质量无序可在不改变干净体系度规的情况下，在平带频率附近产生发散局域化长度或任意短的尾巴。

Conclusion: 量子度规并不能单独决定所有局域化尺度，无序可以选择超出量子度规预测的局域化长度。

Abstract: The quantum metric length has been proposed as the localization length scale in one-dimensional flat bands. Here, we study this quantum metric scaling and the exceptions to it in a classical flat band formed by a locally resonant mass--spring system. We find that the localization length follows the quantum metric length near the flat band but departs from this proportionality at the exact flat-band frequency. Selective mass disorder also produces divergent localization lengths or arbitrarily short tails near the flat-band frequency without changing the clean metric. These exceptions show that disorder can select localization scales beyond those predicted by the quantum metric alone.

</details>
